from django.contrib import admin

from .models import AuditLog, Department, FacultyProfile, PositionTitle, UserProfile


@admin.register(Department)
class DepartmentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("id", "name")
    search_fields = ("name",)
    ordering = ("name",)


@admin.register(PositionTitle)
class PositionTitleAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("id", "title")
    search_fields = ("title",)
    ordering = ("title",)


@admin.register(FacultyProfile)
class FacultyProfileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "employee_id",
        "last_name",
        "first_name",
        "department_ref",
        "position_ref",
        "can_handle_classes",
        "is_active",
    )
    list_filter = ("is_active", "can_handle_classes", "department_ref", "position_ref")
    search_fields = ("employee_id", "first_name", "last_name", "email", "contact_number")
    list_select_related = ("department_ref", "position_ref")

import secrets
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F
from django.utils import timezone
from common.fields import SafeJsonField
from common.phone import normalize_ph_phone

SUFFIX_CHOICES = [
    ("", "None"),
    ("Jr.", "Jr."),
    ("Sr.", "Sr."),
    ("II", "II"),
    ("III", "III"),
    ("IV", "IV"),
]

USER_ROLE_CHOICES = [
    ("ADMIN", "System Administrator"),
    ("PRINCIPAL", "School Principal"),
    ("DEPT_HEAD", "Department Head"),
    ("FACULTY", "Classroom Faculty / Adviser"),
    ("PARENT", "Parent / Guardian"),
]


class Department(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "departments"
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.name


class PositionTitle(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "position_titles"
        ordering = ["title"]

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.title


class FacultyProfile(models.Model):
    """
    3NF Faculty entity:
    - Name, email, and active login state live in auth.User (via UserProfile) when linked,
      with standalone first_name/last_name fallback for directory-only faculty records.
    - Department and Position reference normalized lookup tables (Department, PositionTitle).
    """

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    employee_id = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, db_index=True)
    first_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    middle_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    last_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    suffix = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=SUFFIX_CHOICES, blank=True, default="")

    department_ref = models.ForeignKey(
        Department,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="faculty_members",
    )
    position_ref = models.ForeignKey(
        PositionTitle,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="faculty_members",
    )

    contact_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    email = models.EmailField(max_length=150, blank=True, default="")
    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to="faculty/", null=True, blank=True)
    photo_updated_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    can_handle_classes = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_column="can_teach", db_index=True)
    qr_token = models.CharField(max_length=128, unique=True, null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)

    bound_device_id = models.CharField(max_length=128, blank=True, null=True, unique=True)
    device_model = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, null=True)
    device_bound_at = models.DateTimeField(blank=True, null=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="created_faculty_profiles",
    )
    updated_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="updated_faculty_profiles",
    )

    class Meta:
        db_table = "faculty_profiles"
        ordering = ["last_name", "first_name"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["is_active", "last_name", "first_name"], name="idx_fac_active_name"),
        ]

    @property
    def department(self) -> str:
        return self.department_ref.name if self.department_ref_id and self.department_ref else ""

    @department.setter
    def department(self, value: str) -> None:
        clean = str(value or "").strip()[:100]
        if clean:
            dept_obj, _ = Department.objects.get_or_create(name=clean)
            self.department_ref = dept_obj
        else:
            self.department_ref = None

    @property
    def position(self) -> str:
        return self.position_ref.title if self.position_ref_id and self.position_ref else ""

    @position.setter
    def position(self, value: str) -> None:
        clean = str(value or "").strip()[:100]
        if clean:
            pos_obj, _ = PositionTitle.objects.get_or_create(title=clean)
            self.position_ref = pos_obj
        else:
            self.position_ref = None

    @property
    def full_name(self) -> str:
        mid = f" {self.middle_name[:1]}." if self.middle_name else ""
        suf = f" {self.suffix}" if self.suffix else ""
        return f"{self.first_name}{mid} {self.last_name}{suf}".strip()

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.contact_number:
            self.contact_number = normalize_ph_phone(self.contact_number)
        if not self.qr_token:
            self.qr_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.full_name} ({self.employee_id})"


class UserProfile(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    user = models.OneToOneField(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="profile"
    )
    faculty = models.OneToOneField(
        FacultyProfile,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="account",
    )
    role = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=USER_ROLE_CHOICES, default="FACULTY", db_index=True)
    phone_number = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, default="", db_index=True)
    avatar = models.ImageField(upload_to="avatars/", null=True, blank=True)
    push_token = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)
    is_profile_completed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    failed_attempts = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    locked_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "user_profiles"

    @property
    def handles_classes(self) -> bool:
        if self.role == "PARENT":
            return False
        if self.faculty_id and self.faculty:
            return bool(self.faculty.can_handle_classes)
        return True

    def is_locked(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.locked_until and timezone.now() < self.locked_until)

    def record_failed_attempt(self) -> None:
        now = timezone.now()
        if self.locked_until and now >= self.locked_until:
            UserProfile.objects.filter(pk=self.pk).update(failed_attempts=1, locked_until=None)
            self.failed_attempts = 1
            self.locked_until = None
            return
        UserProfile.objects.filter(pk=self.pk).update(failed_attempts=F("failed_attempts") + 1)
        self.refresh_from_db(fields=["failed_attempts"])
        if self.failed_attempts >= 5:
            self.locked_until = now + timezone.timedelta(minutes=15)
            self.save(update_fields=["locked_until"])

    def reset_failed_attempts(self) -> None:
        if self.failed_attempts > 0 or self.locked_until is not None:
            UserProfile.objects.filter(pk=self.pk).update(failed_attempts=0, locked_until=None)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.phone_number:
            self.phone_number = normalize_ph_phone(self.phone_number)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    table_name = models.CharField(max_length=64, db_index=True)
    record_id = models.BigIntegerField()
    action = models.CharField(max_length=10, db_index=True)
    old_data = SafeJsonField(null=True, blank=True)
    new_data = SafeJsonField(null=True, blank=True)
    changed_fields = SafeJsonField(null=True, blank=True)
    performed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="audit_actions",
    )
    client_ip = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)
    user_agent = models.TextField(blank=True, default="")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "audit_logs"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db.models import Q

from .models import FacultyProfile, UserProfile

User = get_user_model()


def resolve_faculty_for_user(user) -> FacultyProfile | None:
    """Resolves the active FacultyProfile linked to a signed-in user in at most 1 SQL query."""
    if not user or not getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False):
        return None

    cached_id = getattr(user, "_resolved_faculty_id", None)
    if cached_id is not None:
        if cached_id == 0:
            return None
        found = (
            FacultyProfile.objects.select_related("account", "department_ref", "position_ref")
            .filter(id=cached_id, is_active=True)
            .first()
        )
        if found:
            return found

    profile = (
        UserProfile.objects.select_related(
            "faculty",
            "faculty__department_ref",
            "faculty__position_ref",
        )
        .filter(user=user)
        .first()
    )

    # Parents must never resolve to a FacultyProfile
    if profile and profile.role == "PARENT":
        setattr(user, "_resolved_faculty_id", 0)
        return None

    if profile and profile.faculty_id and profile.faculty and profile.faculty.is_active:
        setattr(user, "_resolved_faculty_id", profile.faculty.id)
        return profile.faculty

    by_account = (
        FacultyProfile.objects.select_related("account", "department_ref", "position_ref")
        .filter(account__user=user, is_active=True)
        .first()
    )
    if by_account:
        setattr(user, "_resolved_faculty_id", by_account.id)
        return by_account

    clean_email = str(getattr(user, "email", "") or "").strip()
    clean_username = str(getattr(user, "username", "") or "").strip()
    lookup = Q(employee_id__iexact=clean_username)
    if clean_email:
        lookup = lookup | Q(email__iexact=clean_email)

    matched = (
        FacultyProfile.objects.select_related("account", "department_ref", "position_ref")
        .filter(lookup, is_active=True)
        .first()
    )
    setattr(user, "_resolved_faculty_id", matched.id if matched else 0)
    return matched


def get_faculty_section_ids(faculty: FacultyProfile | None) -> set[int]:
    """Returns all active Section IDs advised or scheduled under a faculty member."""
    if not faculty:
        return set()

    from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod, Section

    advised_ids = set(
        Section.objects.filter(adviser_id=faculty.id, is_active=True).values_list("id", flat=True)
    )
    scheduled_ids = set(
        ClassPeriod.objects.filter(faculty_id=faculty.id, is_active=True).values_list(
            "section_id", flat=True
        )
    )
    return advised_ids | scheduled_ids


def get_faculty_student_ids(faculty: FacultyProfile | None) -> set[int]:
    """Returns all enrolled Student IDs belonging to sections handled by a faculty member."""
    section_ids = get_faculty_section_ids(faculty)
    if not section_ids:
        return set()

    from apps.students.models import Enrollment

    return set(
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id__in=section_ids,
            status="ENROLLED",
            student__is_active=True,
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils.html import strip_tags
from rest_framework import serializers

from common.phone import normalize_ph_phone
from .models import FacultyProfile, UserProfile

User = get_user_model()


def _clean_str(val: object, max_len: int = 150) -> str:
    return strip_tags(str(val or "")).strip()[:max_len]


class FacultyDropdownSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):

class FacultyProfileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    full_name = serializers.CharField(read_only=True)
    department = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    position = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    has_bound_device = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = FacultyProfile
        fields = [
            "id",
            "employee_id",
            "first_name",
            "middle_name",
            "last_name",
            "suffix",
            "full_name",
            "department",
            "position",
            "contact_number",
            "email",
            "photo",
            "photo_url",
            "can_handle_classes",
            "qr_token",
            "is_active",
            "bound_device_id",
            "device_model",
            "device_bound_at",
            "has_bound_device",
            "created_at",
            "updated_at",
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["id", "qr_token", "device_bound_at", "created_at", "updated_at"]

    def get_photo_url(self, obj: FacultyProfile) -> str | None:
        if not obj.photo:
            return None
        request = self.context.get("request")
        url = obj.photo.url
        return request.build_absolute_uri(url) if request else url

    def get_has_bound_device(self, obj: FacultyProfile) -> bool:
        return bool(obj.bound_device_id)

    def validate_employee_id(self, value: str) -> str:
        clean = _clean_str(value, 50)
        if not clean:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Employee ID is required.")
        qs = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(employee_id__iexact=clean)
        if self.instance:
            qs = qs.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)
        if qs.exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("This Employee ID is already assigned.")
        return clean

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data: dict) -> FacultyProfile:
        dept = validated_data.pop("department", "")
        pos = validated_data.pop("position", "")
        faculty = FacultyProfile(**validated_data)
        faculty.department = dept
        faculty.position = pos
        faculty.save()
        return faculty

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance: FacultyProfile, validated_data: dict) -> FacultyProfile:
        if "department" in validated_data:
            instance.department = validated_data.pop("department")
        if "position" in validated_data:
            instance.position = validated_data.pop("position")
        for attr, val in validated_data.items():
            setattr(instance, attr, val)
        instance.save()
        return instance


class UserAccountSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_blank=False)
    role = serializers.CharField(required=False, default="FACULTY")
    phone_number = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    employee_id = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    department = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    position = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    can_handle_classes = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, default=True)
    faculty_id = serializers.IntegerField(source="profile.faculty_id", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            "id",
            "username",
            "password",
            "first_name",
            "last_name",
            "email",
            "is_active",
            "role",
            "phone_number",
            "employee_id",
            "department",
            "position",
            "can_handle_classes",
            "faculty_id",
        ]

    def validate_password(self, value: str) -> str:
        validate_password(value)
        return value

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        data = super().to_representation(instance)
        prof = getattr(instance, "profile", None)
        fac = getattr(prof, "faculty", None) if prof else None
        data["role"] = prof.role if prof else ("ADMIN" if instance.is_superuser else "FACULTY")
        data["phone_number"] = (
            prof.phone_number if (prof and prof.phone_number) else (fac.contact_number if fac else "")
        )
        data["employee_id"] = fac.employee_id if fac else ""
        data["department"] = fac.department if fac else ""
        data["position"] = fac.position if fac else ""
        data["can_handle_classes"] = bool(prof.handles_classes) if prof else True
        data["faculty_id"] = fac.id if fac else None
        data["is_profile_completed"] = bool(prof and prof.is_profile_completed)
        return data

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data: dict):
        raw_role = str(validated_data.pop("role", "FACULTY") or "FACULTY").upper()
        clean_role = "ADMIN" if raw_role in ("SUPERADMIN", "FACULTY_ADMIN") else raw_role
        phone = normalize_ph_phone(validated_data.pop("phone_number", ""))
        employee_id = _clean_str(validated_data.pop("employee_id", ""), 50)
        department = _clean_str(validated_data.pop("department", ""), 100)
        position = _clean_str(validated_data.pop("position", ""), 100)
        can_handle = bool(validated_data.pop("can_handle_classes", True))
        password = validated_data.pop("password", None)

        user = User(**validated_data)
        user.is_staff = clean_role == "ADMIN"
        user.is_superuser = clean_role == "ADMIN"
        if password:
            user.set_password(password)
        else:
            user.set_unusable_password()
        user.save()

        faculty_obj = None
        if clean_role != "PARENT":
            emp_code = employee_id or f"EMP-{user.id:04d}"
            faculty_obj, _ = FacultyProfile.objects.update_or_create(
                employee_id=emp_code,
                defaults={
                    "first_name": user.first_name or user.username,
                    "last_name": user.last_name or "Staff",
                    "email": user.email or "",
                    "contact_number": phone,
                    "can_handle_classes": can_handle,
                    "is_active": user.is_active,
                },
            )
            if department:
                faculty_obj.department = department
            if position:
                faculty_obj.position = position
            faculty_obj.save()

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=user,
            defaults={
                "role": clean_role,
                "faculty": faculty_obj,
                "phone_number": phone,
                "is_profile_completed": bool(user.first_name and user.last_name),
            },
        )
        user.profile = profile
        return user

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data: dict):
        raw_role = validated_data.pop("role", None)
        phone = validated_data.pop("phone_number", None)
        employee_id = validated_data.pop("employee_id", None)
        department = validated_data.pop("department", None)
        position = validated_data.pop("position", None)
        can_handle = validated_data.pop("can_handle_classes", None)
        password = validated_data.pop("password", None)

        for attr, val in validated_data.items():
            setattr(instance, attr, val)

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=instance)
        if raw_role is not None:
            clean_role = str(raw_role).upper()
            profile.role = "ADMIN" if clean_role in ("SUPERADMIN", "FACULTY_ADMIN") else clean_role
            instance.is_staff = profile.role == "ADMIN"
            instance.is_superuser = profile.role == "ADMIN"

        if password:
            instance.set_password(password)
        instance.save()

        if phone is not None:
            profile.phone_number = normalize_ph_phone(phone)
        profile.is_profile_completed = bool(instance.first_name and instance.last_name)
        profile.save()

        if profile.faculty:
            fac = profile.faculty
            fac.first_name = instance.first_name or fac.first_name
            fac.last_name = instance.last_name or fac.last_name
            fac.email = instance.email or fac.email
            if employee_id:
                clean_emp = _clean_str(employee_id, 50)
                if (
                    FacultyProfile.objects.filter(employee_id__iexact=clean_emp)
                    .exclude(id=fac.id)
                    .exists()
                ):
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(
                        {"employee_id": "This Employee ID is already assigned."}
                    )
                fac.employee_id = clean_emp
            if phone is not None:
                fac.contact_number = profile.phone_number
            if department is not None:
                fac.department = department
            if position is not None:
                fac.position = position
            if can_handle is not None:
                fac.can_handle_classes = False if profile.role == "PARENT" else bool(can_handle)
            fac.is_active = instance.is_active
            fac.save()

        instance.profile = profile
        return instance

def _clean_input(value: object, max_length: int = 120) -> str:
    return strip_tags(str(value or "")).strip()[:max_length]


def build_user_session_payload(*, user, request=None) -> dict:
    """Builds the unified authenticated session payload in minimal queries."""
    profile = getattr(user, "profile", None) or (
        UserProfile.objects.select_related(
            "faculty",
            "faculty__department_ref",
            "faculty__position_ref",
        )
        .filter(user=user)
        .first()
    )
    faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    role = profile.role if profile else ("ADMIN" if user.is_superuser else "FACULTY")
    can_handle_classes = (
        False
        if role == "PARENT"
        else (bool(faculty.can_handle_classes) if faculty else True)
    )

    photo_field = (
        faculty.photo
        if (faculty and faculty.photo)
        else (profile.avatar if (profile and profile.avatar) else None)
    )
    photo_url = None
    if photo_field:
        photo_url = request.build_absolute_uri(photo_field.url) if request else photo_field.url

    return {
        "id": user.id,
        "username": user.username,
        "first_name": user.first_name or (faculty.first_name if faculty else ""),
        "middle_name": faculty.middle_name if faculty else "",
        "last_name": user.last_name or (faculty.last_name if faculty else ""),
        "suffix": faculty.suffix if faculty else "",
        "full_name": (
            faculty.full_name
            if faculty
            else (user.get_full_name() or user.username).strip()
        ),
        "email": user.email or (faculty.email if faculty else ""),
        "role": role,
        "faculty_id": faculty.id if faculty else None,
        "employee_id": faculty.employee_id if faculty else "",
        "department": faculty.department if faculty else "",
        "position": faculty.position if faculty else "",
        "contact_number": (
            faculty.contact_number
            if (faculty and faculty.contact_number)
            else (profile.phone_number if profile else "")
        ),
        "phone_number": (
            profile.phone_number
            if (profile and profile.phone_number)
            else (faculty.contact_number if faculty else "")
        ),
        "photo_url": photo_url,
        "can_handle_classes": can_handle_classes,
        "has_bound_device": bool(faculty and faculty.bound_device_id),
        "device_model": faculty.device_model if faculty else None,
        "is_profile_completed": bool(profile and profile.is_profile_completed),
    }


def authenticate_with_identifier(*, identifier: str, password: str, request=None) -> dict:
    clean_identifier = _clean_input(identifier, 150)
    if not clean_identifier or not password:
        raise AppError("MISSING_CREDENTIALS", "Username/Employee ID and password are required.", 400)

    normalized_phone = normalize_ph_phone(clean_identifier)
    lookup = (
        Q(username__iexact=clean_identifier)
        | Q(email__iexact=clean_identifier)
        | Q(profile__faculty__employee_id__iexact=clean_identifier)
    )
    if normalized_phone:
        lookup = lookup | Q(profile__phone_number=normalized_phone)

    existing_user = (
        User.objects.select_related(
            "profile",
            "profile__faculty",
            "profile__faculty__department_ref",
            "profile__faculty__position_ref",
        )
        .filter(lookup)
        .first()
    )

    profile = getattr(existing_user, "profile", None) if existing_user else None
    if profile and profile.is_locked():
        raise AppError(
            "ACCOUNT_LOCKED",
            "Too many failed sign-in attempts. Please wait 15 minutes or ask an administrator to unlock your account.",
            423,
        )

    target_username = existing_user.username if existing_user else clean_identifier
    authenticated_user = authenticate(request=request, username=target_username, password=password)

    if not authenticated_user:
        if profile:
            profile.record_failed_attempt()
        raise AppError("INVALID_CREDENTIALS", "Invalid account credentials.", 401)

    if not authenticated_user.is_active:
        raise AppError("INACTIVE_ACCOUNT", "This account is currently deactivated.", 403)

    if profile:
        profile.reset_failed_attempts()

    refresh = RefreshToken.for_user(authenticated_user)
    user_payload = build_user_session_payload(user=authenticated_user, request=request)
    return {
        "access": str(refresh.access_token),
        "refresh": str(refresh),
        "user": user_payload,
    }


@transaction.atomic
def update_own_profile(*, user, data: dict, files=None, request=None) -> dict:
    first_name = _clean_input(data.get("first_name", user.first_name), 100)
    middle_name = _clean_input(data.get("middle_name", ""), 100)
    last_name = _clean_input(data.get("last_name", user.last_name), 100)
    suffix = _clean_input(data.get("suffix", ""), 20)
    if suffix not in VALID_SUFFIXES:
        suffix = ""

    email = _clean_input(data.get("email", user.email), 150)
    raw_phone = data.get("phone_number") or data.get("contact_number")
    contact = normalize_ph_phone(raw_phone) if raw_phone is not None else None

    if email and User.objects.filter(email__iexact=email).exclude(pk=user.pk).exists():
        raise AppError("EMAIL_TAKEN", "This email address is already in use.", 400)

    user.first_name = first_name
    user.last_name = last_name
    user.email = email
    user.save(update_fields=["first_name", "last_name", "email"])

    photo_file = files.get("photo") or files.get("avatar") if files else None
    clear_photo = str(data.get("remove_photo", "")).lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")

    profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
    if contact is not None:
        profile.phone_number = contact
    if clear_photo:
        profile.avatar = None
    elif photo_file:
        profile.avatar = photo_file
    profile.is_profile_completed = bool(user.first_name and user.last_name)
    profile.save()

    faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    if faculty:
        faculty.first_name = first_name or faculty.first_name
        faculty.middle_name = middle_name
        faculty.last_name = last_name or faculty.last_name
        faculty.suffix = suffix
        faculty.email = email
        if contact is not None:
            faculty.contact_number = contact
        if "department" in data:
            faculty.department = _clean_input(data.get("department"), 100)
        if "position" in data:
            faculty.position = _clean_input(data.get("position"), 100)
        if clear_photo:
            faculty.photo = None
            faculty.photo_updated_at = timezone.now()
        elif photo_file:
            faculty.photo = photo_file
            faculty.photo_updated_at = timezone.now()
        faculty.save()

    return build_user_session_payload(user=user, request=request)


@transaction.atomic
def reset_faculty_bound_device(*, faculty_id: int) -> FacultyProfile:
    faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.select_related("account").filter(id=faculty_id).first()
    if not faculty:
        raise AppError("FACULTY_NOT_FOUND", "Faculty profile not found.", 404)
    faculty.bound_device_id = None
    faculty.device_model = None
    faculty.device_bound_at = None
    faculty.save(update_fields=["bound_device_id", "device_model", "device_bound_at", "updated_at"])
    return faculty


@transaction.atomic
def unlock_all_locked_accounts() -> int:
    """Clears failed sign-in counters and temporary lockouts across all UserProfiles."""
    return UserProfile.objects.filter(
        Q(failed_attempts__gt=0) | Q(locked_until__isnull=False)
    ).update(failed_attempts=0, locked_until=None)

    def test_01_auth_profile_validation_device_reset_and_unlock(self):
        # Login & JWT/Token issuance
        res = self.client.post("/api/v1/accounts/login/", {"username": "crud_admin", "password": "AttendSure2026!"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("access", res.data)
        self.assertIn("token", res.data)

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)

        # GET / PATCH /me/
        me_get = self.client.get("/api/v1/accounts/me/")
        self.assertEqual(me_get.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(me_get.data["username"], "crud_admin")

        me_patch = self.client.patch("/api/v1/accounts/me/", {"first_name": "Lead", "department": "Executive"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(me_patch.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(me_patch.data["first_name"], "Lead")

        # Validate field
        val = self.client.get("/api/v1/accounts/validate-field/", {"field": "username", "value": "crud_admin"})
        self.assertEqual(val.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(val.data["is_taken"])

        # Register mobile push token
        pt = self.client.post("/api/v1/accounts/push-token/", {"push_token": "ExponentPushToken[test123]"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(pt.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(pt.data["registered"])

        # Reset device binding
        reset_res = self.client.post(f"/api/v1/accounts/reset-device/{self.faculty_profile.id}/", {}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(reset_res.status_code, 200)
        self.faculty_profile.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(self.faculty_profile.bound_device_id)

        # Unlock accounts
        unlock_res = self.client.post("/api/v1/accounts/unlock-accounts/", {}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(unlock_res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(unlock_res.data["ok"])

        # Audit logs read
        logs_res = self.client.get("/api/v1/accounts/audit-logs/")
        self.assertEqual(logs_res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("records", logs_res.data)

    def test_02_faculty_and_users_full_crud(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)

        # Faculty CRUD (/v1/accounts/faculty/)
        create_f = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/accounts/faculty/",
            {
                "employee_id": "EMP-NEW-99",
                "first_name": "Carlos",
                "last_name": "Mendoza",
                "email": "carlos@attendsure.edu",
                "department": "Science",
                "position": "Faculty II",
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(create_f.status_code, 201)
        fid = create_f.data["id"]

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/accounts/faculty/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/accounts/faculty/dropdown/").status_code, 200)

        upd_f = self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/accounts/faculty/{fid}/", {"position": "Master Faculty I"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(upd_f.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(upd_f.data["position"], "Master Faculty I")

        del_f = self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/accounts/faculty/{fid}/")
        self.assertEqual(del_f.status_code, 204)

        # Users CRUD (/v1/accounts/users/) + self-delete guard
        create_u = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/accounts/users/",
            {
                "username": "temp_staff_user",
                "password": "TempPassword2026!",
                "first_name": "Ana",
                "last_name": "Cruz",
                "email": "ana@attendsure.edu",
                "employee_id": "EMP-ANA-01",
                "role": "FACULTY",
                "is_teaching": True,
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(create_u.status_code, 201)
        uid = create_u.data["id"]

        upd_u = self.client.patch(
            f"/api/v1/accounts/users/{uid}/",
            {"employee_id": "EMP-ANA-02", "department": "English"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(upd_u.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(upd_u.data["employee_id"], "EMP-ANA-02")
        self.assertEqual(upd_u.data["department"], "English")

        # Self-delete must be blocked with 400
        self_del = self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/accounts/users/{self.admin_user.id}/")
        self.assertEqual(self_del.status_code, 400)

        # Deleting other user succeeds
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/accounts/users/{uid}/").status_code, 204)

    def test_03_school_structure_and_holidays_full_crud(self):
        # Public branding works without auth
        pub = self.client.get("/api/v1/school/public-branding/")
        self.assertEqual(pub.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(pub.data["school_name"], "AttendSure National High School")

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)

        # School Settings GET / PUT / DELETE logo
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/school/settings/").status_code, 200)
        upd_set = self.client.put(
            "/api/v1/school/settings/",
            {"school_name": "AttendSure Science High", "geofence_radius_meters": 250},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(upd_set.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(upd_set.data["school_name"], "AttendSure Science High")

        del_logo = self.client.delete("/api/v1/school/settings/?field=school_logo")
        self.assertEqual(del_logo.status_code, 200)

        # School Years CRUD
        sy_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/school/school-years/",
            {"code": "2027-2028", "start_date": "2027-06-01", "baseline_cutoff_date": "2027-06-05", "end_date": "2028-03-31", "is_active": False},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(sy_c.status_code, 201)
        sy_id = sy_c.data["id"]
        self.assertEqual(self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/school/school-years/{sy_id}/", {"end_date": "2028-04-15"}, format="json").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/school-years/{sy_id}/").status_code, 204)

        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/rooms/{rid}/").status_code, 204)

        # Subjects CRUD
        sub_c = self.client.post("/api/v1/school/subjects/", {"code": "SCI7", "title": "Science 7", "units": 2}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(sub_c.status_code, 201)
        sub_id = sub_c.data["id"]
        self.assertEqual(self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/school/subjects/{sub_id}/", {"title": "General Science 7"}, format="json").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/subjects/{sub_id}/").status_code, 204)

        # Sections CRUD
        sec_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/school/sections/",
            {"name": "Emerald", "grade_level": self.grade.id, "adviser": self.faculty_profile.id, "room_number": "Room 102"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(sec_c.status_code, 201)
        sec_id = sec_c.data["id"]
        self.assertEqual(self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/school/sections/{sec_id}/", {"capacity": 50}, format="json").status_code, 200)

        # Class Periods CRUD
        per_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/school/periods/",
            {
                "section": sec_id,
                "subject": self.subject.id,
                "faculty_id": self.faculty_profile.id,
                "start_time": "09:00",
                "end_time": "10:00",
                "days_of_week": "MON-FRI",
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(per_c.status_code, 201)
        per_id = per_c.data["id"]
        self.assertEqual(self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/school/periods/{per_id}/", {"end_time": "10:15"}, format="json").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/periods/{per_id}/").status_code, 204)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/sections/{sec_id}/").status_code, 204)

        # School Holidays CRUD + Partial PATCH + Collection PATCH (Weekend Rest toggle)
        wk = self.client.patch("/api/v1/school/holidays/", {"weekend_rest_enabled": False}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(wk.status_code, 200)
        self.assertFalse(wk.data["weekend_rest_enabled"])
        wk_legacy = self.client.patch("/api/calendar/exceptions/", {"weekend_rest_enabled": True}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(wk_legacy.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(wk_legacy.data["weekend_rest_enabled"])

        hol_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/school/holidays/",
            {"date": "2026-08-31", "type": "HOLIDAY", "reason": "National Heroes Day"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(hol_c.status_code, 201)
        hid = hol_c.data["id"]
        # Partial PATCH updating only date must succeed!
        hol_p = self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/school/holidays/{hid}/", {"date": "2026-09-01"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(hol_p.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(hol_p.data["date"], "2026-09-01")
        self.assertEqual(hol_p.data["reason"], "National Heroes Day")
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/school/holidays/{hid}/").status_code, 204)

    def test_04_students_enrollments_and_parent_portal_crud(self):

    def test_05_attendance_qr_scan_offline_sync_manual_crud_and_exit_passes(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.faculty_user)

        # Signed QR Scan
        signed_qr = sign_student_qr(self.student.lrn, self.student.qr_token)
        scan = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/attendance/scan/",
            {"qr_text": signed_qr, "schedule_id": self.period.id},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(scan.status_code, 201)
        self.assertTrue(scan.data["ok"])

        # Offline Queue Sync (duplicate handling)
        sync = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/attendance/offline-sync/",
            {"scans": [{"qr_token": signed_qr, "schedule_id": self.period.id, "scanned_at": timezone.now().isoformat()}]},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(sync.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(sync.data["duplicate_count"], 1)

        # Assigned classes & adviser matrix
        classes = self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/classes/")
        self.assertEqual(classes.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(len(classes.data) >= 1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(classes.data[0]["students"][0]["attendance_record_id"])

        matrix = self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/adviser-matrix/")
        self.assertEqual(matrix.status_code, 200)

        # Attendance Records Read / Update / Delete + Parent RBAC block
        recs = self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/records/")
        self.assertEqual(recs.status_code, 200)
        rec_id = recs.data["records"][0]["id"]

        upd_rec = self.client.put("/api/v1/attendance/records/", {"id": rec_id, "status": "EXCUSED"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(upd_rec.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(upd_rec.data["status"], "EXCUSED")

        # Parent cannot delete attendance record
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.parent_user)
        parent_del = self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/attendance/records/?id={rec_id}")
        self.assertEqual(parent_del.status_code, 403)

        # Faculty can delete attendance record
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.faculty_user)
        del_rec = self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/attendance/records/?id={rec_id}")
        self.assertEqual(del_rec.status_code, 200)

        # Re-create manual attendance
        man_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/attendance/records/",
            {"student_id": self.student.id, "schedule_id": self.period.id, "status": "PRESENT"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(man_c.status_code, 201)

        # Exit Passes full CRUD + Approve / Decline / Mark Used + PATCH preserves valid window
        now = timezone.now()
        ep_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/",
            {
                "bearer_type": "FACULTY",
                "pass_type": "Official Travel",
                "reason": "Division Math Seminar",
                "valid_from": now.isoformat(),
                "valid_until": (now + timedelta(hours=3)).isoformat(),
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(ep_c.status_code, 201)
        ep_id = ep_c.data["id"]
        orig_valid_until = ep_c.data["valid_until"]

        # Partial PATCH updating only reason must preserve valid_until
        ep_p = self.client.patch(f"/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/{ep_id}/", {"reason": "Updated Seminar Location"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(ep_p.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(ep_p.data["reason"], "Updated Seminar Location")
        self.assertEqual(ep_p.data["valid_until"], orig_valid_until)

        # List exit passes works cleanly for both faculty & leadership
        ep_list = self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/")
        self.assertEqual(ep_list.status_code, 200)

        # Admin approves & marks used
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(f"/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/{ep_id}/approve/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(f"/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/{ep_id}/mark-used/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/{ep_id}/").status_code, 204)

    def test_06_campus_gps_messaging_chat_and_reports_full_crud(self):
        # Campus Boundary + Faculty Check-In + Audit + Anomalies + Emergency Roll-Call
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        b_put = self.client.put(
            "/api/v1/campus/boundary/",
            {"name": "Main Campus", "latitude": 8.4858, "longitude": 124.6567, "radius_meters": 220},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(b_put.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(b_put.data["radius_meters"], 220)

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.faculty_user)
        # Mismatched phone hardware ID must be rejected (1-to-1 device binding)
        bad_dev = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/campus/check-in/",
            {
                "latitude": 8.4858,
                "longitude": 124.6567,
                "accuracy": 10,
                "battery_level": 88,
                "face_verified": True,
                "liveness_passed": True,
                "client_device_id": "DEV-WRONG-999",
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(bad_dev.status_code, 403)

        # Bound phone hardware ID succeeds
        chk = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/campus/check-in/",
            {
                "latitude": 8.4858,
                "longitude": 124.6567,
                "accuracy": 10,
                "battery_level": 88,
                "face_verified": True,
                "liveness_passed": True,
                "client_device_id": "DEV-ORIG-001",
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(chk.status_code, 201)
        self.assertTrue(chk.data["is_inside_geofence"])

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/campus/audit/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/campus/anomalies/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/campus/roll-call/").status_code, 200)

        # Parent must be blocked from emergency roll-call
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.parent_user)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/campus/roll-call/").status_code, 403)

        # Push Notifications Broadcast + Read + Update + Mark-Read + Delete
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        notif_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/messaging/notifications/",
            {
                "title": "Early Dismissal",
                "message": "Classes end at 3:00 PM today.",
                "category": "ANNOUNCEMENT",
                "target_role": "PARENT",
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(notif_c.status_code, 201)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(notif_c.data["dispatched_count"], 1)
        nid = notif_c.data["id"]

        notif_list = self.client.get("/api/v1/messaging/notifications/", {"scope": "all"})
        self.assertEqual(notif_list.status_code, 200)

        notif_u = self.client.patch(
            f"/api/v1/messaging/notifications/{nid}/",
            {"title": "Early Dismissal (Updated)"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(notif_u.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(notif_u.data["title"], "Early Dismissal (Updated)")

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.parent_user)
        mr = self.client.post("/api/v1/messaging/notifications/mark-read/", {"ids": [nid]}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(mr.status_code, 200)

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/messaging/notifications/{nid}/").status_code, 204)

        # Parent-Faculty Chat full CRUD
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.faculty_user)
        threads = self.client.get("/api/v1/messaging/threads/")
        self.assertEqual(threads.status_code, 200)

        msg_c = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/messaging/messages/",
            {"student_id": self.student.id, "faculty_id": self.faculty_profile.id, "body": "Hello parent!"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(msg_c.status_code, 201)
        mid = msg_c.data["id"]

        msg_u = self.client.put("/api/v1/messaging/messages/", {"id": mid, "body": "Hello parent, updated!"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(msg_u.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(msg_u.data["body"], "Hello parent, updated!")

        # Invalid message ID returns clean 400
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete("/api/v1/messaging/messages/?id=invalid").status_code, 400)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.delete(f"/api/v1/messaging/messages/?id={mid}").status_code, 200)

        # All Reports, Analytics, DTR Actions, Health & Cache Maintenance
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        now = timezone.localdate()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/dashboard/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/sf1/", {"section_id": self.section.id}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/sf2/", {"section_id": self.section.id, "month": now.month, "year": now.year}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/v1/reports/sf2/{self.section.id}/", {"month": "November", "year": 2026}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/sf4/", {"month": now.month, "year": now.year, "school_year": "2026-2027"}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/dtr/", {"faculty_id": self.faculty_profile.id, "month": now.month, "year": now.year}).status_code, 200)

        # DTR submit & approve actions
        dtr_sub = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/reports/dtr/",
            {"action": "SUBMIT_DTR", "faculty_id": self.faculty_profile.id, "month": now.month, "year": now.year, "regular_hours": "8:00 AM - 5:00 PM"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(dtr_sub.status_code, 200)
        dtr_app = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/reports/dtr/",
            {"action": "APPROVE_DEPT_HEAD", "faculty_id": self.faculty_profile.id, "month": now.month, "year": now.year},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(dtr_app.status_code, 200)

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/export/", {"type": "sf1", "section_id": self.section.id}).status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/edfi-export/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.post(
                "/api/v1/reports/audit-logs/",
                {"action": "PRINT", "report_name": "SF1", "section_id": self.section.id, "document_tracking_id": "TRK-001"},
                format="json",
            ).status_code,
            201,
        )
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/audit-logs/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/dropout-risk/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/sdg-impact/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/system-status/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post("/api/v1/reports/system-status/", {"action": "RETRY_FAILED"}, format="json").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/v1/health/db/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post("/api/v1/cache/flush/").status_code, 200)

    def test_07_students_bulk_csv_class_list_import_and_unsigned_qr_defense(self):
        csv_body = (
            "lrn,first_name,middle_name,last_name,suffix,sex,birthdate,section_name,grade_level,parent_contact,barangay,municipality_city,province\n"
            "127901260501,Miguel,Reyes,Navarro,,Male,2013-03-12,Diamond,Grade 7,09171234501,Macasandig,Cagayan de Oro City,Misamis Oriental\n"
            "127901260502,Angela,Cruz,Ramos,,Female,2013-08-24,Sapphire,Grade 8,09171234502,Nazareth,Cagayan de Oro City,Misamis Oriental\n"
            "BADLRN,Invalid,Row,Test,,Male,2013-01-01,Diamond,Grade 7,09171234503,Carmen,Cagayan de Oro City,Misamis Oriental\n"
        ).encode("utf-8")

        # Non-leadership faculty must be blocked from bulk CSV import (403)
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.faculty_user)
        denied = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/students/bulk-import/",
            {"file": SimpleUploadedFile("class_list.csv", csv_body, content_type="text/csv")},
            format="multipart",
        )
        self.assertEqual(denied.status_code, 403)

        # Unsigned raw LRN scan must be rejected with 400 when ALLOW_LEGACY_UNSIGNED_QR=False
        unsigned_scan = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/attendance/scan/",
            {"qr_text": self.student.lrn, "schedule_id": self.period.id},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(unsigned_scan.status_code, 400)

        # School leadership imports valid rows, auto-creates Sapphire, and skips invalid LRN
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        res1 = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/students/bulk-import/",
            {"file": SimpleUploadedFile("class_list.csv", csv_body, content_type="text/csv")},
            format="multipart",
        )
        self.assertEqual(res1.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res1.data["created_count"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(res1.data["skipped_count"], 1)

        # Re-uploading updates existing LRNs cleanly without duplicating students
        res2 = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/students/bulk-import/",
            {"file": SimpleUploadedFile("class_list.csv", csv_body, content_type="text/csv")},
            format="multipart",
        )
        self.assertEqual(res2.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res2.data["created_count"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res2.data["updated_count"], 2)

class SecurityAndUtilityUnitTests(APITestCase):
    def test_international_and_ph_phone_normalization(self):
        self.assertEqual(normalize_ph_phone("0917-123-4567"), "+639171234567")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_ph_phone("9171234567"), "+639171234567")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_ph_phone("639171234567"), "+639171234567")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_international_phone("+1 (415) 555-0199"), "+14155550199")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_international_phone("+44 7700 900077"), "+447700900077")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_international_phone("0065 9123 4567"), "+6591234567")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_international_phone(""), "")

    def test_gps_distance_handles_finite_and_invalid_values(self):
        dist = calculate_distance_meters(8.4858, 124.6567, 8.4858, 124.6567)
        self.assertEqual(dist, 0.0)
        self.assertTrue(math.isinf(calculate_distance_meters(float("nan"), 124.6567, 8.4858, 124.6567)))
        self.assertTrue(math.isinf(calculate_distance_meters(float("inf"), 124.6567, 8.4858, 124.6567)))
        verdict = verify_campus_gps(
            latitude=float("inf"),
            longitude=124.6567,
            campus_lat=8.4858,
            campus_lon=124.6567,
            accuracy_meters=float("nan"),
        )
        self.assertFalse(verdict["is_valid"])
        self.assertFalse(verdict["is_inside"])

    @override_settings(ALLOW_LEGACY_UNSIGNED_QR=False)
    def test_qr_signer_accepts_signed_and_rejects_unsigned_by_default(self):
        signed = sign_student_qr("127901269999", "STU-TOKEN-999")
        verified = verify_student_qr(signed)
        self.assertIsNotNone(verified)
        self.assertTrue(verified.is_signed)
        self.assertEqual(verified.lrn, "127901269999")
        self.assertEqual(verified.qr_token, "STU-TOKEN-999")

        self.assertIsNone(verify_student_qr("STU-TOKEN-999"))
        self.assertIsNone(verify_student_qr("ATTENDSURE:127901269999:STU-TOKEN-999:FORGED000000"))

    def test_empty_email_parent_cannot_hijack_teacher_profile(self):
        FacultyProfile.objects.create(
            employee_id="EMP-EMPTY-1",
            first_name="Real",
            last_name="Teacher",
            email="",
            is_active=True,
        )
        parent_user = User.objects.create_user(username="parent_empty", password="Password123!", email="")
        UserProfile.objects.create(user=parent_user, role="PARENT")

        self.assertIsNone(resolve_faculty_for_user(parent_user))

    def test_expired_lockout_resets_counter_on_next_failure(self):
        u = User.objects.create_user(username="lock_tester", password="Password123!")
        profile = UserProfile.objects.create(
            user=u,
            role="FACULTY",
            failed_attempts=5,
            locked_until=timezone.now() - timezone.timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        self.assertFalse(profile.is_locked())
        profile.record_failed_attempt()
        profile.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(profile.failed_attempts, 1)
        self.assertIsNone(profile.locked_until)


class CompetitionEndpointsAndRbacTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        cache.clear()
        self.admin_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username="demo_admin", password="AttendSure2026!", is_staff=True, is_superuser=True
        )
        UserProfile.objects.create(user=self.admin_user, role="ADMIN", is_profile_completed=True)

        self.teacher_profile = FacultyProfile.objects.create(
            employee_id="EMP-T1",
            first_name="Maria",
            last_name="Clara",
            email="maria@attendsure.edu",
            can_handle_classes=True,
            is_active=True,
        )
        self.teacher_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username="demo_teacher", password="AttendSure2026!", email="maria@attendsure.edu"
        )
        UserProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.teacher_user,
            faculty=self.teacher_profile,
            role="FACULTY",
            is_profile_completed=True,
        )

        self.parent_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username="demo_parent", password="AttendSure2026!", email="parent@attendsure.edu"
        )
        UserProfile.objects.create(user=self.parent_user, role="PARENT", is_profile_completed=True)

        self.sy = SchoolYear.objects.create(
            code="2026-2027",
            start_date=date(2026, 6, 1),
            baseline_cutoff_date=date(2026, 6, 5),
            end_date=date(2027, 3, 31),
            is_active=True,
        )
        self.div = AdministrativeDivision.objects.create(division="Global Pilot Division")
        SchoolSettings.objects.create(
            school_id="304118",
            school_name="AttendSure International Pilot High School",
            division_ref=self.div,
            active_school_year=self.sy,
        )
        self.grade = GradeLevel.objects.create(code="G7", name="Grade 7", level_order=7)
        self.section = Section.objects.create(
            school_year=self.sy, grade_level=self.grade, name="Newton", adviser=self.teacher_profile
        )
        self.subject = Subject.objects.create(code="MATH7", title="Math 7", grade_level=self.grade)
        self.period = ClassPeriod.objects.create(
            schedule_code="P1",
            section=self.section,
            subject=self.subject,
            faculty=self.teacher_profile,
            start_time=time(8, 0),
            end_time=time(9, 0),
        )
        self.student = Student.objects.create(
            lrn="127901269999",
            first_name="Sofia",
            last_name="Bautista",
            sex="Female",
            birthdate=date(2012, 5, 10),
            parent_contact="+639171234567",
            parent_user=self.parent_user,

            section=self.section,

    def test_edfi_v6_export_standard_and_gdpr_pseudonymized_and_rbac(self):
        # Non-leaders cannot export Ed-Fi datasets
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.teacher_user)
        denied = self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/edfi-export/")
        self.assertEqual(denied.status_code, 403)

        # Leaders receive Ed-Fi v6.0 standard export
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        std_res = self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/edfi-export/?days=14&anonymize=0")
        self.assertEqual(std_res.status_code, 200)
        std_json = std_res.json()
        self.assertEqual(std_json["edFiVersion"], "6.0")
        self.assertEqual(std_json["privacyMode"], "STANDARD_INSTITUTIONAL")
        self.assertEqual(std_json["students"][0]["firstName"], "Sofia")
        self.assertEqual(std_json["students"][0]["studentUniqueId"], "127901269999")

        # GDPR pseudonymized mode strips PII and replaces LRN with deterministic SHA-256 pseudonym
        anon_res = self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/edfi-export/?days=14&anonymize=1")
        self.assertEqual(anon_res.status_code, 200)
        anon_json = anon_res.json()
        self.assertEqual(anon_json["privacyMode"], "GDPR_PSEUDONYMIZED")
        self.assertNotIn("firstName", anon_json["students"][0])
        self.assertTrue(anon_json["students"][0]["studentUniqueId"].startswith("STU-"))
        self.assertEqual(anon_json["students"][0]["birthDate"], "2012-01-01")

    def test_seed_competition_demo_command_is_idempotent(self):
        call_command("seed_competition_demo", verbosity=0)
        count_first = Student.objects.count()
        call_command("seed_competition_demo", verbosity=0)
        count_second = Student.objects.count()
        self.assertGreaterEqual(count_first, 24)
        self.assertEqual(count_first, count_second)

    def test_exit_passes_sf2_and_sf4_3nf_regression(self):
        now = timezone.now()
        ExitPass.objects.create(
            pass_number="EP-REG-001",
            faculty=self.teacher_profile,
            issued_by=self.teacher_profile,
            pass_type="Official Travel",
            reason="Division conference",
            valid_from=now,
            valid_until=now + timezone.timedelta(hours=3),
            status="ACTIVE",
        )
        ExitPass.objects.create(
            pass_number="EP-REG-002",
            student=self.student,
            issued_by=self.teacher_profile,
            pass_type="Clinic Clearance",
            reason="Medical checkup",
            valid_from=now,
            valid_until=now + timezone.timedelta(hours=2),
            status="ACTIVE",
        )

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin_user)
        ep_res = self.client.get("/api/v1/attendance/exit-passes/")
        self.assertEqual(ep_res.status_code, 200)
        results = ep_res.data.get("results", ep_res.data)
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 2)
        faculty_row = next(r for r in results if r["pass_number"] == "EP-REG-001")
        self.assertEqual(faculty_row["bearer_type"], "FACULTY")
        self.assertEqual(faculty_row["faculty"], self.teacher_profile.id)
        self.assertEqual(faculty_row["teacher"], self.teacher_profile.id)
        self.assertEqual(faculty_row["faculty_name"], self.teacher_profile.full_name)

        sf2_res = self.client.get(f"/api/v1/reports/sf2/{self.section.id}/?month=November&year=2026")
        self.assertEqual(sf2_res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(sf2_res.data["section_name"], "Newton")

        sf4_res = self.client.get("/api/v1/reports/sf4/?month=November&year=2026&school_year=2026-2027")
        self.assertEqual(sf4_res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(sf4_res.data["school_year"], "2026-2027")

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\accounts\views.py
from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError as DjangoValidationError
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status, viewsets
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.throttling import AnonRateThrottle, UserRateThrottle
from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework_simplejwt.tokens import RefreshToken

from common.audit import AuditedModelViewSet, record_audit_log
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.permissions import IsSchoolLeader, get_user_role, is_school_leader
from common.phone import normalize_ph_phone
from .models import AuditLog, FacultyProfile, UserProfile

    FacultyDropdownSerializer,
    FacultyProfileSerializer,
    UserAccountSerializer,
)

    authenticate_with_identifier,
    build_user_session_payload,
    reset_faculty_bound_device,
    unlock_all_locked_accounts,
    update_own_profile,
)

User = get_user_model()


class LoginThrottle(AnonRateThrottle):
    rate = "15/minute"


class AccountWriteThrottle(UserRateThrottle):
    rate = "30/minute"


def _format_audit_row(item: AuditLog) -> dict:
    performer = (
        (item.performed_by.get_full_name() or item.performed_by.username).strip()
        if item.performed_by
        else "System"
    )
    return {
        "id": item.id,
        "table_name": item.table_name,
        "record_id": item.record_id,
        "action": item.action,
        "performed_by": performer,
        "user": performer,
        "client_ip": item.client_ip or "",
        "ip_address": item.client_ip or "",
        "changed_fields": item.changed_fields,
        "new_data": item.new_data,
        "created_at": timezone.localtime(item.created_at).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p"),
    }


def _is_judge_demo_enabled() -> bool:
    return bool(getattr(settings, "DEBUG", False) or getattr(settings, "ENABLE_JUDGE_DEMO", False))


class LoginView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]
    throttle_classes = [LoginThrottle]

    def post(self, request):
        identifier = (
            request.data.get("username")
            or request.data.get("identifier")
            or request.data.get("email")
            or ""
        )
        password = request.data.get("password") or ""
        payload = authenticate_with_identifier(
            identifier=identifier,
            password=password,
            request=request,
        )
        user_obj = User.objects.filter(id=payload["user"]["id"]).first()
        if user_obj:
            drf_token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user_obj)
            payload["token"] = drf_token.key

        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name="auth_user",
            record_id=payload["user"]["id"],
            action="LOGIN",
            new_data={"username": payload["user"]["username"], "role": payload["user"]["role"]},
        )
        return Response(payload, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class DemoStatusView(APIView):
    """Reports whether 1-click competition judge login is enabled by server config."""

    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response({"enabled": _is_judge_demo_enabled()})


class DemoLoginView(APIView):
    """Authenticates a real seeded database account ONLY when DEBUG or ENABLE_JUDGE_DEMO is True."""

    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]
    throttle_classes = [LoginThrottle]

    ROLE_MAP = {
        "ADMIN": ("demo_admin", ["ADMIN"]),
        "PRINCIPAL": ("demo_principal", ["PRINCIPAL", "ADMIN"]),
        "DEPT_HEAD": ("demo_dept_head", ["DEPT_HEAD", "PRINCIPAL"]),
        "FACULTY": ("demo_teacher", ["FACULTY"]),
        "TEACHER": ("demo_teacher", ["FACULTY"]),
        "PARENT": ("demo_parent", ["PARENT"]),
    }

    def post(self, request):
        if not _is_judge_demo_enabled():
            return Response(
                {"error": "Demo sign-in is disabled in production."},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        role_key = str(request.data.get("role") or "ADMIN").strip().upper()
        preferred_username, allowed_roles = self.ROLE_MAP.get(role_key, ("demo_admin", ["ADMIN"]))

        user_obj = (
            User.objects.select_related("profile", "profile__faculty")
            .filter(username__iexact=preferred_username, is_active=True)
            .first()
        )
        if not user_obj:
            user_obj = (
                User.objects.select_related("profile", "profile__faculty")
                .filter(is_active=True, profile__role__in=allowed_roles)
                .order_by("id")
                .first()
            )
        if not user_obj:
            raise AppError(
                "ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND",
                "No active database account exists for this role yet.",
                404,
            )

        refresh = RefreshToken.for_user(user_obj)
        drf_token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user_obj)
        session_user = build_user_session_payload(user=user_obj, request=request)

        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name="auth_user",
            record_id=user_obj.id,
            action="LOGIN",
            new_data={"username": user_obj.username, "mode": "JUDGE_DEMO"},
        )
        return Response(
            {
                "access": str(refresh.access_token),
                "refresh": str(refresh),
                "token": drf_token.key,
                "user": session_user,
            }
        )


class CurrentUserView(APIView):
    """Returns and updates the signed-in user's own profile."""

    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(build_user_session_payload(user=request.user, request=request))

    def put(self, request):
        payload = update_own_profile(
            user=request.user,
            data=request.data,
            files=request.FILES,
            request=request,
        )
        changed_keys = [
            str(k)
            for k in (request.data.keys() if hasattr(request.data, "keys") else ["profile"])
            if str(k) not in ("password", "csrfmiddlewaretoken")
        ]
        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name="user_profiles",
            record_id=request.user.id,
            action="PROFILE",
            changed_fields=changed_keys or ["profile"],
        )
        return Response(payload)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)


CurrentProfileView = CurrentUserView


class ChangePasswordView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    throttle_classes = [AccountWriteThrottle]

    def post(self, request):
        current_password = str(
            request.data.get("current_password") or request.data.get("old_password") or ""
        )
        new_password = str(request.data.get("new_password") or "")
        if not current_password or not new_password:
            return Response(
                {"error": "Both current password and new password are required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        if not request.user.check_password(current_password):
            return Response(
                {"error": "Current password is incorrect."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        try:
            validate_password(new_password, user=request.user)
        except DjangoValidationError as exc:
            return Response({"error": " ".join(exc.messages)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        request.user.set_password(new_password)
        request.user.save(update_fields=["password"])
        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name="auth_user",
            record_id=request.user.id,
            action="PASSWORD",
        )
        return Response({"ok": True, "message": "Password updated."})


class FieldAvailabilityView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        field = str(request.query_params.get("field") or "").strip().lower()
        value = str(request.query_params.get("value") or "").strip()
        exclude_id = request.query_params.get("exclude_id")

        if not field or not value:
            return Response({"available": True, "is_taken": False})

        if field == "username":
            qs = User.objects.filter(username__iexact=value)
        elif field == "email":
            qs = User.objects.filter(email__iexact=value)
        elif field == "employee_id":
            qs = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(employee_id__iexact=value)
        elif field in ("phone", "phone_number", "contact_number"):
            norm = normalize_ph_phone(value)
            qs = UserProfile.objects.filter(phone_number=norm)
        else:
            return Response({"available": True, "is_taken": False})

        if exclude_id and str(exclude_id).isascii() and str(exclude_id).isdigit():
            qs = qs.exclude(id=int(exclude_id))

        taken = qs.exists()
        return Response({"available": not taken, "is_taken": taken})


ValidateFieldView = FieldAvailabilityView


class RegisterPushTokenView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    throttle_classes = [AccountWriteThrottle]

    def post(self, request):
        token = str(request.data.get("push_token") or request.data.get("token") or "").strip()
        if token and not token.startswith(("ExponentPushToken[", "ExpoPushToken[")):
            return Response(
                {"error": "Invalid mobile push token format."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=request.user)
        profile.push_token = token or None
        profile.save(update_fields=["push_token"])
        return Response({"ok": True, "registered": True})


SavePushTokenView = RegisterPushTokenView


class ResetBoundDeviceView(APIView):
    """Clears the phone hardware lock on a faculty profile so a replacement phone can be paired."""

    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsSchoolLeader]

    def post(self, request, pk: int | None = None, **kwargs):
        target_id = (
            pk
            or kwargs.get("faculty_id")
            or request.data.get("faculty_id")
            or request.data.get("teacher_id")
        )
        if not target_id or not str(target_id).isascii() or not str(target_id).isdigit():
            raise AppError("INVALID_ID", "Valid faculty profile ID is required.", 400)

        faculty = reset_faculty_bound_device(faculty_id=int(target_id))
        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name=faculty._meta.db_table,
            record_id=faculty.id,
            action="RESET_DEV",
            new_data={"employee_id": faculty.employee_id},
        )
        return Response({"ok": True, "message": f"Phone lock cleared for {faculty.full_name}."})


ResetDeviceBindingView = ResetBoundDeviceView
