class FacultyProfileViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = FacultyProfileSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = FacultyProfile.objects.select_related(
            "account",
            "department_ref",
            "position_ref",
        ).order_by("last_name", "first_name")

        search = str(self.request.query_params.get("search") or "").strip()
        if search:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(employee_id__icontains=search)
                | Q(first_name__icontains=search)
                | Q(last_name__icontains=search)
                | Q(department_ref__name__icontains=search)
            )

        active_only = str(self.request.query_params.get("is_active") or "").strip().lower()
        if active_only in ("1", "true", "yes"):
            qs = qs.filter(is_active=True)

        return qs

    def check_permissions(self, request):
        super().check_permissions(request)
        if request.method not in permissions.SAFE_METHODS and not is_school_leader(request.user):
            self.permission_denied(request, message="Only school leaders can modify faculty records.")


class UserAccountViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = UserAccountSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = User.objects.select_related(
            "profile",
            "profile__faculty",
            "profile__faculty__department_ref",
            "profile__faculty__position_ref",
        ).order_by("username")

        if get_user_role(self.request.user) != "ADMIN" and not self.request.user.is_superuser:
            return qs.filter(id=self.request.user.id)

        search = str(self.request.query_params.get("search") or "").strip()
        if search:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(username__icontains=search)
                | Q(first_name__icontains=search)
                | Q(last_name__icontains=search)
                | Q(email__icontains=search)
            )
        return qs

    def check_permissions(self, request):
        super().check_permissions(request)
        if request.method not in permissions.SAFE_METHODS:
            if get_user_role(request.user) != "ADMIN" and not request.user.is_superuser:
                self.permission_denied(
                    request, message="Only system administrators can manage user accounts."
                )

    def destroy(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        instance = self.get_object()
        if instance.id == request.user.id:
            raise AppError("CANNOT_DELETE_SELF", "You cannot delete your own active account.", 400)
        return super().destroy(request, *args, **kwargs)


class AuditLogListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsSchoolLeader]

    def get(self, request):
        limit_raw = str(request.query_params.get("limit") or "100")
        limit = min(int(limit_raw), 500) if (limit_raw.isascii() and limit_raw.isdigit()) else 100
        logs = AuditLog.objects.select_related("performed_by").order_by("-created_at")[:limit]
        results = [_format_audit_row(item) for item in logs]
        return Response({"results": results, "records": results, "count": len(results)})


class AuditLogViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsSchoolLeader]
    queryset = AuditLog.objects.select_related("performed_by").order_by("-created_at")

    def list(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return AuditLogListView().get(request)

from django.db import models
from django.db.models import CheckConstraint, Q, UniqueConstraint
from django.utils import timezone

ATTENDANCE_STATUS_CHOICES = [
    ("PRESENT", "Present"),
    ("LATE", "Late"),
    ("CUTTING", "Cutting Classes"),
    ("ABSENT", "Absent"),
    ("EXCUSED", "Excused"),
]

SCAN_METHOD_CHOICES = [
    ("QR", "Printed QR ID Scan"),
    ("MANUAL", "Manual Class List Entry"),
]

EXIT_PASS_STATUS_CHOICES = [
    ("PENDING", "Pending Approval"),
    ("ACTIVE", "Active"),
    ("USED", "Used"),
    ("EXPIRED", "Expired"),
    ("REVOKED", "Revoked"),
]


class ClassAttendance(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey("students.Student", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="class_attendances")
    class_period = models.ForeignKey(
        "school.ClassPeriod", on_delete=models.CASCADE, db_column="schedule_id", related_name="attendance_records"
    )
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile", on_delete=models.CASCADE, db_column="faculty_id", related_name="recorded_scans"
    )
    attendance_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    scanned_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now, db_index=True)
    scan_method = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=SCAN_METHOD_CHOICES, default="QR")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ATTENDANCE_STATUS_CHOICES, default="PRESENT")

    class Meta:
        db_table = "subject_attendance_logs"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["student", "class_period", "attendance_date"],
                name="uq_class_attendance_daily",
            )
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["attendance_date", "student"], name="idx_clsatt_date_stu"),
            models.Index(fields=["class_period", "attendance_date"], name="idx_clsatt_per_date"),
        ]


class DailyAttendance(models.Model):
    """
    3NF Daily Summary:
    - Keyed strictly by (student, attendance_date).
    - Section is resolved through Enrollment without storing redundant section_id.
    """

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey("students.Student", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="daily_attendances")
    attendance_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ATTENDANCE_STATUS_CHOICES, default="PRESENT", db_index=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")
    recorded_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "daily_attendance_summaries"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["student", "attendance_date"], name="uq_daily_attendance_student_date")
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["attendance_date", "status"], name="idx_dailyatt_date_status"),
        ]


class ExitPass(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    pass_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, db_index=True)
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="exit_passes",
    )
    student = models.ForeignKey(
        "students.Student", on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="exit_passes"
    )
    issued_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="issued_exit_passes"
    )
    pass_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, default="Exit Permit")
    reason = models.TextField()
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to="exit_passes/", null=True, blank=True)
    valid_from = models.DateTimeField()
    valid_until = models.DateTimeField(db_column="valid_to")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=EXIT_PASS_STATUS_CHOICES, default="ACTIVE", db_index=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "gate_passes"
        constraints = [
            CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    (Q(student__isnull=False) & Q(faculty__isnull=True))
                    | (Q(student__isnull=True) & Q(faculty__isnull=False))
                ),
                name="ck_exitpass_single_bearer",
            )
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["status", "-created_at"], name="idx_exitpass_status_created"),
        ]

from django.db.models import Count, Q
from django.utils import timezone
from common.media import build_media_url
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from common.qr_signer import sign_student_qr
from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod, Section
from apps.school.selectors import format_section_label
from apps.students.models import Enrollment
from .models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance, ExitPass

FacultyModel = Section._meta.get_field("adviser").related_model
PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD = next(
    f.name for f in ClassPeriod._meta.fields if f.db_column == "faculty_id"
)
PASS_FACULTY_FIELD = next(
    f.name for f in ExitPass._meta.fields if f.db_column == "faculty_id"
)


def resolve_faculty_for_user(user):
    if not user or not getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False):
        return None
    if get_user_role(user) == "PARENT":
        return None

    by_account = (
        FacultyModel.objects.select_related("account")
        .filter(account__user=user, is_active=True)
        .first()
    )
    if by_account:
        return by_account

    lookup = Q()
    clean_username = str(getattr(user, "username", "") or "").strip()
    clean_email = str(getattr(user, "email", "") or "").strip()
    if clean_username:
        lookup |= Q(employee_id__iexact=clean_username)
    if clean_email:
        lookup |= Q(email__iexact=clean_email)

    if lookup:
        return (
            FacultyModel.objects.select_related("account")
            .filter(lookup, is_active=True)
            .first()
        )
    return None


def get_faculty_section_ids(faculty_member) -> set[int]:
    if not faculty_member:
        return set()
    advisory_ids = set(
        Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty_member, is_active=True).values_list("id", flat=True)
    )
    period_sec_ids = set(
        faculty_member.assigned_periods.filter(is_active=True, section__is_active=True)
        .exclude(section_id__isnull=True)
        .values_list("section_id", flat=True)
    )
    return advisory_ids | period_sec_ids


def get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member) -> set[int]:
    section_ids = get_faculty_section_ids(faculty_member)
    if not section_ids:
        return set()
    return set(
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id__in=section_ids,
            status__in=["ENROLLED", "TRANSFERRED_IN"],
            student__is_active=True,
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )


def format_class_list_items(
    students,
    request=None,
    period_logs=None,
    daily_logs=None,
    period_id=None,
    period_record_ids=None,
) -> list[dict]:
    period_logs = period_logs or {}
    daily_logs = daily_logs or {}
    period_record_ids = period_record_ids or {}
    return [
        {
            "id": st.id,
            "lrn": st.lrn,
            "full_name": st.full_name,
            "name": st.full_name,
            "sex": st.sex,
            "gender": st.sex,
            "photo_url": build_media_url(st.photo, request),
            "attendance_status": (
                (period_logs.get((period_id, st.id)) if period_id else period_logs.get(st.id))
                or daily_logs.get(st.id)
                or "UNSCANNED"
            ),
            "attendance_record_id": (
                period_record_ids.get((period_id, st.id))
                if period_id
                else period_record_ids.get(st.id)
            ),
            "parent_contact": st.parent_contact,
            "qr_token": st.qr_token,
            "qr_payload": sign_student_qr(st.lrn, st.qr_token),
        }
        for st in students
    ]


def get_assigned_classes_with_class_lists(
    *,
    user,
    view_all: bool = False,
    include_students: bool = True,
    request=None,
) -> list[dict]:
    faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    leader = is_school_leader(user)
    today = timezone.localdate()

    periods_qs = ClassPeriod.objects.none()
    extra_sections_qs = Section.objects.none()

    if faculty_member and not view_all:
        own_periods = ClassPeriod.objects.filter(
            Q(**{PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD: faculty_member}) | Q(section__adviser=faculty_member),
            is_active=True,
        ).distinct()
        own_advisory = Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty_member, is_active=True)

        if own_periods.exists() or own_advisory.exists():
            periods_qs = own_periods
            extra_sections_qs = own_advisory
        elif leader:
            periods_qs = ClassPeriod.objects.filter(is_active=True)
            extra_sections_qs = Section.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    elif leader:
        periods_qs = ClassPeriod.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        extra_sections_qs = Section.objects.filter(is_active=True)

    periods = list(
        periods_qs.select_related(
            "section", "section__grade_level", "subject", PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD
        ).order_by("start_time")
    )
    covered_section_ids = {p.section_id for p in periods if p.section_id}
    extra_sections = list(
        extra_sections_qs.exclude(id__in=covered_section_ids)
        .select_related("grade_level")
        .order_by("grade_level__level_order", "name")
    )

    all_section_ids = covered_section_ids | {s.id for s in extra_sections}
    if not all_section_ids:
        return []

    if not include_students:
        counts_by_sec = dict(
            Enrollment.objects.filter(
                section_id__in=all_section_ids, status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True
            )
            .values("section_id")
            .annotate(cnt=Count("id"))
            .values_list("section_id", "cnt")
        )
        results = []
        for p in periods:
            if not p.section:
                continue
            start_s = str(p.start_time)[:5] if p.start_time else ""
            end_s = str(p.end_time)[:5] if p.end_time else ""
            cnt = counts_by_sec.get(p.section_id, 0)
            results.append({
                "id": p.id,
                "schedule_id": p.id,
                "section_id": p.section_id,
                "name": p.section.name,
                "section_name": p.section.name,
                "grade_level": p.section.grade_level.name if p.section.grade_level else "",
                "subject_code": p.subject.code if p.subject else "",
                "subject_title": p.subject.title if p.subject else "",
                "room": p.section.room_number or p.room_number,
                "start_time": start_s,
                "end_time": end_s,
                "time_display": f"{start_s} - {end_s}".strip(" -"),
                "total_students": cnt,
                "students": [],
                "is_adviser": bool(faculty_member and p.section.adviser_id == faculty_member.id),
            })
        return results

    enrollments = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id__in=all_section_ids, status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True
        )
        .select_related("student")
        .order_by("student__last_name", "student__first_name")
    )
    students_by_sec: dict[int, list] = {}
    all_student_ids = []
    for enr in enrollments:
        students_by_sec.setdefault(enr.section_id, []).append(enr.student)
        all_student_ids.append(enr.student_id)

    period_logs = {}
    period_record_ids = {}
    for row in ClassAttendance.objects.filter(
        attendance_date=today,
        student_id__in=all_student_ids,
    ).values("id", "class_period_id", "student_id", "status"):
        key = (row["class_period_id"], row["student_id"])
        period_logs[key] = row["status"]
        period_record_ids[key] = row["id"]

    daily_logs = dict(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(
            attendance_date=today,
            student_id__in=all_student_ids,
        ).values_list("student_id", "status")
    )

    results = []
    for p in periods:
        if not p.section:
            continue
        class_list = format_class_list_items(
            students_by_sec.get(p.section_id, []),
            request=request,
            period_logs=period_logs,
            daily_logs=daily_logs,
            period_id=p.id,
            period_record_ids=period_record_ids,
        )
        start_s = str(p.start_time)[:5] if p.start_time else ""
        end_s = str(p.end_time)[:5] if p.end_time else ""
        results.append({
            "id": p.id,
            "schedule_id": p.id,
            "section_id": p.section_id,
            "name": p.section.name,
            "section_name": p.section.name,
            "grade_level": p.section.grade_level.name if p.section.grade_level else "",
            "subject_code": p.subject.code if p.subject else "",
            "subject_name": p.subject.title if p.subject else "",
            "subject_title": p.subject.title if p.subject else "",
            "room": p.section.room_number or p.room_number,
            "start_time": start_s,
            "end_time": end_s,
            "time_display": f"{start_s} - {end_s}".strip(" -"),
            "total_students": len(class_list),
            "students": class_list,
            "is_adviser": bool(faculty_member and p.section.adviser_id == faculty_member.id),
        })

    for sec in extra_sections:
        class_list = format_class_list_items(
            students_by_sec.get(sec.id, []), request=request, daily_logs=daily_logs
        )
        results.append({
            "id": sec.id,
            "schedule_id": sec.id,
            "section_id": sec.id,
            "name": sec.name,
            "section_name": sec.name,
            "grade_level": sec.grade_level.name if sec.grade_level else "",
            "subject_code": "ADV",
            "subject_name": "Advisory Homeroom",
            "subject_title": "Advisory Homeroom",
            "room": sec.room_number or "",
            "start_time": "",
            "end_time": "",
            "time_display": "Homeroom",
            "total_students": len(class_list),
            "students": class_list,
            "is_adviser": bool(faculty_member and sec.adviser_id == faculty_member.id),
        })

    return results


def get_section_class_list(*, user, section_id: int | None = None, request=None) -> list[dict]:
    if not section_id:
        return get_assigned_classes_with_class_lists(user=user, request=request)

    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty_member or section_id not in get_faculty_section_ids(faculty_member):
            return []

    today = timezone.localdate()
    students = [
        enr.student
        for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id=section_id, status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True
        )
        .select_related("student")
        .order_by("student__last_name", "student__first_name")
    ]
    student_ids = [s.id for s in students]

    period_logs = dict(
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(student_id__in=student_ids, attendance_date=today).values_list(
            "student_id", "status"
        )
    )
    daily_logs = dict(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(student_id__in=student_ids, attendance_date=today).values_list(
            "student_id", "status"
        )
    )
    return format_class_list_items(
        students, request=request, period_logs=period_logs, daily_logs=daily_logs
    )


def get_adviser_matrix(*, user, section_id: int | None = None, request=None) -> dict:
    today = timezone.localdate()
    now_time = timezone.localtime().time()
    faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    leader = is_school_leader(user)

    advisory_ids = (
        set(Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty_member, is_active=True).values_list("id", flat=True))
        if faculty_member
        else set()
    )

    if leader:
        sections_qs = (
            Section.objects.filter(is_active=True)
            .select_related("grade_level")
            .order_by("grade_level__level_order", "name")
        )
    elif faculty_member:
        sections_qs = (
            Section.objects.filter(id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(faculty_member), is_active=True)
            .select_related("grade_level")
            .order_by("grade_level__level_order", "name")
        )
    else:
        sections_qs = Section.objects.none()

    available = [
        {
            "id": s.id,
            "name": s.name,
            "grade_level": s.grade_level.name if s.grade_level else "",
            "is_adviser": s.id in advisory_ids,
        }
        for s in sections_qs
    ]

    target = (
        sections_qs.filter(id=section_id).first()
        if section_id
        else (sections_qs.filter(id__in=advisory_ids).first() or sections_qs.first())
    )
    if not target:
        return {
            "has_advisory_class": False,
            "is_adviser": False,
            "available_sections": available,
            "schedules": [],
            "matrix": [],
        }

    periods = list(
        ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=target, is_active=True)
        .select_related("subject", PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD)
        .order_by("start_time")
    )
    students = [
        e.student
        for e in Enrollment.objects.filter(section=target, status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True)
        .select_related("student")
        .order_by("student__last_name", "student__first_name")
    ]
    student_ids = [s.id for s in students]

    log_map: dict[tuple[int, int], tuple[str, str]] = {}
    first_seen_map: dict[int, str] = {}
    started_period_ids: set[int] = set()

    on_campus_ids = set(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(
            attendance_date=today,
            student_id__in=student_ids,
            status__in=["PRESENT", "LATE"],
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )

    attendance_rows = (
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(attendance_date=today, student_id__in=student_ids)
        .values("student_id", "class_period_id", "status", "scanned_at", "class_period__subject__code")
        .order_by("scanned_at")
    )

    for row in attendance_rows:
        started_period_ids.add(row["class_period_id"])
        t_str = timezone.localtime(row["scanned_at"]).strftime("%I:%M %p") if row["scanned_at"] else ""
        st_code = row["status"]
        log_map[(row["student_id"], row["class_period_id"])] = (st_code, t_str)

        if st_code in ("PRESENT", "LATE"):
            on_campus_ids.add(row["student_id"])
            if row["student_id"] not in first_seen_map:
                subj_code = row["class_period__subject__code"] or "CLASS"
                first_seen_map[row["student_id"]] = f"{t_str} ({subj_code})" if t_str else subj_code

    matrix = []
    total_periods = len(periods)

    for idx, st in enumerate(students, start=1):
        attended = cutting = 0
        in_campus = st.id in on_campus_ids
        subj_cells = []

        for p in periods:
            rec = log_map.get((st.id, p.id))
            if rec:
                st_status, scan_t = rec
                if st_status in ("PRESENT", "LATE", "EXCUSED"):
                    attended += 1
                elif st_status in ("ABSENT", "CUTTING") and in_campus:
                    st_status = "CUTTING"
                    cutting += 1
            else:
                scan_t = ""
                if in_campus and (p.id in started_period_ids or (p.start_time and now_time >= p.start_time)):
                    st_status = "CUTTING"
                    cutting += 1
                else:
                    st_status = "UNSCANNED"

            subj_cells.append({
                "schedule_id": p.id,
                "subject_code": p.subject.code if p.subject else "SUB",
                "status": st_status,
                "time": scan_t,
            })

        campus_status = first_seen_map.get(st.id) or ("Present Today" if in_campus else "Not Seen Yet")

        matrix.append({
            "number": idx,
            "student_id": st.id,
            "student_name": st.full_name,
            "lrn": st.lrn,
            "photo_url": build_media_url(st.photo, request),
            "campus_status": campus_status,
            "presence_state": "ACTIVE" if in_campus else "NONE",
            "attended_count": attended,
            "cutting_count": cutting,
            "total_subjects": total_periods,
            "compliance_rate": round((attended / total_periods) * 100) if total_periods else 0,
            "subjects": subj_cells,
        })

    schedules_list = []
    for p in periods:
        period_faculty = getattr(p, PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD, None)
        schedules_list.append({
            "schedule_id": p.id,
            "subject_code": p.subject.code if p.subject else "",
            "subject_name": p.subject.title if p.subject else "",
            "faculty_name": period_faculty.full_name if period_faculty else "Unassigned",
            "time_window": f"{str(p.start_time)[:5]} - {str(p.end_time)[:5]}" if p.start_time else "",
        })

    return {
        "has_advisory_class": True,
        "is_adviser": target.id in advisory_ids,
        "section_id": target.id,
        "section_name": target.name,
        "grade_level": target.grade_level.name if target.grade_level else "",
        "total_students": len(students),
        "available_sections": available,
        "schedules": schedules_list,
        "matrix": matrix,
    }


def get_attendance_logs(
    *,
    user,
    target_date,
    section_id: int | None = None,
    period_id: int | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    qs = (
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(attendance_date=target_date)
        .select_related(
            "student",
            "class_period",
            "class_period__subject",
            "class_period__section",
            "class_period__section__grade_level",
        )
        .order_by("-scanned_at")
    )

    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty_member:
            return []
        qs = qs.filter(student_id__in=get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member))

    if section_id:
        qs = qs.filter(class_period__section_id=section_id)
    if period_id:
        qs = qs.filter(class_period_id=period_id)

    return [
        {
            "id": item.id,
            "student_id": item.student_id,
            "student_name": item.student.full_name,
            "lrn": item.student.lrn,
            "schedule_id": item.class_period_id,
            "subject_code": item.class_period.subject.code if (item.class_period and item.class_period.subject) else "",
            "section": format_section_label(
                item.class_period.section.grade_level.name
                if (item.class_period and item.class_period.section and item.class_period.section.grade_level)
                else "",
                item.class_period.section.name if (item.class_period and item.class_period.section) else "",
            ),
            "attendance_date": str(item.attendance_date),
            "status": item.status,
            "scan_method": item.scan_method,
            "scanned_at": timezone.localtime(item.scanned_at).strftime("%I:%M:%S %p"),
        }
        for item in qs[:300]
    ]


def get_exit_passes_for_user(*, user, status_filter: str = "ALL", search: str = ""):
    role = get_user_role(user)
    qs = ExitPass.objects.select_related("student", PASS_FACULTY_FIELD, "issued_by").order_by("-created_at")

    if status_filter and status_filter.upper() != "ALL":
        clean_st = status_filter.upper()
        if clean_st == "DECLINED":
            qs = qs.filter(status__in=["REVOKED", "DECLINED"])
        else:
            qs = qs.filter(status=clean_st)
    if search:
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(pass_number__icontains=search)
            | Q(student__first_name__icontains=search)
            | Q(student__last_name__icontains=search)
            | Q(**{f"{PASS_FACULTY_FIELD}__first_name__icontains": search})
            | Q(**{f"{PASS_FACULTY_FIELD}__last_name__icontains": search})
            | Q(reason__icontains=search)
        )
    if role == "PARENT":
        return qs.filter(student__parent_user=user)
    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty_member:
            return qs.none()
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(**{PASS_FACULTY_FIELD: faculty_member})
            | Q(issued_by=faculty_member)
            | Q(student_id__in=get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member))
        )
    return qs

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\attendance\serializers.py

import os
import uuid
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.accounts.models import FacultyProfile
from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_student_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
from apps.messaging.services import notify_student_parent
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.media import build_media_url, decode_uploaded_image
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from .models import ExitPass

ALLOWED_PROOF_EXTS = {".pdf", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"}
MAX_PROOF_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024


class ExitPassProofField(serializers.Field):
    """Accepts multipart file uploads (PDF/Image) or Base64 data URIs with strict size & extension validation."""

    def to_representation(self, value):
        return build_media_url(value, self.context.get("request"))

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        if data in (None, "", "null", "undefined"):
            return None

        if hasattr(data, "read"):
            ext = os.path.splitext(str(getattr(data, "name", "") or ""))[1].lower()
            if ext not in ALLOWED_PROOF_EXTS:
                raise serializers.ValidationError("Allowed proof formats: PDF, JPG, PNG, or WEBP.")
            size = getattr(data, "size", 0) or 0
            if size > MAX_PROOF_BYTES:
                raise serializers.ValidationError("Proof attachment cannot exceed 5 MB.")
            if ext == ".pdf":
                header = data.read(5)
                data.seek(0)
                if header != b"%PDF-":
                    raise serializers.ValidationError("Uploaded PDF file is invalid or corrupted.")
                return data
            return decode_uploaded_image(data, prefix="exit_proof")

        if isinstance(data, str) and data.strip().startswith("data:image"):
            decoded = decode_uploaded_image(data, prefix="exit_proof")
            if not isinstance(decoded, serializers.SkipField):
                return decoded

        raise serializers.SkipField()


class ExitPassSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    bearer_type = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source="student.full_name", read_only=True, default="")
    student_lrn = serializers.CharField(source="student.lrn", read_only=True, default="")
    teacher = serializers.IntegerField(source="faculty_id", read_only=True, allow_null=True)
    teacher_name = serializers.CharField(source="faculty.full_name", read_only=True, default="")
    faculty_name = serializers.CharField(source="faculty.full_name", read_only=True, default="")
    faculty_emp_id = serializers.CharField(source="faculty.employee_id", read_only=True, default="")
    adviser_name = serializers.CharField(source="issued_by.full_name", read_only=True, default="School Staff")
    valid_to = serializers.DateTimeField(source="valid_until", required=False)
    attachment = ExitPassProofField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    attachment_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = ExitPass
        fields = [
            "id",
            "pass_number",
            "bearer_type",
            "student",
            "student_name",
            "student_lrn",
            "teacher",
            "teacher_name",
            "faculty",
            "faculty_name",
            "faculty_emp_id",
            "issued_by",
            "adviser_name",
            "pass_type",
            "reason",
            "attachment",
            "attachment_url",
            "valid_from",
            "valid_until",
            "valid_to",
            "status",
            "created_at",
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["id", "pass_number", "issued_by", "status", "created_at"]
        extra_kwargs = {
            "valid_from": {"required": False},
            "valid_until": {"required": False},
        }

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        empty_vals = ("", "null", "none", "undefined")
        if "faculty" in mutable and str(mutable["faculty"]).strip().lower() in empty_vals:
            mutable["faculty"] = None
        if not mutable.get("faculty"):
            for alias in ("faculty_id", "teacher", "teacher_id"):
                if alias in mutable and str(mutable[alias]).strip().lower() not in empty_vals:
                    mutable["faculty"] = mutable[alias]
                    break
        if "student" in mutable and str(mutable["student"]).strip().lower() in empty_vals:
            mutable["student"] = None
        if not mutable.get("student") and "student_id" in mutable:
            if str(mutable["student_id"]).strip().lower() not in empty_vals:
                mutable["student"] = mutable["student_id"]
        if "attachment" not in mutable and "proof_file" in mutable:
            mutable["attachment"] = mutable["proof_file"]
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def get_bearer_type(self, obj) -> str:
        return "STUDENT" if obj.student_id else "FACULTY"

    def get_attachment_url(self, obj):
        return build_media_url(obj.attachment, self.context.get("request"))

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        data = super().to_representation(instance)
        if data.get("status") == "REVOKED":
            data["status"] = "DECLINED"
        return data

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if self.instance:
            valid_from = attrs.get("valid_from", self.instance.valid_from)
            valid_until = attrs.get("valid_until", self.instance.valid_until)
            if valid_from and valid_until and valid_until <= valid_from:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({"valid_until": "Return time must be after departure time."})
            return attrs
        now = timezone.now()
        attrs.setdefault("valid_from", now)
        attrs.setdefault("valid_until", attrs["valid_from"] + timedelta(hours=4))
        if attrs["valid_until"] <= attrs["valid_from"]:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"valid_until": "Return time must be after departure time."})
        return attrs

    def create(self, validated_data):
        request = self.context["request"]
        user = request.user
        role = get_user_role(user)
        leader = is_school_leader(user)

        if role == "PARENT":
            raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Parent accounts cannot issue exit passes.", 403)

        own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        issuer = own_faculty or (FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() if leader else None)
        if not issuer:
            raise AppError(
                "NO_FACULTY_PROFILE",
                "A linked faculty profile is required to issue an exit pass.",
                403,
            )

        target_student = validated_data.get("student")
        target_faculty = validated_data.get("faculty")

        if target_student:
            if not leader and target_student.id not in get_faculty_student_ids(own_faculty):
                raise AppError(
                    "FORBIDDEN",
                    "You can only issue exit passes for students in your assigned classes.",
                    403,
                )
            validated_data["faculty"] = None
        else:
            validated_data["student"] = None
            if not leader:
                validated_data["faculty"] = own_faculty
            elif not target_faculty:
                validated_data["faculty"] = issuer

        is_student_pass = bool(validated_data.get("student"))
        initial_status = "ACTIVE" if (leader or is_student_pass) else "PENDING"
        pass_code = f"EP-{timezone.localdate().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"

        pass_obj = ExitPass.objects.create(
            **validated_data,
            pass_number=pass_code,
            issued_by=issuer,
            status=initial_status,
        )
        if pass_obj.student:
            notify_student_parent(
                student=pass_obj.student,
                title=f"Exit Pass Issued: {pass_obj.student.first_name}",
                message=f"Exit pass #{pass_code} issued for {pass_obj.student.full_name} ({pass_obj.reason}).",
                category="GATE_PASS",
            )
        return pass_obj

    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        if validated_data.get("student") is not None:
            validated_data["faculty"] = None
        elif validated_data.get("faculty") is not None:
            validated_data["student"] = None
        return super().update(instance, validated_data)

from datetime import datetime, time as dt_time, timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.dateparse import parse_datetime

from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.gps import verify_campus_gps
from common.media import build_media_url
from common.permissions import is_school_leader
from common.qr_signer import verify_student_qr
from apps.campus.selectors import get_active_campus_coords
from apps.messaging.services import notify_student_parent
from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod, Subject
from apps.school.selectors import format_section_label
from apps.school.services import sync_period_days
from apps.students.models import Enrollment, Student
from .models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance, ExitPass
from .selectors import (
    FacultyModel,
    PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
)


def determine_arrival_status(period: ClassPeriod | None, scanned_at, grace_minutes: int = 15) -> str:
    if not period or not period.start_time:
        return "PRESENT"
    local_dt = timezone.localtime(scanned_at)
    cutoff = timezone.make_aware(
        datetime.combine(local_dt.date(), period.start_time),
        timezone.get_current_timezone(),
    ) + timedelta(minutes=grace_minutes)
    return "LATE" if local_dt > cutoff else "PRESENT"


@transaction.atomic
def record_student_qr_scan(
    *,
    user,
    raw_qr: str,
    period_id: int | None = None,
    latitude=None,
    longitude=None,
    accuracy=None,
    is_mock_location: bool = False,
    request=None,
) -> dict:
    faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    leader = is_school_leader(user)
    if not faculty_member and not leader:
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Only faculty and school admins can scan student QR cards.", 403)

    if latitude is not None and longitude is not None and not leader:
        c_lat, c_lon, radius, _ = get_active_campus_coords()
        gps = verify_campus_gps(
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            campus_lat=c_lat,
            campus_lon=c_lon,
            radius_meters=radius,
            accuracy_meters=accuracy,
            is_mock_location=is_mock_location,
        )
        if not gps["is_inside"]:
            raise AppError("OUTSIDE_CAMPUS", gps["reason"], 403)

    clean_raw = str(raw_qr or "").strip()
    if clean_raw.upper().startswith("ATTENDSURE:FACULTY:") or "ATTENDSURE_FACULTY_PASS" in clean_raw.upper():
        raise AppError(
            "FACULTY_QR_SCANNED",
            "That is a Faculty ID Pass. Please scan a Student QR card from the Students or My Classes tab.",
            400,
        )

    qr_data = verify_student_qr(clean_raw)
    if not qr_data:
        raise AppError(
            "INVALID_QR",
            "This QR card is old or invalid. Open the student's QR card in the Students tab and save the new QR image.",
            400,
        )

    student = Student.objects.filter(
        Q(qr_token=qr_data.qr_token) & Q(lrn__iexact=qr_data.lrn)
        if qr_data.is_signed
        else (Q(qr_token=qr_data.qr_token) | Q(lrn__iexact=qr_data.lrn)),
        is_active=True,
    ).first()
    if not student:
        raise AppError("STUDENT_NOT_FOUND", "This student QR card is not registered.", 404)

    enrollment = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, status="ENROLLED")
        .select_related("section", "section__grade_level")
        .order_by("-id")
        .first()
    )
    if not enrollment or not enrollment.section:
        raise AppError(
            "NOT_ENROLLED",
            f"{student.full_name} has no class section yet. Assign a section in the Students tab first.",
            400,
        )

    section = enrollment.section
    section_label = format_section_label(
        section.grade_level.name if section.grade_level else "", section.name
    )

    period_qs = ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=section, is_active=True).select_related("subject")
    active_period = None
    if period_id:
        active_period = period_qs.filter(id=period_id).first()
    if not active_period and faculty_member:
        active_period = period_qs.filter(**{PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD: faculty_member}).first()

    is_adviser = bool(faculty_member and section.adviser_id == faculty_member.id)
    active_faculty_id = getattr(active_period, f"{PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD}_id", None) if active_period else None
    if not (
        leader
        or is_adviser
        or (active_period and faculty_member and active_faculty_id == faculty_member.id)
    ):
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN_SECTION", f"You are not assigned to {section_label}.", 403)

    target_period = active_period or period_qs.order_by("start_time").first()
    if not target_period:
        homeroom_subj, _ = Subject.objects.get_or_create(
            code="HOMEROOM",
            defaults={
                "title": "Advisory Homeroom",
                "grade_level": section.grade_level,
                "units": 1,
            },
        )
        target_period = ClassPeriod.objects.create(
            schedule_code=f"HR-{section.id}",
            section=section,
            subject=homeroom_subj,
            room=section.room,
            start_time=dt_time(7, 30),
            end_time=dt_time(8, 30),
            is_active=True,
            **{PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD: section.adviser or faculty_member},
        )
        sync_period_days(target_period, "MON-FRI")

    now = timezone.now()
    today = timezone.localdate()

    missed_count = (
        ClassPeriod.objects.filter(
            section=section, is_active=True, start_time__lt=target_period.start_time
        )
        .exclude(
            attendance_records__student=student,
            attendance_records__attendance_date=today,
            attendance_records__status__in=["PRESENT", "LATE"],
        )
        .count()
    )
    note = (
        f"CUTTING ALERT: Missed {missed_count} earlier class(es) today."
        if missed_count > 0
        else "Classroom QR attendance saved."
    )

    arrival_status = determine_arrival_status(target_period, now)
    period_faculty = getattr(target_period, PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD, None)
    recorder = period_faculty or faculty_member or FacultyModel.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("id").first()
    if not recorder:
        raise AppError("NO_FACULTY_PROFILE", "Add at least one active faculty member first.", 400)

    record, created = ClassAttendance.objects.get_or_create(
        student=student,
        class_period=target_period,
        attendance_date=today,
        defaults={
            "recorded_by": recorder,
            "scanned_at": now,
            "scan_method": "QR",
            "status": arrival_status,
        },
    )

    if created:
        DailyAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
            student=student,
            attendance_date=today,
            defaults={"status": arrival_status, "remarks": note[:255]},
        )
        subject_name = target_period.subject.title if target_period.subject else "Class"
        time_display = timezone.localtime(now).strftime("%I:%M %p")
        notify_student_parent(
            student=student,
            title=f"Cutting Alert: {student.first_name}" if missed_count > 0 else f"Attendance: {student.first_name} ({arrival_status})",
            message=(
                f"{student.full_name} scanned into {subject_name} at {time_display}, missing {missed_count} earlier class(es)."
                if missed_count > 0
                else f"{student.full_name} was marked {arrival_status} in {subject_name} at {time_display}."
            ),
            category="CUTTING_ALERT" if missed_count > 0 else "ATTENDANCE",
        )

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "success": True,
        "is_duplicate": not created,
        "student_id": student.id,
        "student_name": student.full_name,
        "lrn": student.lrn,
        "section": section_label,
        "status": record.status,
        "scanned_at": timezone.localtime(record.scanned_at).strftime("%I:%M:%S %p"),
        "attendance_note": note,
        "photo_url": build_media_url(student.photo, request),
    }


@transaction.atomic
def sync_offline_qr_scans(*, user, raw_scans: list[dict]) -> dict:
    faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    if not faculty_member and not is_school_leader(user):
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Only faculty and school admins can sync offline scans.", 403)

    parsed = []
    tokens = set()
    for item in raw_scans[:500]:
        qr_data = verify_student_qr(item.get("qr_token") or item.get("code") or "")
        if not qr_data:
            continue
        tokens.add(qr_data.qr_token)
        tokens.add(qr_data.lrn)
        parsed.append({
            "qr": qr_data,
            "period_id": item.get("schedule_id") or item.get("period_id"),
            "scanned_at": parse_datetime(str(item.get("scanned_at", ""))) or timezone.now(),
        })

    students = Student.objects.filter(Q(qr_token__in=tokens) | Q(lrn__in=tokens), is_active=True)
    student_lookup = {}
    for st in students:
        student_lookup[st.qr_token] = st
        student_lookup[st.lrn] = st

    enrollments = {
        e.student_id: e.section
        for e in Enrollment.objects.filter(student__in=students, status="ENROLLED").select_related("section")
    }

    synced = duplicates = 0
    rejected = len(raw_scans) - len(parsed)

    for entry in parsed:
        st = student_lookup.get(entry["qr"].qr_token)
        if not st and not entry["qr"].is_signed:
            st = student_lookup.get(entry["qr"].lrn)
        if st and entry["qr"].is_signed and st.lrn.lower() != entry["qr"].lrn.lower():
            rejected += 1
            continue

        sec = enrollments.get(st.id) if st else None
        if not st or not sec:
            rejected += 1
            continue

        pid = entry["period_id"]
        period = (
            ClassPeriod.objects.filter(id=int(pid), section=sec, is_active=True).first()
            if (pid and str(pid).isdigit())
            else ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=sec, is_active=True).order_by("start_time").first()
        )
        if not period:
            rejected += 1
            continue

        period_faculty = getattr(period, PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD, None)
        recorder = period_faculty or faculty_member
        if not recorder:
            rejected += 1
            continue

        scan_dt = entry["scanned_at"]
        if timezone.is_naive(scan_dt):
            scan_dt = timezone.make_aware(scan_dt, timezone.get_current_timezone())
        scan_date = timezone.localtime(scan_dt).date()
        status_val = determine_arrival_status(period, scan_dt)

        _, created = ClassAttendance.objects.get_or_create(
            student=st,
            class_period=period,
            attendance_date=scan_date,
            defaults={
                "recorded_by": recorder,
                "scanned_at": scan_dt,
                "scan_method": "QR",
                "status": status_val,
            },
        )
        if not created:
            duplicates += 1
            continue

        DailyAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
            student=st,
            attendance_date=scan_date,
            defaults={"status": status_val, "remarks": "Synced from offline QR queue."},
        )
        synced += 1

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "success": True,
        "synced_count": synced,
        "duplicate_count": duplicates,
        "rejected_count": rejected,
    }


@transaction.atomic
def save_manual_attendance(
    *, user, student_id: int, status_code: str, period_id: int | None = None, date_str: str | None = None
) -> ClassAttendance:
    clean_status = status_code.upper().strip()
    if clean_status not in {"PRESENT", "LATE", "CUTTING", "ABSENT", "EXCUSED"}:
        raise AppError("INVALID_STATUS", "Invalid attendance status.", 400)

    student = Student.objects.filter(id=student_id, is_active=True).first()
    if not student:
        raise AppError("STUDENT_NOT_FOUND", "Student not found.", 404)

    faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    if not is_school_leader(user) and (
        not faculty_member or student.id not in get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member)
    ):
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Access denied for this student.", 403)

    enr = Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, status="ENROLLED").select_related("section").first()
    sec = enr.section if enr else None
    period = (
        ClassPeriod.objects.filter(id=period_id).first()
        if period_id
        else (ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=sec, is_active=True).order_by("start_time").first() if sec else None)
    )
    if not period:
        raise AppError("NO_SCHEDULE", "No active class period found.", 400)

    target_date = timezone.localdate()
    if date_str:
        try:
            target_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except ValueError:
            pass

    period_faculty = getattr(period, PERIOD_FACULTY_FIELD, None)
    recorder = faculty_member or period_faculty or FacultyModel.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("id").first()
    if not recorder:
        raise AppError("NO_FACULTY_PROFILE", "Add at least one active faculty member first.", 400)

    log, _ = ClassAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
        student=student,
        class_period=period,
        attendance_date=target_date,
        defaults={
            "recorded_by": recorder,
            "status": clean_status,
            "scan_method": "MANUAL",
            "scanned_at": timezone.now(),
        },
    )
    DailyAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
        student=student,
        attendance_date=target_date,
        defaults={"status": clean_status, "remarks": "Manual faculty update."},
    )
    return log


@transaction.atomic
def change_exit_pass_status(*, user, exit_pass: ExitPass, next_status: str) -> ExitPass:
    if next_status in ("ACTIVE", "REVOKED") and not is_school_leader(user):
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Only school admins can approve or decline passes.", 403)
    if next_status == "USED":
        if exit_pass.status != "ACTIVE":
            raise AppError("INVALID_PASS_STATE", f"Pass is currently {exit_pass.status}.", 400)
        if timezone.now() > exit_pass.valid_until:
            exit_pass.status = "EXPIRED"
            exit_pass.save(update_fields=["status", "updated_at"])
            raise AppError("PASS_EXPIRED", "Exit pass has expired.", 400)

    exit_pass.status = next_status
    exit_pass.save(update_fields=["status", "updated_at"])

    if next_status == "USED" and exit_pass.student:
        notify_student_parent(
            student=exit_pass.student,
            title=f"Authorized Campus Exit: {exit_pass.student.first_name}",
            message=f"{exit_pass.student.full_name} exited campus using approved pass #{exit_pass.pass_number}.",
            category="GATE_PASS",
        )
    return exit_pass

from datetime import datetime
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from common.audit import AuditedModelViewSet, record_audit_log
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from .models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance
from .selectors import (
    PASS_FACULTY_FIELD,
    get_adviser_matrix,
    get_assigned_classes_with_class_lists,
    get_attendance_logs,
    get_exit_passes_for_user,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    get_section_class_list,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
)
from .serializers import ExitPassSerializer
from .services import (
    change_exit_pass_status,
    record_student_qr_scan,
    save_manual_attendance,
    sync_offline_qr_scans,
)


class ScanStudentQrView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        raw_qr = (
            request.data.get("qr_text")
            or request.data.get("code")
            or request.data.get("raw_identifier")
            or request.data.get("qr_token")
            or ""
        )
        period_id = request.data.get("schedule_id") or request.data.get("period_id")
        result = record_student_qr_scan(
            user=request.user,
            raw_qr=str(raw_qr),
            period_id=int(period_id) if (period_id and str(period_id).isdigit()) else None,
            latitude=request.data.get("latitude"),
            longitude=request.data.get("longitude"),
            accuracy=request.data.get("accuracy"),
            is_mock_location=bool(request.data.get("is_mock_location", False)),
            request=request,
        )
        code = status.HTTP_200_OK if result["is_duplicate"] else status.HTTP_201_CREATED
        return Response(result, status=code)


class OfflineQrSyncView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        scans = request.data.get("scans") or []
        if not isinstance(scans, list) or not scans:
            return Response({"error": "Non-empty scans list is required."}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        return Response(sync_offline_qr_scans(user=request.user, raw_scans=scans))


class ClassAttendanceView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        date_str = request.query_params.get("date")
        target_date = timezone.localdate()
        if date_str:
            try:
                target_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
            except ValueError:
                pass
        sec_id = request.query_params.get("section_id")
        per_id = request.query_params.get("schedule_id") or request.query_params.get("period_id")
        records = get_attendance_logs(
            user=request.user,
            target_date=target_date,
            section_id=int(sec_id) if (sec_id and str(sec_id).isdigit()) else None,
            period_id=int(per_id) if (per_id and str(per_id).isdigit()) else None,
        )
        return Response({"records": records, "total": len(records)})

    def post(self, request):
        student_id = request.data.get("student_id")
        if not student_id or not str(student_id).isdigit():
            return Response({"error": "Valid student_id is required."}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        per_id = request.data.get("schedule_id") or request.data.get("period_id")
        log = save_manual_attendance(
            user=request.user,
            student_id=int(student_id),
            status_code=str(request.data.get("status", "PRESENT")),
            period_id=int(per_id) if (per_id and str(per_id).isdigit()) else None,
            date_str=request.data.get("attendance_date"),
        )
        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name=log._meta.db_table,
            record_id=log.id,
            action="CREATE",
            new_data={"student_id": log.student_id, "status": log.status},
        )
        return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "id": log.id, "status": log.status}, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    def put(self, request):
        rec_id = request.data.get("id") or request.query_params.get("id")
        if rec_id is not None and str(rec_id).strip() != "":
            if not str(rec_id).isdigit():
                return Response({"error": "Valid attendance record id is required."}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
            existing = ClassAttendance.objects.filter(id=int(rec_id)).first()
            if not existing:
                return Response({"error": "Attendance record not found."}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)
            log = save_manual_attendance(
                user=request.user,
                student_id=existing.student_id,
                status_code=str(request.data.get("status", existing.status)),
                period_id=existing.class_period_id,
                date_str=str(existing.attendance_date),
            )
            record_audit_log(
                request=request,
                table_name=log._meta.db_table,
                record_id=log.id,
                action="UPDATE",
                new_data={"student_id": log.student_id, "status": log.status},
            )
            return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "id": log.id, "status": log.status})
        return self.post(request)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    def delete(self, request):
        if get_user_role(request.user) == "PARENT":
            return Response({"error": "Parent accounts cannot remove attendance records."}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        rec_id = request.data.get("id") or request.query_params.get("id")
        if not rec_id or not str(rec_id).isdigit():
            return Response({"error": "Valid attendance record id is required."}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        existing = ClassAttendance.objects.filter(id=int(rec_id)).first()
        if not existing:
            return Response({"error": "Attendance record not found."}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        if not is_school_leader(request.user):
            faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
            if not faculty_member or existing.student_id not in get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member):
                return Response({"error": "You can only remove attendance for your assigned students."}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        student_id = existing.student_id
        att_date = existing.attendance_date
        rec_pk = existing.id
        existing.delete()

        remaining = ClassAttendance.objects.filter(student_id=student_id, attendance_date=att_date).order_by("-scanned_at").first()
        if remaining:
            DailyAttendance.objects.filter(student_id=student_id, attendance_date=att_date).update(status=remaining.status)
        else:
            DailyAttendance.objects.filter(student_id=student_id, attendance_date=att_date).delete()

        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name=ClassAttendance._meta.db_table,
            record_id=rec_pk,
            action="DELETE",
            new_data={"student_id": student_id, "attendance_date": str(att_date)},
        )
        return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "message": "Attendance record removed."})


class AssignedClassListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        view_all = str(request.query_params.get("mode", "")).lower() == "all"
        summary_only = str(request.query_params.get("summary", "")).lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
        return Response(
            get_assigned_classes_with_class_lists(
                user=request.user,
                view_all=view_all,
                include_students=not summary_only,
                request=request,
            )
        )

        parsed_id = int(sec_id) if (sec_id and str(sec_id).isdigit()) else None

            get_adviser_matrix(

class ExitPassViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = ExitPassSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        return get_exit_passes_for_user(
            user=self.request.user,
            status_filter=self.request.query_params.get("status", "ALL"),
            search=self.request.query_params.get("search", "").strip(),
        )

    def _assert_can_modify_pass(self, instance):
        user = self.request.user
        if get_user_role(user) == "PARENT":
            raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Parent accounts cannot modify or delete exit passes.", 403)
        if is_school_leader(user):
            return
        own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        pass_faculty_id = getattr(instance, f"{PASS_FACULTY_FIELD}_id", None)
        if not own_faculty or (instance.issued_by_id != own_faculty.id and pass_faculty_id != own_faculty.id):
            raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "You can only edit or remove exit passes you issued.", 403)
        if instance.status == "USED":
            raise AppError("PASS_LOCKED", "Used exit passes cannot be modified by non-administrators.", 400)

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        self._assert_can_modify_pass(serializer.instance)
        super().perform_update(serializer)

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        self._assert_can_modify_pass(instance)
        super().perform_destroy(instance)

    @action(detail=True, methods=["post"], url_path="approve")
    def approve(self, request, pk=None):
        updated = change_exit_pass_status(user=request.user, exit_pass=self.get_object(), next_status="ACTIVE")
        return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "status": updated.status})

    @action(detail=True, methods=["post"], url_path="decline")
    def decline(self, request, pk=None):
        updated = change_exit_pass_status(user=request.user, exit_pass=self.get_object(), next_status="REVOKED")
        return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "status": updated.status})

    @action(detail=True, methods=["post"], url_path="mark-used")
    def mark_used(self, request, pk=None):
        updated = change_exit_pass_status(user=request.user, exit_pass=self.get_object(), next_status="USED")
        return Response({"ok": True, "success": True, "status": updated.status})

from django.apps import AppConfig


class CampusConfig(AppConfig):
    default_auto_field = "django.db.models.BigAutoField"
    name = "apps.campus"
    label = "campus"
    verbose_name = "Campus GPS & Time Records"

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import UniqueConstraint
from django.utils import timezone


class CampusBoundary(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=120, default="Main Campus")
    center_lat = models.FloatField(default=8.4856)
    center_lng = models.FloatField(default=124.6565)
    radius_meters = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=150)
    grace_minutes = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=10)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "geofence_settings"

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.name} ({self.radius_meters}m)"


class FacultyAttendanceLog(models.Model):
    ACTION_CHOICES = [
        ("TIME_IN_AM", "Morning Time In"),
        ("TIME_OUT_AM", "Morning Time Out"),
        ("TIME_IN_PM", "Afternoon Time In"),
        ("TIME_OUT_PM", "Afternoon Time Out"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="campus_logs",
    )
    log_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    action = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ACTION_CHOICES, db_index=True)
    recorded_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now, db_index=True)
    latitude = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    longitude = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    distance_meters = models.FloatField(default=0.0)
    is_within_boundary = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    face_verified = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    device_id = models.CharField(max_length=128, blank=True, default="")
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")

    class Meta:
        db_table = "faculty_attendance_logs"
        ordering = ["-recorded_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["faculty", "log_date"], name="idx_faclog_fac_date"),
            models.Index(fields=["log_date", "action"], name="idx_faclog_date_act"),
        ]


class BoundaryAlert(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("OPEN", "Open Alert"),
        ("RESOLVED", "Returned Inside Campus"),
        ("EXCUSED", "Official Business / Excused"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="boundary_alerts",
    )
    incident_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    left_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    returned_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    outside_minutes = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    last_lat = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    last_lng = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    distance_meters = models.FloatField(default=0.0)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="OPEN", db_index=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")

    class Meta:
        db_table = "geofence_loafing_alerts"
        ordering = ["-left_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["faculty", "incident_date"], name="idx_bndry_fac_date"),
            models.Index(fields=["incident_date", "status"], name="idx_bndry_date_stat"),
        ]


class MonthlyTimeRecord(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="monthly_dtrs",
    )
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(db_index=True)
    year = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(db_index=True)
    regular_hours = models.CharField(max_length=100, default="8:00 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM")
    saturday_hours = models.CharField(max_length=100, default="As Required")
    is_submitted = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    submitted_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    is_dept_head_approved = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="approved_monthly_dtrs",
    )
    dept_head_approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "monthly_time_records"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["faculty", "month", "year"], name="uq_dtr_faculty_month_year")
        ]
