from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import AuditLog, FacultyProfile
from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_section_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
from apps.attendance.models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance, ExitPass
from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod, SchoolSettings
from apps.school.selectors import format_section_label
from apps.students.models import Enrollment
from common.media import build_media_url
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from .models import BoundaryAlert, CampusBoundary, FacultyAttendanceLog

ONLINE_WINDOW_MINUTES = 5


def get_active_campus_coords() -> tuple[float, float, int, str]:
    boundary = (
        CampusBoundary.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        .only("center_lat", "center_lng", "radius_meters", "name")
        .first()
    )
    if not boundary:
        school = (
            SchoolSettings.objects.select_related("campus_boundary")
            .only("school_name", "campus_boundary")
            .first()
        )
        if school and school.campus_boundary:
            boundary = school.campus_boundary

    lat = float(boundary.center_lat) if (boundary and boundary.center_lat is not None) else 8.4858
    lon = float(boundary.center_lng) if (boundary and boundary.center_lng is not None) else 124.6567
    radius = int(boundary.radius_meters) if (boundary and boundary.radius_meters) else 150
    name = boundary.name if boundary else "Main Campus"
    return lat, lon, radius, name


def get_campus_boundary_overview(*, user) -> dict:
    now_dt = timezone.now()
    today = timezone.localdate()
    online_cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(minutes=ONLINE_WINDOW_MINUTES)

    lat, lon, radius, name = get_active_campus_coords()
    leader = is_school_leader(user)

    if leader:
        faculty_members = list(
            FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True)
            .select_related("department_ref", "position_ref")
            .order_by("last_name", "first_name")
        )
        logs_qs = (
            FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.filter(log_date=today)
            .select_related("faculty")
            .order_by("-recorded_at")[:300]
        )
        alerts_qs = (
            BoundaryAlert.objects.filter(incident_date=today)
            .select_related("faculty")
            .order_by("-left_at")[:100]
        )
    else:
        own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        faculty_members = [own_faculty] if own_faculty else []
        logs_qs = (
            FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.filter(faculty=own_faculty, log_date=today)
            .select_related("faculty")
            .order_by("-recorded_at")[:60]
            if own_faculty
            else FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.none()
        )
        alerts_qs = (
            BoundaryAlert.objects.filter(faculty=own_faculty, incident_date=today)
            .select_related("faculty")
            .order_by("-left_at")
            if own_faculty
            else BoundaryAlert.objects.none()
        )

    raw_logs = list(logs_qs)
    latest_by_faculty: dict[int, FacultyAttendanceLog] = {}
    logs: list[dict] = []

    for item in raw_logs:
        latest_by_faculty.setdefault(item.faculty_id, item)
        local_rec = timezone.localtime(item.recorded_at)
        fac_name = item.faculty.full_name if item.faculty else "Unknown"
        emp_id = item.faculty.employee_id if item.faculty else ""
        logs.append(
            {
                "id": item.id,
                "faculty_id": item.faculty_id,
                "employee_id": emp_id,
                "faculty_name": fac_name,
                "action": item.action,
                "latitude": float(item.latitude) if item.latitude is not None else lat,
                "longitude": float(item.longitude) if item.longitude is not None else lon,
                "distance_meters": float(item.distance_meters or 0.0),
                "is_inside_geofence": bool(item.is_within_boundary),
                "verified_inside": bool(item.is_within_boundary),
                "face_verified": bool(item.face_verified),
                "recorded_at": local_rec.strftime("%I:%M:%S %p"),
                "timestamp": local_rec.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p"),
            }
        )

    faculty_list: list[dict] = []
    online_count = 0
    verified_inside_count = 0
    outside_count = 0

    for fac in faculty_members:
        latest = latest_by_faculty.get(fac.id)
        is_online = bool(latest and latest.recorded_at >= online_cutoff)
        inside = bool(latest and latest.is_within_boundary)

        if is_online:
            online_count += 1
        if latest:
            if inside:
                verified_inside_count += 1
            else:
                outside_count += 1

        local_seen = timezone.localtime(latest.recorded_at) if latest else None
        bound_id = fac.bound_device_id

        faculty_list.append(
            {
                "id": fac.id,
                "faculty_id": fac.id,
                "employee_id": fac.employee_id,
                "full_name": fac.full_name,
                "faculty_name": fac.full_name,
                "department": fac.department,
                "position": fac.position,
                "is_online": is_online,
                "status": "ONLINE" if is_online else "OFFLINE",
                "is_inside_geofence": inside,
                "verified_inside": inside,
                "latitude": float(latest.latitude) if (latest and latest.latitude is not None) else None,
                "longitude": float(latest.longitude) if (latest and latest.longitude is not None) else None,
                "last_seen": local_seen.strftime("%I:%M:%S %p") if local_seen else "No check-in today",
                "recorded_at": local_seen.strftime("%I:%M:%S %p") if local_seen else None,
                "bound_device_id": bound_id,
                "is_device_bound": bool(bound_id),
                "device_model": fac.device_model or "",
            }
        )

    offline_count = max(len(faculty_members) - online_count, 0)

    boundary_alerts = [
        {
            "id": a.id,
            "faculty_id": a.faculty_id,
            "employee_id": a.faculty.employee_id if a.faculty else "",
            "faculty_name": a.faculty.full_name if a.faculty else "Unknown",
            "trigger_reason": a.remarks or f"Outside campus boundary ({round(a.distance_meters)}m)",
            "reason": a.remarks or f"Outside campus boundary ({round(a.distance_meters)}m)",
            "status": a.status,
            "created_at": timezone.localtime(a.left_at).strftime("%I:%M %p"),
            "timestamp": timezone.localtime(a.left_at).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p"),
        }
        for a in alerts_qs
    ]

    return {
        "name": name,
        "latitude": lat,
        "longitude": lon,
        "radius_meters": radius,
        "geofence_radius_meters": radius,
        "is_active": True,
        "inside_count": verified_inside_count,
        "verified_inside": verified_inside_count,
        "outside_count": outside_count,
        "online_users_count": online_count,
        "offline_users_count": offline_count,
        "incidents_today": len(boundary_alerts),
        "faculty_list": faculty_list,
        "heartbeats": logs,
        "logs": logs,
        "boundary_alerts": boundary_alerts,
        "recent_breaches": boundary_alerts,
    }


def get_campus_audit_history(*, user) -> dict:
    overview = get_campus_boundary_overview(user=user)
    tracked_tables = {
        CampusBoundary._meta.db_table,
        SchoolSettings._meta.db_table,
        FacultyAttendanceLog._meta.db_table,
        BoundaryAlert._meta.db_table,
    }

    audit_qs = (
        AuditLog.objects.select_related("performed_by")
        .filter(table_name__in=tracked_tables)
        .order_by("-created_at")
    )
    if not is_school_leader(user):
        audit_qs = audit_qs.filter(performed_by=user)

    audit_logs = [
        {
            "id": f"audit-{item.id}",
            "record_id": item.id,
            "category": "AUDIT",
            "user": (item.performed_by.get_full_name() or item.performed_by.username)
            if item.performed_by
            else "System",
            "faculty_name": (item.performed_by.get_full_name() or item.performed_by.username)
            if item.performed_by
            else "System",
            "action": item.action,
            "status": "LOGGED",
            "details": f"{item.action} on {item.table_name}",
            "table_name": item.table_name,
            "ip_address": item.client_ip or "",
            "timestamp": timezone.localtime(item.created_at).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p"),
        }
        for item in audit_qs[:60]
    ]

    records: list[dict] = []
    for alert in overview["boundary_alerts"]:
        records.append(
            {
                "id": f"alert-{alert['id']}",
                "category": "BREACH",
                "user": alert["faculty_name"],
                "faculty_name": alert["faculty_name"],
                "employee_id": alert.get("employee_id", ""),
                "action": "BOUNDARY_ALERT",
                "status": alert["status"],
                "details": alert["trigger_reason"],
                "is_inside_geofence": False,
                "ip_address": "",
                "timestamp": alert["timestamp"],
            }
        )

    for hb in overview["heartbeats"][:100]:
        records.append(
            {
                "id": f"hb-{hb['id']}",
                "category": "HEARTBEAT",
                "user": hb["faculty_name"],
                "faculty_name": hb["faculty_name"],
                "employee_id": hb.get("employee_id", ""),
                "action": "INSIDE_CAMPUS" if hb["is_inside_geofence"] else "OUTSIDE_CAMPUS",
                "status": "INSIDE" if hb["is_inside_geofence"] else "OUTSIDE",
                "details": f"GPS ({hb['latitude']:.5f}, {hb['longitude']:.5f})",
                "latitude": hb["latitude"],
                "longitude": hb["longitude"],
                "is_inside_geofence": hb["is_inside_geofence"],
                "ip_address": "",
                "timestamp": hb["timestamp"],
            }
        )

    records.extend(audit_logs)
    return {
        "ok": True,
        "count": len(records),
        "records": records,
        "results": records,
        "heartbeats": overview["heartbeats"],
        "loafing_incidents": overview["boundary_alerts"],
        "audit_logs": audit_logs,
    }


def get_live_campus_anomalies(*, user) -> list[dict]:
    leader = is_school_leader(user)
    if not (leader or get_user_role(user) == "FACULTY"):
        return []

    today = timezone.localdate()
    now_dt = timezone.now()
    now_time = timezone.localtime(now_dt).time()

    student_passes = set(
        ExitPass.objects.filter(
            student__isnull=False, status="ACTIVE", valid_from__lte=now_dt, valid_until__gte=now_dt
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )
    faculty_passes = set(
        ExitPass.objects.filter(
            faculty__isnull=False, status="ACTIVE", valid_from__lte=now_dt, valid_until__gte=now_dt
        ).values_list("faculty_id", flat=True)
    )
    present_today = set(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(
            attendance_date=today, status__in=["PRESENT", "LATE"]
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )

    periods_qs = ClassPeriod.objects.select_related("section", "subject", "faculty").filter(
        is_active=True, start_time__lte=now_time
    )
    if not leader:
        own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not own_faculty:
            return []
        periods_qs = periods_qs.filter(section_id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(own_faculty))

    periods = list(periods_qs)
    period_ids = [p.id for p in periods]
    section_ids = {p.section_id for p in periods if p.section_id}

    verified_pairs = set(
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(
            attendance_date=today,
            class_period_id__in=period_ids,
            status__in=["PRESENT", "LATE", "EXCUSED"],
        ).values_list("student_id", "class_period_id")
    )
    started_periods = {pid for _, pid in verified_pairs}

    class_lists_by_sec: dict[int, list] = {}
    for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
        section_id__in=section_ids, status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True
    ).select_related("student"):
        class_lists_by_sec.setdefault(enr.section_id, []).append(enr.student)

    anomalies = []
    time_str = timezone.localtime(now_dt).strftime("%I:%M %p")

    for period in periods:
        if not period.section or period.id not in started_periods:
            continue
        for st in class_lists_by_sec.get(period.section_id, []):
            if st.id in present_today and (st.id, period.id) not in verified_pairs and st.id not in student_passes:
                subj = period.subject.code if period.subject else "CLASS"
                anomalies.append(
                    {
                        "category": "STUDENT",
                        "anomaly_type": "CUTTING_CLASS",
                        "severity": "HIGH",
                        "person_id": st.id,
                        "lrn": st.lrn,
                        "name": st.full_name,
                        "section": period.section.name,
                        "subject": subj,
                        "details": f"Present earlier today but missing from {subj} without an exit pass.",
                        "detected_at": time_str,
                    }
                )

    if leader:
        active_periods = [p for p in periods if p.faculty_id and p.end_time and p.end_time >= now_time]
        fac_ids = {p.faculty_id for p in active_periods}
        latest_logs = {}
        for log in FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
            faculty_id__in=fac_ids, log_date=today
        ).order_by("faculty_id", "-recorded_at"):
            latest_logs.setdefault(log.faculty_id, log)

        for period in active_periods:
            fac = period.faculty
            if not fac or fac.id in faculty_passes:
                continue
            latest = latest_logs.get(fac.id)
            if latest and not latest.is_within_boundary:
                subj = period.subject.code if period.subject else "CLASS"
                anomalies.append(
                    {
                        "category": "FACULTY",
                        "anomaly_type": "OUTSIDE_GEOFENCE",
                        "severity": "HIGH",
                        "person_id": fac.id,
                        "employee_id": fac.employee_id,
                        "name": fac.full_name,
                        "section": period.section.name if period.section else "",
                        "subject": subj,
                        "details": f"Outside campus boundary during {subj} without an exit pass.",
                        "detected_at": timezone.localtime(latest.recorded_at).strftime("%I:%M %p"),
                    }
                )

    return anomalies


def get_emergency_roll_call(*, request=None) -> dict:
    today = timezone.localdate()

    departed_ids = set(
        ExitPass.objects.filter(
            student__isnull=False, valid_from__date=today, status__in=["ACTIVE", "USED"]
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
    )
    present_rows = list(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(attendance_date=today, status__in=["PRESENT", "LATE"])
        .exclude(student_id__in=departed_ids)
        .select_related("student")
    )
    present_student_ids = [r.student_id for r in present_rows]

    section_by_student = {
        enr.student_id: enr.section
        for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
            student_id__in=present_student_ids, status="ENROLLED"
        ).select_related("section", "section__grade_level")
    }

    students = []
    for row in present_rows:
        sec = section_by_student.get(row.student_id)
        students.append(
            {
                "student_id": row.student_id,
                "lrn": row.student.lrn,
                "full_name": row.student.full_name,
                "section": format_section_label(
                    sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else "",
                    sec.name if sec else "",
                ),
                "parent_contact": row.student.parent_contact,
                "photo_url": build_media_url(row.student.photo, request),
            }
        )
    students.sort(key=lambda item: (item["section"], item["full_name"]))

    latest_inside_by_faculty: dict[int, bool] = {}
    for log in FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.filter(log_date=today).order_by("-recorded_at"):
        latest_inside_by_faculty.setdefault(log.faculty_id, bool(log.is_within_boundary))

    inside_faculty_ids = [fid for fid, is_in in latest_inside_by_faculty.items() if is_in]
    faculty = [
        {
            "faculty_id": f.id,
            "employee_id": f.employee_id,
            "full_name": f.full_name,
            "department": f.department,
        }
        for f in FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id__in=inside_faculty_ids, is_active=True)
        .select_related("department_ref")
        .order_by("last_name")
    ]

    return {
        "timestamp": timezone.localtime().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p"),
        "total_people_on_campus": len(students) + len(faculty),
        "students_count": len(students),
        "faculty_count": len(faculty),
        "students": students,
        "faculty": faculty,
    }

from rest_framework import serializers


class CampusBoundaryInputSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    name = serializers.CharField(max_length=120, required=False, default="Main Campus")
    latitude = serializers.FloatField(min_value=-90.0, max_value=90.0)
    longitude = serializers.FloatField(min_value=-180.0, max_value=180.0)
    radius_meters = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=20, max_value=5000, default=150)
    grace_minutes = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=0, max_value=120, required=False, default=10)


class FacultyCheckInSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    action = serializers.ChoiceField(
        choices=["TIME_IN_AM", "TIME_OUT_AM", "TIME_IN_PM", "TIME_OUT_PM"],
        required=False,
    )
    latitude = serializers.FloatField(min_value=-90.0, max_value=90.0)
    longitude = serializers.FloatField(min_value=-180.0, max_value=180.0)
    accuracy = serializers.FloatField(min_value=0.0, required=False, allow_null=True)
    battery_level = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=0, max_value=100, default=100, required=False)
    is_mock_location = serializers.BooleanField(default=False, required=False)
    client_device_id = serializers.CharField(max_length=128, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    device_model = serializers.CharField(max_length=100, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    face_verified = serializers.BooleanField(default=True, required=False)
    liveness_passed = serializers.BooleanField(default=True, required=False)

# backend/apps/campus/services.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from common.audit import record_audit_log
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.gps import verify_campus_gps
from apps.accounts.selectors import resolve_faculty_for_user
from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod, SchoolSettings
from .models import BoundaryAlert, CampusBoundary, FacultyAttendanceLog
from .selectors import get_active_campus_coords


def _infer_attendance_action(local_dt) -> str:
    hr = local_dt.hour
    if hr < 11:
        return "TIME_IN_AM"
    if hr == 11 or (hr == 12 and local_dt.minute < 30):
        return "TIME_OUT_AM"
    if hr < 16:
        return "TIME_IN_PM"
    return "TIME_OUT_PM"


@transaction.atomic
def update_campus_boundary(
    *,
    request,
    name: str,
    latitude: float,
    longitude: float,
    radius_meters: int,
    grace_minutes: int = 10,
) -> CampusBoundary:
    if not (20 <= radius_meters <= 5000):
        raise AppError("INVALID_RADIUS", "Campus radius must be between 20 and 5000 meters.", 400)

    boundary, _ = CampusBoundary.objects.update_or_create(
        id=1,
        defaults={
            "name": name.strip() or "Main Campus",
            "center_lat": latitude,
            "center_lng": longitude,
            "radius_meters": radius_meters,
            "grace_minutes": grace_minutes,
            "is_active": True,
        },
    )

    school = SchoolSettings.objects.first()
    if school and school.campus_boundary_id != boundary.id:
        school.campus_boundary = boundary
        school.save(update_fields=["campus_boundary", "updated_at"])

    record_audit_log(
        request=request,
        table_name=CampusBoundary._meta.db_table,
        record_id=boundary.id,
        action="UPDATE",
        new_data={"latitude": latitude, "longitude": longitude, "radius_meters": radius_meters},
    )
    return boundary


@transaction.atomic
def record_faculty_check_in(*, user, validated_data: dict, header_device_id: str = "") -> dict:
    faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    if not faculty:
        raise AppError("NO_FACULTY_PROFILE", "No faculty profile is linked to this account.", 403)

    if validated_data.get("face_verified") is False or validated_data.get("liveness_passed") is False:
        raise AppError("FACE_CHECK_FAILED", "Mobile on-device face check failed.", 403)

    device_id = str(validated_data.get("client_device_id") or header_device_id or "").strip()
    device_model = str(validated_data.get("device_model") or "").strip()[:100]
    if device_id:
        if not faculty.bound_device_id:
            faculty.bound_device_id = device_id
            if device_model:
                faculty.device_model = device_model
            faculty.device_bound_at = timezone.now()
            faculty.save(update_fields=["bound_device_id", "device_model", "device_bound_at"])
        elif faculty.bound_device_id != device_id:
            raise AppError(
                "DEVICE_MISMATCH",
                "This account is bound to another phone. Ask an administrator to reset your phone lock.",
                403,
            )

    campus_lat, campus_lon, radius, _ = get_active_campus_coords()
    gps = verify_campus_gps(
        latitude=validated_data["latitude"],
        longitude=validated_data["longitude"],
        campus_lat=campus_lat,
        campus_lon=campus_lon,
        radius_meters=radius,
        accuracy_meters=validated_data.get("accuracy"),
        is_mock_location=validated_data.get("is_mock_location", False),
    )
    if not gps["is_valid"]:
        raise AppError("INVALID_GPS", gps["reason"], 400)

    now = timezone.now()
    local_now = timezone.localtime(now)
    action_code = validated_data.get("action") or _infer_attendance_action(local_now)

    log = FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.create(
        faculty=faculty,
        log_date=local_now.date(),
        action=action_code,
        recorded_at=now,
        latitude=validated_data["latitude"],
        longitude=validated_data["longitude"],
        distance_meters=float(gps["distance_meters"] or 0.0),
        is_within_boundary=bool(gps["is_inside"]),
        face_verified=True,
        device_id=device_id,
        remarks=gps["reason"][:255],
    )

    if not gps["is_inside"]:
        BoundaryAlert.objects.get_or_create(
            faculty=faculty,
            incident_date=local_now.date(),
            status="OPEN",
            defaults={
                "left_at": now,
                "last_lat": validated_data["latitude"],
                "last_lng": validated_data["longitude"],
                "distance_meters": float(gps["distance_meters"] or 0.0),
                "remarks": gps["reason"][:255],
            },
        )
    else:
        BoundaryAlert.objects.filter(
            faculty=faculty, incident_date=local_now.date(), status="OPEN"
        ).update(status="RESOLVED", returned_at=now)

        cutoff = (local_now - timedelta(minutes=15)).time()
        unlogged_period = (
            ClassPeriod.objects.filter(
                faculty=faculty,
                is_active=True,
                start_time__lte=cutoff,
                end_time__gte=local_now.time(),
            )
            .exclude(attendance_records__attendance_date=local_now.date())
            .select_related("subject")
            .first()
        )
        if unlogged_period:
            BoundaryAlert.objects.get_or_create(
                faculty=faculty,
                incident_date=local_now.date(),
                remarks=f"On campus via mobile check-in, but no QR scans recorded for {unlogged_period.subject.code}.",
                defaults={"left_at": now, "status": "OPEN"},
            )

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "success": True,
        "heartbeat_id": log.id,
        "action": log.action,
        "is_inside_geofence": gps["is_inside"],
        "distance_meters": gps["distance_meters"],
        "allowed_radius": gps["allowed_radius"],
        "message": gps["reason"],
    }

# backend/apps/campus/views.py
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from common.permissions import IsLeadershipOrReadOnly, IsStaffOrFaculty
from .selectors import (
    get_campus_audit_history,
    get_campus_boundary_overview,
    get_emergency_roll_call,
    get_live_campus_anomalies,
)
from .serializers import CampusBoundaryInputSerializer, FacultyCheckInSerializer
from .services import record_faculty_check_in, update_campus_boundary


class CampusBoundaryView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrReadOnly]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(get_campus_boundary_overview(user=request.user))

    def put(self, request):
        payload = (
            {k: request.data.get(k) for k in request.data.keys()}
            if hasattr(request.data, "keys")
            else dict(request.data)
        )
        if "geofence_radius_meters" in payload and "radius_meters" not in payload:
            payload["radius_meters"] = payload["geofence_radius_meters"]
        serializer = CampusBoundaryInputSerializer(data=payload)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        update_campus_boundary(request=request, **serializer.validated_data)
        return Response(get_campus_boundary_overview(user=request.user))

    def post(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)


class FacultyCheckInView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsStaffOrFaculty]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = FacultyCheckInSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        result = record_faculty_check_in(
            user=request.user,
            validated_data=serializer.validated_data,
            header_device_id=request.headers.get("X-Device-UUID", ""),
        )
        return Response(result, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class BoundaryAuditView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsStaffOrFaculty]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(get_campus_audit_history(user=request.user))


class CampusAnomalyListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsStaffOrFaculty]

    def get(self, request):
        anomalies = get_live_campus_anomalies(user=request.user)
        return Response({"ok": True, "total": len(anomalies), "anomalies": anomalies, "results": anomalies})


class EmergencyRollCallView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsStaffOrFaculty]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(get_emergency_roll_call(request=request))

# backend/apps/messaging/models.py
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone


class Notification(models.Model):
    CATEGORY_CHOICES = [
        ("ATTENDANCE", "Classroom Attendance"),
        ("CUTTING_ALERT", "Cutting Class Alert"),
        ("GATE_PASS", "Exit Pass Update"),
        ("GEOFENCE", "Campus Boundary Alert"),
        ("ANNOUNCEMENT", "School Announcement"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    recipient = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="notifications", db_index=True
    )
    student = models.ForeignKey(
        "students.Student", on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, related_name="parent_notifications"
    )
    title = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    message = models.TextField()
    category = models.CharField(max_length=25, choices=CATEGORY_CHOICES, default="ATTENDANCE", db_index=True)
    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)
    is_pushed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "app_notifications"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["recipient", "is_read", "-created_at"], name="idx_notif_rec_read_dt"),
        ]


class ChatMessage(models.Model):
    """
    3NF Parent-Faculty Message:
    - Removed redundant parent FK because student.parent_user defines the parent account.
    """

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey("students.Student", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="chat_messages")
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="chat_messages",
    )
    sender = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="sent_chat_messages"
    )
    body = models.TextField()
    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "parent_faculty_messages"
        ordering = ["created_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["student", "faculty", "created_at"], name="idx_chat_thread_time"),
        ]

from django.db.models import Count, OuterRef, Q, Subquery
from django.utils import timezone
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.media import build_media_url
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from apps.accounts.models import FacultyProfile
from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_student_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
from apps.school.models import ClassPeriod
from apps.school.selectors import format_section_label
from apps.students.models import Enrollment, Student
from .models import ChatMessage


def resolve_default_faculty_for_student(student: Student) -> FacultyProfile | None:
    enr = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, status="ENROLLED")
        .select_related("section", "section__adviser")
        .order_by("-id")
        .first()
    )
    if enr and enr.section:
        if enr.section.adviser:
            return enr.section.adviser
        period = (
            ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=enr.section, is_active=True, faculty__isnull=False)
            .select_related("faculty")
            .first()
        )
        if period and period.faculty:
            return period.faculty
    return FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("id").first()


def authorize_chat_thread(
    *, user, student_id: int, faculty_id: int | None = None, teacher_id: int | None = None
):
    student = Student.objects.select_related("parent_user").filter(id=student_id, is_active=True).first()
    if not student:
        raise AppError("STUDENT_NOT_FOUND", "Student not found.", 404)

    resolved_faculty_id = faculty_id or teacher_id
    faculty = (
        FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id=resolved_faculty_id, is_active=True).first()
        if resolved_faculty_id
        else None
    )
    faculty = faculty or resolve_default_faculty_for_student(student)
    if not faculty:
        raise AppError("FACULTY_NOT_FOUND", "Assigned faculty member not found.", 404)

    role = get_user_role(user)
    if role == "PARENT":
        if student.parent_user_id != user.id:
            raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "This student is not linked to your parent account.", 403)
        return student, faculty, user

    if not student.parent_user:
        raise AppError("NO_PARENT_LINKED", "This student has no linked parent account yet.", 400)

    if not is_school_leader(user):
        own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not own_faculty or student.id not in get_faculty_student_ids(own_faculty):
            raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "Student is not in your assigned classes.", 403)

    return student, faculty, student.parent_user


def get_chat_threads_for_user(*, user, request=None) -> list[dict]:
    role = get_user_role(user)
    latest_msg = ChatMessage.objects.filter(student=OuterRef("pk")).order_by("-created_at")

    if role == "PARENT":
        base_qs = Student.objects.filter(parent_user=user, is_active=True)
    else:
        own_teacher = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if is_school_leader(user) and not own_teacher:
            base_qs = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True, parent_user__isnull=False)
        elif own_teacher:
            base_qs = Student.objects.filter(
                id__in=get_faculty_student_ids(own_teacher),
                is_active=True,
                parent_user__isnull=False,
            )
        else:
            base_qs = Student.objects.none()

    students = list(
        base_qs.select_related("parent_user")
        .annotate(
            last_body=Subquery(latest_msg.values("body")[:1]),
            last_dt=Subquery(latest_msg.values("created_at")[:1]),
            unread_total=Count(
                "chat_messages",
                filter=Q(chat_messages__is_read=False) & ~Q(chat_messages__sender=user),
            ),
        )
        .order_by("last_name", "first_name")[:150]
    )

    enrollments = {
        e.student_id: e.section
        for e in Enrollment.objects.filter(student_id__in=[s.id for s in students], status="ENROLLED")
        .select_related("section", "section__grade_level", "section__adviser")
    }

    own_teacher = resolve_faculty_for_user(user) if role != "PARENT" else None
    threads = []

    for st in students:
        sec = enrollments.get(st.id)
        teacher = own_teacher or (sec.adviser if (sec and sec.adviser) else None) or resolve_default_faculty_for_student(st)
        if not teacher or not st.parent_user:
            continue

        section_label = format_section_label(sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else "", sec.name if sec else "")
        parent_name = st.guardian_name or st.mother_maiden_name or st.father_name or st.parent_user.get_full_name() or st.parent_user.username
        photo_field = teacher.photo if role == "PARENT" else st.photo

        threads.append({
            "thread_key": f"{st.id}-{teacher.id}",
            "student_id": st.id,
            "student_name": st.full_name,
            "student_lrn": st.lrn,
            "section": section_label,
            "faculty_id": teacher.id,
            "teacher_id": teacher.id,
            "faculty_name": teacher.full_name,
            "teacher_name": teacher.full_name,
            "parent_id": st.parent_user_id,
            "parent_name": parent_name,
            "photo_url": build_media_url(photo_field, request),
            "last_message": st.last_body or "Tap to start conversation",
            "last_time": timezone.localtime(st.last_dt).strftime("%I:%M %p") if st.last_dt else "",
            "unread_count": st.unread_total or 0,
        })

    threads.sort(key=lambda item: (item["unread_count"] > 0, bool(item["last_time"])), reverse=True)
    return threads


def get_thread_messages(
    *, user, student_id: int, faculty_id: int | None = None, teacher_id: int | None = None
) -> dict:
    resolved_faculty_id = faculty_id or teacher_id
    student, faculty, _ = authorize_chat_thread(
        user=user, student_id=student_id, faculty_id=resolved_faculty_id
    )
    qs = (
        ChatMessage.objects.filter(student=student, faculty=faculty)
        .select_related("sender")
        .order_by("created_at")
    )

    qs.filter(is_read=False).exclude(sender=user).update(is_read=True)

    return {
        "student_id": student.id,
        "student_name": student.full_name,
        "teacher_id": faculty.id,
        "faculty_id": faculty.id,
        "teacher_name": faculty.full_name,
        "faculty_name": faculty.full_name,
        "messages": [
            {
                "id": m.id,
                "sender_id": m.sender_id,
                "sender_name": m.sender.get_full_name() or m.sender.username,
                "is_mine": m.sender_id == user.id,
                "body": m.body,
                "is_read": m.is_read,
                "created_at": timezone.localtime(m.created_at).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p"),
                "time_only": timezone.localtime(m.created_at).strftime("%I:%M %p"),
            }
            for m in qs[:200]
        ],
    }

from rest_framework import serializers
from .models import Notification


class NotificationSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source="student.full_name", read_only=True, default="")
    recipient_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    target_role = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_blank=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Notification
        fields = [
            "id", "recipient", "recipient_name", "student", "student_name", "title",
            "message", "category", "is_read", "is_pushed", "target_role", "created_at",
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["id", "is_pushed", "created_at"]
        extra_kwargs = {"recipient": {"required": False}}

    def get_recipient_name(self, obj) -> str:
        if not obj.recipient:
            return ""
        return obj.recipient.get_full_name() or obj.recipient.username

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\messaging\services.py

from django.db import transaction

from apps.accounts.models import UserProfile
from common.exceptions import AppError
from .models import ChatMessage, Notification
from .tasks import deliver_expo_push_task


def send_user_notification(
    *,
    recipient,
    title: str,
    message: str,
    category: str = "ATTENDANCE",
    student=None,
) -> Notification | None:
    if not recipient or not recipient.is_active:
        return None

    notif = Notification.objects.create(
        recipient=recipient,
        student=student,
        title=title[:150],
        message=message,
        category=category,
    )

    profile = getattr(recipient, "profile", None) or UserProfile.objects.filter(user=recipient).first()
    push_token = profile.push_token if profile else None
    if push_token and str(push_token).startswith(("ExponentPushToken", "ExpoPushToken")):
        transaction.on_commit(
            lambda: deliver_expo_push_task.delay(
                notification_id=notif.id,
                push_token=push_token,
                title=title[:150],
                body=message[:250],
            )
        )
    return notif


def notify_student_parent(*, student, title: str, message: str, category: str = "ATTENDANCE"):
    if not student:
        return None

    parent_user = student.parent_user
    if not parent_user and student.parent_contact:
        matched = (
            UserProfile.objects.filter(
                role="PARENT",
                phone_number=student.parent_contact,
                user__is_active=True,
            )
            .select_related("user")
            .first()
        )
        if matched:
            parent_user = matched.user
            student.parent_user = parent_user
            student.save(update_fields=["parent_user"])

    if not parent_user:
        return None

    return send_user_notification(
        recipient=parent_user,
        title=title,
        message=message,
        category=category,
        student=student,
    )


def mark_notifications_read(*, user, notification_ids: list[int] | None = None) -> int:
    qs = Notification.objects.filter(recipient=user, is_read=False)
    if notification_ids:
        qs = qs.filter(id__in=notification_ids)
    return qs.update(is_read=True)


def retry_pending_push_notifications(limit: int = 100) -> int:
    """Re-queues unpushed notifications for active mobile devices."""
    pending = (
        Notification.objects.filter(is_pushed=False)
        .select_related("recipient__profile")
        .order_by("-created_at")[:limit]
    )
    queued = 0
    for notif in pending:
        profile = getattr(notif.recipient, "profile", None)
        push_token = profile.push_token if profile else None
        if push_token and str(push_token).startswith(("ExponentPushToken", "ExpoPushToken")):
            deliver_expo_push_task.delay(
                notification_id=notif.id,
                push_token=push_token,
                title=notif.title[:150],
                body=notif.message[:250],
            )
            queued += 1
    return queued


@transaction.atomic
def create_chat_message(
    *, sender, student, faculty=None, teacher=None, parent_user=None, body: str
) -> ChatMessage:
    clean_body = str(body or "").strip()
    if not clean_body:
        raise AppError("EMPTY_MESSAGE", "Message text cannot be empty.", 400)
    if len(clean_body) > 1000:
        raise AppError("MESSAGE_TOO_LONG", "Message cannot exceed 1000 characters.", 400)

    target_faculty = faculty or teacher
    if not target_faculty:
        raise AppError("FACULTY_NOT_FOUND", "Assigned faculty member is required.", 400)

    resolved_parent = parent_user or student.parent_user

    msg = ChatMessage.objects.create(
        student=student,
        faculty=target_faculty,
        sender=sender,
        body=clean_body,
    )

    linked_account = getattr(target_faculty, "account", None)
    faculty_user = linked_account.user if linked_account else None
    recipient = faculty_user if (resolved_parent and sender.id == resolved_parent.id) else resolved_parent
    sender_name = sender.get_full_name() or sender.username

    if recipient:
        send_user_notification(
            recipient=recipient,
            title=f"New Message ({student.first_name}): {sender_name}",
            message=clean_body[:180],
            category="ANNOUNCEMENT",
            student=student,
        )
    return msg


@transaction.atomic
def update_chat_message(*, user, message_id: int, new_body: str) -> ChatMessage:
    clean_body = str(new_body or "").strip()
    if not clean_body or len(clean_body) > 1000:
        raise AppError("INVALID_MESSAGE", "Message must be between 1 and 1000 characters.", 400)

    msg = ChatMessage.objects.filter(id=message_id).first()
    if not msg:
        raise AppError("MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND", "Message not found.", 404)
    if msg.sender_id != user.id:
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "You can only edit your own messages.", 403)

    msg.body = clean_body
    msg.save(update_fields=["body"])
    return msg


@transaction.atomic
def delete_chat_message(*, user, message_id: int) -> None:
    msg = ChatMessage.objects.filter(id=message_id).first()
    if not msg:
        raise AppError("MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND", "Message not found.", 404)
    if msg.sender_id != user.id and not user.is_superuser:
        raise AppError("FORBIDDEN", "You can only delete your own messages.", 403)
    msg.delete()

import logging
import requests
from celery import shared_task
from .models import Notification

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
EXPO_PUSH_URL = "https://exp.host/--/api/v2/push/send"


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=2, default_retry_delay=5)
def deliver_expo_push_task(self, notification_id: int, push_token: str, title: str, body: str):
    if not str(push_token or "").startswith(("ExponentPushToken", "ExpoPushToken")):
        return
    try:
        resp = requests.post(
            EXPO_PUSH_URL,
            json={"to": push_token, "sound": "default", "title": title[:150], "body": body[:250]},
            timeout=5,
        )
        resp.raise_for_status()
        Notification.objects.filter(id=notification_id).update(is_pushed=True)
    except requests.RequestException as exc:
        logger.warning("Push retry for notification %s: %s", notification_id, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)

import inspect
import re

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.html import strip_tags
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.throttling import UserRateThrottle
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from common.audit import AuditedModelViewSet, record_audit_log
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from .models import Notification
from .selectors import authorize_chat_thread, get_chat_threads_for_user, get_thread_messages
from .serializers import NotificationSerializer
from .services import (
    create_chat_message,
    delete_chat_message,
    mark_notifications_read,
    send_user_notification,
    update_chat_message,
)

User = get_user_model()

MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 150
MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 2000
MAX_MARK_READ_IDS = 200
CONTROL_CHARS_RE = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f]")


class MessagingWriteThrottle(UserRateThrottle):
    """Prevents message flooding and notification spam."""

    rate = "60/minute"


def _parse_positive_int(value: object) -> int | None:
    """Safely parses ASCII positive integers without crashing on Unicode digits."""
    text = str(value or "").strip()
    if not text.isascii() or not text.isdigit():
        return None
    parsed = int(text)
    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None


def _clean_plain_text(value: object, max_length: int) -> str:
    """Removes HTML tags and hidden control characters, then enforces max length."""
    raw = str(value or "")
    without_controls = CONTROL_CHARS_RE.sub("", raw)
    cleaned = strip_tags(without_controls).strip()
    return cleaned[:max_length]


def _queue_push_alerts(notifications: list[Notification], push_map: dict[int, str], title: str, body: str) -> None:
    """Dispatches Expo push background tasks safely after the DB transaction commits."""
    try:
        from .tasks import deliver_expo_push_task
    except ImportError:
        return

    short_title = title[:MAX_TITLE_LENGTH]
    short_body = body[:250]

    for notif in notifications:
        token = push_map.get(notif.recipient_id, "")
        if token and token.startswith(("ExponentPushToken[", "ExpoPushToken[")):
            try:
                deliver_expo_push_task.delay(
                    notification_id=notif.id,
                    push_token=token,
                    title=short_title,
                    body=short_body,
                )
            except Exception:
                # Push queue failure must never break saved in-app notifications
                continue


def _get_allowed_faculty_parent_ids(user) -> list[int]:
    """Returns active parent user IDs strictly for learners handled by this faculty member."""
    try:
        from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_student_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
        from apps.students.models import Student

        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty:
            return []
        student_ids = get_faculty_student_ids(faculty)
        if not student_ids:
            return []
        return list(
            Student.objects.filter(
                id__in=student_ids,
                is_active=True,
                parent_user_id__isnull=False,
                parent_user__is_active=True,
            )
            .values_list("parent_user_id", flat=True)
            .distinct()
        )
    except Exception:
        return []


def _call_authorize_thread(*, user, student_id: int, faculty_id: int | None):
    """Supports both updated `faculty_id` and legacy `teacher_id` selector signatures."""
    params = inspect.signature(authorize_chat_thread).parameters
    if "faculty_id" in params:
        return authorize_chat_thread(user=user, student_id=student_id, faculty_id=faculty_id)
    return authorize_chat_thread(user=user, student_id=student_id, teacher_id=faculty_id)


def _call_get_thread_messages(*, user, student_id: int, faculty_id: int | None):
    """Supports both updated `faculty_id` and legacy `teacher_id` selector signatures."""
    params = inspect.signature(get_thread_messages).parameters
    if "faculty_id" in params:
        return get_thread_messages(user=user, student_id=student_id, faculty_id=faculty_id)
    return get_thread_messages(user=user, student_id=student_id, teacher_id=faculty_id)


def _call_create_chat_message(*, sender, student, faculty, parent_user, body: str):
    """Supports both 3NF (`faculty` without `parent_user`) and legacy service signatures."""
    params = inspect.signature(create_chat_message).parameters
    kwargs = {"sender": sender, "student": student, "body": body}
    if "faculty" in params:
        kwargs["faculty"] = faculty
    elif "teacher" in params:
        kwargs["teacher"] = faculty
    if "parent_user" in params:
        kwargs["parent_user"] = parent_user
    return create_chat_message(**kwargs)


class NotificationViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = NotificationSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    http_method_names = ["get", "post", "put", "patch", "delete", "head", "options"]

    def get_throttles(self):
        if self.action in ("create", "update", "partial_update", "destroy"):
            return [MessagingWriteThrottle()]
        return super().get_throttles()

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = (
            Notification.objects.select_related("student", "recipient")
            .only(
                "id",
                "recipient_id",
                "recipient__id",
                "recipient__username",
                "recipient__first_name",
                "recipient__last_name",
                "student_id",
                "student__id",
                "student__lrn",
                "student__first_name",
                "student__middle_name",
                "student__last_name",
                "student__suffix",
                "title",
                "message",
                "category",
                "is_read",
                "is_pushed",
                "created_at",
            )
            .order_by("-created_at")
        )
        scope = str(self.request.query_params.get("scope") or "").strip().lower()
        if is_school_leader(self.request.user) and (
            scope == "all" or self.action in ("retrieve", "update", "partial_update", "destroy")
        ):
            return qs
        return qs.filter(recipient=self.request.user)

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        leader = is_school_leader(self.request.user)
        validated = serializer.validated_data
        if not leader:
            disallowed = set(validated.keys()) - {"is_read"}
            if disallowed:
                self.permission_denied(
                    self.request,
                    message="Only school leaders can edit notification content.",
                )
        if "title" in validated:
            cleaned_title = _clean_plain_text(validated["title"], MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
            if not cleaned_title:
                raise ValidationError({"title": "Title cannot be empty."})
            validated["title"] = cleaned_title
        if "message" in validated:
            cleaned_msg = _clean_plain_text(validated["message"], MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH)
            if not cleaned_msg:
                raise ValidationError({"message": "Message cannot be empty."})
            validated["message"] = cleaned_msg
        if "category" in validated:
            validated["category"] = str(validated["category"] or "ANNOUNCEMENT").strip().upper()
        super().perform_update(serializer)

    def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        validated = dict(serializer.validated_data)

        target_role = str(validated.pop("target_role", "") or "").strip().upper()
        title = _clean_plain_text(validated.get("title"), MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
        message = _clean_plain_text(validated.get("message"), MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH)
        category = str(validated.get("category") or "ANNOUNCEMENT").strip().upper()
        student = validated.get("student")

        if not title or not message:
            return Response(
                {"error": "Both title and message are required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        leader = is_school_leader(request.user)
        user_role = get_user_role(request.user)

        # Group broadcast path (fast single-query bulk insert)
        if target_role:
            if target_role not in ("PARENT", "FACULTY", "ALL"):
                return Response(
                    {"error": "Invalid target_role. Choose PARENT, FACULTY, or ALL."},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )

            if not (leader or user_role == "FACULTY"):
                return Response(
                    {"error": "Only faculty and school leaders can send group notices."},
                    status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                )

            # Regular faculty can only broadcast to parents of their own handled students
            if not leader:
                if target_role != "PARENT":
                    return Response(
                        {"error": "Only school leaders can broadcast to all faculty or the entire school."},
                        status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                    )
                allowed_parent_ids = _get_allowed_faculty_parent_ids(request.user)
                users_qs = User.objects.filter(id__in=allowed_parent_ids, is_active=True)
            else:
                users_qs = User.objects.filter(is_active=True)
                if target_role == "PARENT":
                    users_qs = users_qs.filter(profile__role="PARENT")
                elif target_role == "FACULTY":
                    users_qs = users_qs.filter(
                        profile__role__in=["FACULTY", "ADMIN", "PRINCIPAL", "DEPT_HEAD"]
                    )

            recipient_rows = list(
                users_qs.select_related("profile")
                .only("id", "is_active", "profile__push_token")
                .distinct()
            )
            if not recipient_rows:
                return Response(
                    {"error": "No active recipients matched the selected group."},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )

            now = timezone.now()
            push_map = {
                u.id: str(getattr(getattr(u, "profile", None), "push_token", "") or "").strip()
                for u in recipient_rows
            }

            # Never attach an individual student's record to a group-wide broadcast (prevents data leak)
            notifications_to_insert = [
                Notification(
                    recipient_id=u.id,
                    student=None,
                    title=title,
                    message=message,
                    category=category,
                    created_at=now,
                )
                for u in recipient_rows
            ]

            with transaction.atomic():
                created_rows = Notification.objects.bulk_create(
                    notifications_to_insert, batch_size=500
                )
                record_audit_log(
                    request=request,
                    table_name=Notification._meta.db_table,
                    record_id=created_rows[0].id if created_rows else 0,
                    action="CREATE",
                    new_data={
                        "target_role": target_role,
                        "dispatched_count": len(created_rows),
                        "title": title[:120],
                    },
                )
                transaction.on_commit(
                    lambda: _queue_push_alerts(created_rows, push_map, title, message)
                )

            first_row = created_rows[0]
            payload = {
                "id": first_row.id,
                "title": first_row.title,
                "message": first_row.message,
                "category": first_row.category,
                "created_at": first_row.created_at,
                "ok": True,
                "dispatched_count": len(created_rows),
            }
            return Response(payload, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

        # Direct single-user notification path
        recipient = validated.get("recipient") or request.user
        if recipient.id != request.user.id:
            if leader:
                pass
            elif user_role == "FACULTY":
                allowed_parent_ids = set(_get_allowed_faculty_parent_ids(request.user))
                if recipient.id not in allowed_parent_ids:
                    return Response(
                        {"error": "You can only notify parents of learners in your classes."},
                        status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                    )
            else:
                return Response(
                    {"error": "You do not have permission to send notices to other users."},
                    status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                )

        # Prevent linking a student that does not belong to a parent recipient
        if student is not None and get_user_role(recipient) == "PARENT":
            if getattr(student, "parent_user_id", None) != recipient.id:
                return Response(
                    {"error": "Selected student is not linked to this parent account."},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )

        with transaction.atomic():
            notif = send_user_notification(
                recipient=recipient,
                title=title,
                message=message,
                category=category,
                student=student,
            )
            record_audit_log(
                request=request,
                table_name=Notification._meta.db_table,
                record_id=notif.id if notif else 0,
                action="CREATE",
                new_data={
                    "recipient_id": recipient.id,
                    "title": title[:120],
                    "category": category,
                },
            )

        payload = dict(self.get_serializer(notif).data) if notif else {}
        payload["ok"] = True
        payload["dispatched_count"] = 1 if notif else 0
        return Response(payload, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class MarkNotificationsReadView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    throttle_classes = [MessagingWriteThrottle]

    def post(self, request):
        raw_ids = request.data.get("notification_ids")
        if raw_ids is None:
            raw_ids = request.data.get("ids")

        clean_ids: list[int] | None = None
        if raw_ids is not None:
            if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
                return Response(
                    {"error": "notification_ids must be a list of numbers."},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )
            parsed_ids = []
            for item in raw_ids[:MAX_MARK_READ_IDS]:
                parsed = _parse_positive_int(item)
                if parsed is not None:
                    parsed_ids.append(parsed)
            clean_ids = list(dict.fromkeys(parsed_ids))

        count = mark_notifications_read(user=request.user, notification_ids=clean_ids)
        return Response({"ok": True, "marked_read_count": count})


class ChatThreadListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        threads = get_chat_threads_for_user(user=request.user, request=request)
        return Response({"threads": threads, "total": len(threads)})


class ChatMessageView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get_throttles(self):
        if self.request.method in ("POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"):
            return [MessagingWriteThrottle()]
        return super().get_throttles()

    def get(self, request):
        student_id = _parse_positive_int(request.query_params.get("student_id"))
        raw_faculty_id = request.query_params.get("faculty_id") or request.query_params.get("teacher_id")
        faculty_id = _parse_positive_int(raw_faculty_id) if raw_faculty_id is not None else None

        if not student_id:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid student_id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        if raw_faculty_id is not None and faculty_id is None:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid faculty_id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        return Response(
            _call_get_thread_messages(
                user=request.user,
                student_id=student_id,
                faculty_id=faculty_id,
            )
        )

    def post(self, request):
        student_id = _parse_positive_int(request.data.get("student_id"))
        raw_faculty_id = request.data.get("faculty_id") or request.data.get("teacher_id")
        faculty_id = _parse_positive_int(raw_faculty_id) if raw_faculty_id is not None else None

        if not student_id:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid student_id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        if raw_faculty_id is not None and faculty_id is None:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid faculty_id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        clean_body = _clean_plain_text(
            request.data.get("body") or request.data.get("message"),
            MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH,
        )
        if not clean_body:
            return Response(
                {"error": "Message text cannot be empty."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        student, faculty, parent_user = _call_authorize_thread(
            user=request.user,
            student_id=student_id,
            faculty_id=faculty_id,
        )
        msg = _call_create_chat_message(
            sender=request.user,
            student=student,
            faculty=faculty,
            parent_user=parent_user,
            body=clean_body,
        )

        local_dt = timezone.localtime(msg.created_at)
        sender_name = (request.user.get_full_name() or request.user.username).strip()

        return Response(
            {
                "id": msg.id,
                "sender_id": msg.sender_id,
                "sender_name": sender_name,
                "is_mine": True,
                "body": msg.body,
                "is_read": False,
                "created_at": local_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p"),
                "time_only": local_dt.strftime("%I:%M %p"),
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    def put(self, request):
        msg_id = _parse_positive_int(request.data.get("id") or request.data.get("message_id"))
        if not msg_id:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid message id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        clean_body = _clean_plain_text(
            request.data.get("body") or request.data.get("message"),
            MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH,
        )
        if not clean_body:
            return Response(
                {"error": "Message text cannot be empty."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        msg = update_chat_message(
            user=request.user,
            message_id=msg_id,
            new_body=clean_body,
        )
        return Response({"ok": True, "id": msg.id, "body": msg.body})

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    def delete(self, request):
        msg_id = _parse_positive_int(request.data.get("id") or request.query_params.get("id"))
        if not msg_id:
            return Response(
                {"error": "A valid message id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        delete_chat_message(user=request.user, message_id=msg_id)
        return Response({"ok": True, "message": "Message deleted."})

"""
AttendSure - Ed-Fi Data Standard v6.0 Interoperability & GDPR Export Engine
File: backend/apps/reports/edfi.py

Produces standards-compliant Ed-Fi v6.0 JSON bundles with optional GDPR Art. 25
pseudonymization (`anonymize=True`) so districts, ministries, and international
NGOs can ingest live attendance, roster, and staff telemetry without vendor lock-in.
"""

from datetime import timedelta
import hashlib
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import FacultyProfile
from apps.attendance.models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance
from apps.school.models import SchoolYear, Section
from apps.school.selectors import get_school_metadata
from apps.students.models import Enrollment, Student


ATTENDANCE_DESCRIPTOR_MAP = {
    "PRESENT": "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#In Attendance",
    "LATE": "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#Tardy",
    "ABSENT": "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#Unexcused Absence",
    "CUTTING": "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#Early departure",
    "EXCUSED": "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#Excused Absence",
}

SEX_DESCRIPTOR_MAP = {
    "M": "uri://ed-fi.org/SexDescriptor#Male",
    "MALE": "uri://ed-fi.org/SexDescriptor#Male",
    "F": "uri://ed-fi.org/SexDescriptor#Female",
    "FEMALE": "uri://ed-fi.org/SexDescriptor#Female",
}


def _pseudonymize(prefix: str, value: str) -> str:
    salt = str(getattr(settings, "SECRET_KEY", "attendsure"))[:16]
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{salt}:{prefix}:{value}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16].upper()
    return f"{prefix}-{digest}"


def build_edfi_v6_export(
    *,
    request=None,
    days: int = 30,
    anonymize: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    days = max(1, min(90, int(days)))
    today = timezone.localdate()
    start_date = today - timedelta(days=days - 1)
    meta = get_school_metadata(request)
    active_sy = SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
    sy_code = active_sy.code if active_sy else f"{today.year}-{today.year + 1}"
    school_id_raw = str(meta.get("school_id") or "ATTENDSURE-001")
    school_numeric_id = int(school_id_raw) if school_id_raw.isdigit() else school_id_raw

    sections_qs = Section.objects.filter(is_active=True).select_related("grade_level", "adviser").order_by("id")
    students_qs = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("id")[:2000]
    enrollments_qs = list(
        Enrollment.objects.filter(status__in=["ENROLLED", "TRANSFERRED_IN"], student__is_active=True)
        .select_related("student", "section", "section__grade_level", "section__school_year")
        .order_by("id")[:2000]
    )
    student_section_map = {enr.student_id: enr.section_id for enr in enrollments_qs if enr.section_id}
    teachers_qs = (
        FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        .select_related("department_ref", "position_ref")
        .order_by("id")[:500]
    )

    daily_qs = (
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(attendance_date__gte=start_date, attendance_date__lte=today)
        .select_related("student")
        .order_by("-attendance_date", "id")[:5000]
    )
    class_qs = (
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(attendance_date__gte=start_date, attendance_date__lte=today)
        .select_related("student", "class_period", "class_period__section", "class_period__subject")
        .order_by("-attendance_date", "-scanned_at")[:5000]
    )

    def student_uid(st: Student) -> str:
        raw = st.lrn or str(st.id)
        return _pseudonymize("STU", raw) if anonymize else raw

    def staff_uid(t: FacultyProfile) -> str:
        raw = t.employee_id or str(t.id)
        return _pseudonymize("STF", raw) if anonymize else raw

    schools_payload = [
        {
            "schoolId": school_numeric_id,
            "nameOfInstitution": meta.get("school_name") or "AttendSure Pilot School",
            "localEducationAgency": meta.get("division") or "District Education Office",
            "region": meta.get("region") or "",
            "schoolTypeDescriptor": "uri://ed-fi.org/SchoolTypeDescriptor#Regular",
            "operationalStatusDescriptor": "uri://ed-fi.org/OperationalStatusDescriptor#Active",
        }
    ]

    sections_payload = [
        {
            "sectionIdentifier": f"SEC-{sec.id}",
            "sectionName": sec.name,
            "gradeLevelDescriptor": (
                f"uri://ed-fi.org/GradeLevelDescriptor#{sec.grade_level.name}"
                if sec.grade_level
                else "uri://ed-fi.org/GradeLevelDescriptor#Ungraded"
            ),
            "schoolReference": {"schoolId": school_numeric_id},
            "adviserStaffUniqueId": staff_uid(sec.adviser) if sec.adviser else None,
        }
        for sec in sections_qs
    ]

    students_payload = []
    for st in students_qs:
        sex_key = str(st.sex or "").strip().upper()
        entry = {
            "studentUniqueId": student_uid(st),
            "sexDescriptor": SEX_DESCRIPTOR_MAP.get(sex_key, "uri://ed-fi.org/SexDescriptor#Not Selected"),
            "birthDate": f"{st.birthdate.year}-01-01" if (anonymize and st.birthdate) else (str(st.birthdate) if st.birthdate else None),
        }
        if not anonymize:
            entry["firstName"] = st.first_name
            entry["middleName"] = st.middle_name or ""
            entry["lastSurname"] = st.last_name
        students_payload.append(entry)

    enrollments_payload = [
        {
            "studentReference": {"studentUniqueId": student_uid(enr.student)},
            "sectionReference": {
                "schoolId": school_numeric_id,
                "sectionIdentifier": f"SEC-{enr.section_id}" if enr.section_id else "",
            },
            "beginDate": str(enr.enrollment_date),
            "enrollmentStatus": enr.status,
            "schoolYear": enr.school_year.code if enr.school_year else sy_code,
            "isConditionalCashTransferRecipient": bool(enr.is_cct_recipient),
        }
        for enr in enrollments_qs
    ]

    staff_payload = []
    for t in teachers_qs:
        entry = {
            "staffUniqueId": staff_uid(t),
            "positionTitle": t.position or "Faculty",
            "department": t.department or "",
            "canTeach": bool(t.can_handle_classes),
        }
        if not anonymize:
            entry["firstName"] = t.first_name
            entry["lastSurname"] = t.last_name
        staff_payload.append(entry)

    school_attendance_events = [
        {
            "studentReference": {"studentUniqueId": student_uid(row.student)},
            "schoolReference": {"schoolId": school_numeric_id},
            "eventDate": str(row.attendance_date),
            "attendanceEventCategoryDescriptor": ATTENDANCE_DESCRIPTOR_MAP.get(
                str(row.status).upper(),
                "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#In Attendance",
            ),
            "sectionIdentifier": (
                f"SEC-{student_section_map[row.student_id]}" if row.student_id in student_section_map else ""
            ),
            "attendanceEventReason": row.remarks or "",
        }
        for row in daily_qs
    ]

    section_attendance_events = [
        {
            "studentReference": {"studentUniqueId": student_uid(row.student)},
            "sectionReference": {
                "schoolId": school_numeric_id,
                "sectionIdentifier": f"SEC-{row.class_period.section_id}" if (row.class_period and row.class_period.section_id) else "",
                "subjectCode": row.class_period.subject.code if (row.class_period and row.class_period.subject) else "",
            },
            "eventDate": str(row.attendance_date),
            "attendanceEventCategoryDescriptor": ATTENDANCE_DESCRIPTOR_MAP.get(
                str(row.status).upper(),
                "uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#In Attendance",
            ),
            "sourceMethod": row.scan_method,
            "scannedAt": timezone.localtime(row.scanned_at).isoformat(),
        }
        for row in class_qs
    ]

    return {
        "edFiVersion": "6.0",
        "standard": "Ed-Fi ODS/API Data Standard v6.0",
        "generatedAt": timezone.localtime().isoformat(),
        "privacyMode": "GDPR_PSEUDONYMIZED" if anonymize else "STANDARD_INSTITUTIONAL",
        "complianceFrameworks": [
            "Ed-Fi Data Standard v6.0",
            "GDPR Art. 5 & Art. 25 (Data Minimization / Pseudonymization)",
            "FERPA & COPPA Student Privacy",
            "Philippines Data Privacy Act (RA 10173)",
            "DepEd Official School Forms (SF1/SF2/SF4/CSC Form 48)",
        ],
        "reportingWindow": {
            "startDate": str(start_date),
            "endDate": str(today),
            "days": days,
            "schoolYear": sy_code,
        },
        "recordCounts": {
            "schools": len(schools_payload),
            "sections": len(sections_payload),
            "students": len(students_payload),
            "studentSectionAssociations": len(enrollments_payload),
            "staffs": len(staff_payload),
            "studentSchoolAttendanceEvents": len(school_attendance_events),
            "studentSectionAttendanceEvents": len(section_attendance_events),
        },
        "schools": schools_payload,
        "sections": sections_payload,
        "students": students_payload,
        "studentSectionAssociations": enrollments_payload,
        "staffs": staff_payload,
        "studentSchoolAttendanceEvents": school_attendance_events,
        "studentSectionAttendanceEvents": section_attendance_events,
    }

import calendar
import time as time_module
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db.models import Count, Q
from django.db.models.functions import ExtractHour
from django.utils import timezone

from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.media import build_media_url

from apps.accounts.models import AuditLog, FacultyProfile, UserProfile

    get_faculty_section_ids,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
