        school_days = []
        cursor = today
        while len(school_days) < 14:
            if cursor.weekday() < 5 or cursor == today:
                school_days.append(cursor)
            cursor -= timedelta(days=1)

        for day_idx, att_date in enumerate(school_days):
            for st_idx, (student, sec, pattern) in enumerate(seeded_students):
                if pattern == "critical_sardo":
                    if day_idx == 0:
                        st_status = "ABSENT"
                    elif day_idx in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 9):
                        st_status = "ABSENT"
                    elif day_idx in (4, 6, 8):
                        st_status = "CUTTING"
                    else:
                        st_status = "LATE"
                elif pattern == "early_warning":
                    if day_idx in (1, 4, 8):
                        st_status = "ABSENT"
                    elif day_idx in (2, 6):
                        st_status = "LATE"
                    else:
                        st_status = "PRESENT"
                elif pattern == "late":
                    st_status = "LATE" if day_idx % 3 == 0 else "PRESENT"
                else:
                    st_status = "PRESENT" if (day_idx + st_idx) % 11 != 0 else "LATE"

                DailyAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
                    student=student,
                    attendance_date=att_date,
                    defaults={
                        "status": st_status,
                        "remarks": "Flagged for home visit" if pattern == "critical_sardo" and st_status == "ABSENT" else "",
                    },
                )

                sec_periods = periods_by_section.get(sec.id, [])
                for p_idx, period in enumerate(sec_periods):
                    scan_hour = min(16, period.start_time.hour)
                    scan_minute = min(55, (st_idx * 2 + p_idx * 5) % 50)
                    scanned_dt = timezone.make_aware(
                        datetime.combine(att_date, time(scan_hour, scan_minute, 10))
                    )
                    ClassAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
                        student=student,
                        class_period=period,
                        attendance_date=att_date,
                        defaults={
                            "recorded_by": period.faculty or adviser_teacher,
                            "scanned_at": scanned_dt,
                            "scan_method": "QR" if st_status != "ABSENT" else "MANUAL",
                            "status": st_status,
                        },
                    )

        # 6. Teacher Campus GPS Telemetry & DTR
        ping_slots = [
            (time(7, 42), "TIME_IN_AM"),
            (time(12, 3), "TIME_OUT_AM"),
            (time(13, 5), "TIME_IN_PM"),
            (time(16, 48), "TIME_OUT_PM"),
        ]
        for teacher in (principal_teacher, adviser_teacher, dept_head_teacher, admin_teacher):
            for att_date in school_days[:10]:
                for ping_time, action_code in ping_slots:
                    ts = timezone.make_aware(datetime.combine(att_date, ping_time))
                    if not FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.filter(faculty=teacher, recorded_at=ts).exists():
                        FacultyAttendanceLog.objects.create(
                            faculty=teacher,
                            log_date=att_date,
                            action=action_code,
                            latitude=8.485812,
                            longitude=124.656715,
                            distance_meters=0.0,
                            is_within_boundary=True,
                            face_verified=True,
                            recorded_at=ts,
                        )
            MonthlyTimeRecord.objects.update_or_create(
                faculty=teacher,
                month=today.month,
                year=today.year,
                defaults={
                    "is_submitted": teacher == adviser_teacher,
                    "submitted_at": now if teacher == adviser_teacher else None,
                    "regular_hours": "7:30 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM",
                    "saturday_hours": "As Required",
                },
            )

        BoundaryAlert.objects.get_or_create(
            faculty=dept_head_teacher,
            incident_date=today - timedelta(days=2),
            defaults={
                "left_at": now - timedelta(days=2),
                "status": "EXCUSED",
                "remarks": "Division Office Official Curriculum Briefing (Excused) — Verified against Division Memorandum No. 2026-114.",
            },
        )

        # 7. Gate Exit Passes
        first_student = seeded_students[0][0]
        ExitPass.objects.update_or_create(
            pass_number="GP-DEMO-2026-001",
            defaults={
                "student": first_student,
                "faculty": None,
                "issued_by": adviser_teacher,
                "pass_type": "Medical / Clinic Clearance",
                "reason": "Scheduled pediatric dental follow-up with guardian escort.",
                "valid_from": now - timedelta(minutes=30),
                "valid_until": now + timedelta(hours=3),
                "status": "ACTIVE",
            },
        )
        ExitPass.objects.update_or_create(
            pass_number="GP-DEMO-2026-002",
            defaults={
                "student": None,
                "faculty": adviser_teacher,
                "issued_by": adviser_teacher,
                "pass_type": "Official Travel Order",
                "reason": "Regional STEM Robotics Coaches Orientation at Division Office.",
                "valid_from": now + timedelta(hours=1),
                "valid_until": now + timedelta(hours=5),
                "status": "PENDING",
            },
        )

        # 8. Notifications, Parent-Teacher Chat & Tamper-Evident Audit Logs
        if not Notification.objects.filter(recipient=parent_user, title__contains="Arrived Safely").exists():
            Notification.objects.create(
                recipient=parent_user,
                student=first_student,
                title=f"{first_student.first_name} Arrived Safely in Class",
                message=f"Signed QR check-in verified for {first_student.full_name} in Mathematics 7 (Room 101).",
                category="ATTENDANCE",
                is_pushed=True,
                is_read=False,
            )
            Notification.objects.create(
                recipient=parent_user,
                student=seeded_students[2][0],
                title="Early-Warning Attendance Follow-Up",
                message="Your child's adviser has requested a brief check-in regarding recent absences.",
                category="CUTTING_ALERT",
                is_pushed=True,
                is_read=False,
            )

        if not ChatMessage.objects.filter(student=first_student, faculty=adviser_teacher).exists():
            ChatMessage.objects.create(
                student=first_student,
                faculty=adviser_teacher,
                sender=teacher_user,
                body="Good morning Ms. Alvarez! Mateo checked in on time for Mathematics 7 and scored 98% on today's quiz.",
                is_read=False,
            )
            ChatMessage.objects.create(
                student=first_student,
                faculty=adviser_teacher,
                sender=parent_user,
                body="Thank you Sir Marco! We really appreciate the real-time arrival notifications on AttendSure.",
                is_read=True,
            )

        if AuditLog.objects.count() < 5:
            for table, action, detail in [
                ("subject_attendance_logs", "CREATE", {"summary": "Batch signed QR check-in verified (Grade 7 - Newton)"}),
                ("deped_reports", "EXPORT", {"report_name": "SF2 Monthly Attendance Register"}),
                ("edfi_export", "EXPORT", {"standard": "Ed-Fi v6.0", "anonymize": True}),
                ("faculty_heartbeats", "VERIFY", {"geofence": "Inside 150m Campus Boundary"}),
            ]:
                AuditLog.objects.create(
                    table_name=table,
                    record_id=1,
                    action=action,
                    new_data=detail,
                    performed_by=admin_user,
                    client_ip="127.0.0.1",
                )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                "AttendSure Competition Demo Dataset seeded successfully!\n"
                "Demo Accounts (password: AttendSure2026!):\n"
                "  - Admin:     demo_admin\n"
                "  - Principal: demo_principal\n"
                "  - Teacher:   demo_teacher\n"
                "  - Parent:    demo_parent\n"
                "1-Click Judge Demo Login is active whenever DEBUG=True or ENABLE_JUDGE_DEMO=True."
            )
        )

from django.db import models, transaction
from django.db.models import UniqueConstraint
from django.utils import timezone

DAY_OF_WEEK_CHOICES = [
    (1, "Monday"),
    (2, "Tuesday"),
    (3, "Wednesday"),
    (4, "Thursday"),
    (5, "Friday"),
    (6, "Saturday"),
    (7, "Sunday"),
]

HOLIDAY_TYPE_CHOICES = [
    ("HOLIDAY", "Holiday"),
    ("SUSPENSION", "Class Suspension"),
    ("EVENT", "School Event"),
]


class SchoolYear(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
    start_date = models.DateField()
    baseline_cutoff_date = models.DateField(db_column="first_friday_june")
    end_date = models.DateField()
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "academic_years"
        ordering = ["-start_date"]

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        with transaction.atomic():
            if self.is_active:
                SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).exclude(pk=self.pk).update(is_active=False)
            super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.code


class AdministrativeDivision(models.Model):
    """3NF Lookup: District -> Division -> Region hierarchy."""

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    region = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    division = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    district = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")

    class Meta:
        db_table = "school_divisions"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["region", "division", "district"], name="uq_region_div_dist")
        ]


class SchoolSettings(models.Model):
    """
    3NF School Profile:
    - Links to SchoolYear instead of duplicating academic_year text.
    - Links to FacultyProfile for Principal details (derives name & title via JOIN).
    - Links to CampusBoundary instead of duplicating latitude/longitude/radius.
    """

    school_id = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    school_name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    division_ref = models.ForeignKey(
        AdministrativeDivision,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="schools",
    )
    active_school_year = models.ForeignKey(
        SchoolYear,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="configured_schools",
    )
    campus_boundary = models.OneToOneField(
        "campus.CampusBoundary",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="school_profile",
    )

    address = models.TextField(blank=True, default="")
    contact_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    email = models.EmailField(blank=True, default="")
    website = models.URLField(max_length=200, blank=True, default="")

    principal_faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_column="principal_faculty_id",
        related_name="headed_schools",
    )
    superintendent_name = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")

    school_logo = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)
    left_logo = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)
    right_logo = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)
    weekend_rest_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "attendsure_school_profile"


class SchoolHoliday(models.Model):
    """1NF & 3NF Compliant: Stores holiday_type atomically instead of '[TYPE] reason' strings."""

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    holiday_date = models.DateField(unique=True, db_index=True)
    holiday_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=HOLIDAY_TYPE_CHOICES, default="HOLIDAY", db_index=True)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "school_holidays"
        ordering = ["holiday_date"]


class Building(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "buildings"
        ordering = ["name"]


class Room(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    building_ref = models.ForeignKey(
        Building, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="rooms"
    )
    capacity = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=45)
    room_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, default="LECTURE")
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "rooms"
        ordering = ["name"]

    @property
    def building(self) -> str:
        return self.building_ref.name if self.building_ref_id and self.building_ref else ""


class GradeLevel(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    stage = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, default="JHS")
    level_order = models.IntegerField(default=1, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "grade_levels"
        ordering = ["level_order"]


class Section(models.Model):
    """3NF Section: Room name comes strictly from Room FK (no duplicate room_number text column)."""

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    school_year = models.ForeignKey(
        SchoolYear, on_delete=models.CASCADE, db_column="academic_year_id", related_name="sections"
    )
    grade_level = models.ForeignKey(GradeLevel, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="sections")
    adviser = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="advising_sections",
    )
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="sections")
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    track_strand = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True, default="")
    capacity = models.IntegerField(default=45)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "sections"
        ordering = ["name"]
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["school_year", "grade_level", "name"], name="uq_section_year_grade_name")
        ]

    @property
    def room_number(self) -> str:
        return self.room.name if self.room_id and self.room else ""


class Subject(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    grade_level = models.ForeignKey(
        GradeLevel, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, related_name="uniform_subjects"
    )
    code = models.CharField(max_length=30, unique=True)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    subject_category = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="Core")
    units = models.IntegerField(default=1)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "subjects"
        ordering = ["code"]


class ClassPeriod(models.Model):
    """
    1NF & 3NF Schedule:
    - Single schedule_code (removed duplicate code).
    - Room resolved via Room FK (removed duplicate room_number).
    - Days stored atomically in ClassPeriodDay (removed multi-valued days_of_week text).
    """

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    schedule_code = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, default="", db_index=True)
    section = models.ForeignKey(Section, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="schedules")
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="schedules")
    faculty = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.FacultyProfile",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_column="faculty_id",
        related_name="assigned_periods",
    )
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name="schedules")
    start_time = models.TimeField(db_index=True)
    end_time = models.TimeField()
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "schedules"
        ordering = ["start_time"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["section", "is_active", "start_time"], name="idx_sched_sec_active_time"),
            models.Index(fields=["faculty", "is_active"], name="idx_sched_fac_active"),
        ]

    @property
    def room_number(self) -> str:
        if self.room_id and self.room:
            return self.room.name
        if self.section_id and self.section and self.section.room_id and self.section.room:
            return self.section.room.name
        return ""

    @property
    def days_of_week(self) -> str:
        day_nums = sorted(d.day_of_week for d in self.days.all())
        if day_nums == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5] or not day_nums:
            return "MON-FRI"
        if day_nums == [1, 3, 5]:
            return "MWF"
        if day_nums == [2, 4]:
            return "TTH"
        names = {1: "MON", 2: "TUE", 3: "WED", 4: "THU", 5: "FRI", 6: "SAT", 7: "SUN"}
        return ",".join(names[n] for n in day_nums if n in names)


class ClassPeriodDay(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    class_period = models.ForeignKey(
        ClassPeriod, on_delete=models.CASCADE, db_column="schedule_id", related_name="days"
    )
    day_of_week = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(choices=DAY_OF_WEEK_CHOICES, default=1)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "schedule_days"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["class_period", "day_of_week"], name="uq_period_day_of_week")
        ]

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\school\selectors.py
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db.models import Count, Q

from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_section_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
from common.media import build_media_url, save_base64_logo_to_disk
from common.permissions import is_school_leader
from .models import ClassPeriod, SchoolSettings, SchoolYear, Section

SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY = "school:metadata:raw"


def format_section_label(grade_name: str, section_name: str) -> str:
    g, s = str(grade_name or "").strip(), str(section_name or "").strip()
    if not g:
        return s
    if not s or g.lower().endswith(s.lower()):
        return g
    return f"{g} - {s}"


def migrate_inline_logos_to_files(school: SchoolSettings) -> SchoolSettings:
    """Replaces raw Base64 logo strings in PostgreSQL with lightweight /media/ paths."""
    updates = {}
    for field_name in ("school_logo", "left_logo", "right_logo"):
        val = getattr(school, field_name, None)
        if val and isinstance(val, str) and (
            val.startswith("data:image") or val.startswith(("/9j/", "iVBORw0KGgo"))
        ):
            disk_path = save_base64_logo_to_disk(val)
            if disk_path:
                updates[field_name] = disk_path
                setattr(school, field_name, disk_path)

    if updates:
        SchoolSettings.objects.filter(pk=school.pk).update(**updates)
        cache.delete(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY)
    return school


def get_school_metadata(request=None, school: SchoolSettings | None = None) -> dict:
    raw = cache.get(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY)
    if raw is None:
        school = school or (
            SchoolSettings.objects.select_related(
                "division_ref",
                "active_school_year",
                "campus_boundary",
                "principal_faculty",
                "principal_faculty__position_ref",
                "principal_faculty__department_ref",
            ).first()
        )
        if not school:
            active_sy = SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
            return {
                "school_id": "",
                "school_name": "",
                "region": "",
                "division": "",
                "district": "",
                "academic_year": active_sy.code if active_sy else "",
                "school_address": "",
                "school_contact": "",
                "school_email": "",
                "website": "",
                "school_head": "",
                "principal_name": "",
                "principal_title": "",
                "superintendent_name": "",
                "left_logo": None,
                "right_logo": None,
                "school_logo": None,
                "latitude": 8.4858,
                "longitude": 124.6567,
                "geofence_radius_meters": 150,
                "is_configured": False,
            }

        school = migrate_inline_logos_to_files(school)
        div = school.division_ref
        sy = school.active_school_year or SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
        principal = school.principal_faculty
        boundary = school.campus_boundary
        head_name = principal.full_name if principal else ""
        head_title = (principal.position if principal and principal.position else "School Principal")

        raw = {
            "school_id": school.school_id,
            "school_name": school.school_name,
            "region": div.region if div else "",
            "division": div.division if div else "",
            "district": div.district if div else "",
            "academic_year": sy.code if sy else "",
            "school_address": school.address,
            "school_contact": school.contact_number,
            "school_email": school.email,
            "website": school.website,
            "school_head": head_name.upper(),
            "principal_name": head_name,
            "principal_title": head_title,
            "superintendent_name": school.superintendent_name,
            "left_logo_path": school.left_logo,
            "right_logo_path": school.right_logo,
            "school_logo_path": school.school_logo,
            "latitude": float(boundary.center_lat) if boundary else 8.4858,
            "longitude": float(boundary.center_lng) if boundary else 124.6567,
            "geofence_radius_meters": int(boundary.radius_meters) if boundary else 150,
            "weekend_rest_enabled": bool(school.weekend_rest_enabled),
            "is_configured": bool(school.school_name and school.school_id),
        }
        cache.set(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY, raw, timeout=300)

    return {
        "school_id": raw["school_id"],
        "school_name": raw["school_name"],
        "region": raw["region"],
        "division": raw["division"],
        "district": raw["district"],
        "academic_year": raw["academic_year"],
        "school_address": raw["school_address"],
        "school_contact": raw["school_contact"],
        "school_email": raw["school_email"],
        "website": raw["website"],
        "school_head": raw["school_head"],
        "principal_name": raw["principal_name"],
        "principal_title": raw["principal_title"],
        "superintendent_name": raw["superintendent_name"],
        "left_logo": build_media_url(raw["left_logo_path"], request),
        "right_logo": build_media_url(raw["right_logo_path"], request),
        "school_logo": build_media_url(raw["school_logo_path"], request),
        "latitude": raw["latitude"],
        "longitude": raw["longitude"],
        "geofence_radius_meters": raw["geofence_radius_meters"],
        "weekend_rest_enabled": raw["weekend_rest_enabled"],
        "is_configured": raw["is_configured"],
    }


def get_sections_for_user(user, *, include_inactive: bool = False):
    qs = Section.objects.all()
    if not (include_inactive and is_school_leader(user)):
        qs = qs.filter(is_active=True)
    qs = (
        qs.select_related("grade_level", "school_year", "adviser", "room", "room__building_ref")
        .annotate(
            enrolled_count=Count(
                "enrollments",
                filter=Q(
                    enrollments__status__in=["ENROLLED", "TRANSFERRED_IN"],
                    enrollments__student__is_active=True,
                ),
                distinct=True,
            )
        )
        .order_by("grade_level__level_order", "name")
    )
    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        qs = qs.filter(id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(faculty)) if faculty else qs.none()
    return qs


def get_class_periods_for_user(user, section_id: int | None = None):
    qs = (
        ClassPeriod.objects.select_related(
            "section",
            "section__grade_level",
            "section__room",
            "subject",
            "faculty",
            "room",
        )
        .prefetch_related("days")
        .order_by("start_time")
    )
    if section_id:
        qs = qs.filter(section_id=section_id)
    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if faculty:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(faculty=faculty) | Q(section_id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(faculty))
            ).distinct()
        else:
            qs = qs.none()
    return qs

import math
from django.db import transaction
from rest_framework import serializers

class RoomSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    building = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")

    class Meta:
        model = Room
        fields = ["id", "name", "building", "capacity", "room_type", "is_active"]

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        b_name = str(validated_data.pop("building", "") or "").strip()[:100]
        if b_name:
            b_obj, _ = Building.objects.get_or_create(name=b_name)
            validated_data["building_ref"] = b_obj
        return super().create(validated_data)

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        if "building" in validated_data:
            b_name = str(validated_data.pop("building") or "").strip()[:100]
            instance.building_ref = Building.objects.get_or_create(name=b_name)[0] if b_name else None
        return super().update(instance, validated_data)

    class Meta:
        model = Subject
        fields = ["id", "code", "title", "grade_level", "subject_category", "tier", "subject_type", "units"]


class SchoolHolidaySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    date = serializers.DateField(source="holiday_date")
    type = serializers.ChoiceField(
        source="holiday_type",
        choices=["HOLIDAY", "SUSPENSION", "EVENT"],
        required=False,
        default="HOLIDAY",
    )
    reason = serializers.CharField(source="title", required=False)

    class Meta:
        model = SchoolHoliday
        fields = ["id", "date", "type", "title", "reason", "created_at"]
        extra_kwargs = {"title": {"required": False}}

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        if not mutable.get("reason") and mutable.get("title"):
            mutable["reason"] = mutable["title"]
        if "type" in mutable and mutable["type"]:
            mutable["type"] = str(mutable["type"]).strip().upper()
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if not self.partial and not str(attrs.get("title") or "").strip():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"title": "Holiday or event title is required."})
        return attrs

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        data = super().to_representation(instance)
        data["type"] = instance.holiday_type
        data["reason"] = instance.title
        data["affects_all_grades"] = True
        return data


class SchoolSettingsSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    school_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    left_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    right_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)

    region = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)
    division = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)
    district = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)
    academic_year = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)
    latitude = serializers.FloatField(required=False, write_only=True)
    longitude = serializers.FloatField(required=False, write_only=True)
    geofence_radius_meters = serializers.IntegerField(required=False, write_only=True)

    principal_faculty = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(
        queryset=FacultyProfile.objects.all(),
        required=False,
        allow_null=True,
    )

    class Meta:
        model = SchoolSettings
        fields = [
            "id",
            "school_id",
            "school_name",
            "region",
            "division",
            "district",
            "academic_year",
            "address",
            "contact_number",
            "email",
            "website",
            "principal_faculty",
            "superintendent_name",
            "school_logo",
            "left_logo",
            "right_logo",
            "latitude",
            "longitude",
            "geofence_radius_meters",
            "weekend_rest_enabled",
            "updated_at",
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            "school_id": {"required": False, "allow_blank": True},
            "school_name": {"required": False, "allow_blank": True},
        }

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        alias_map = {
            "school_address": "address",
            "school_contact": "contact_number",
            "school_email": "email",
            "radius_meters": "geofence_radius_meters",
            "geofence_radius": "geofence_radius_meters",
            "emblem": "school_logo",
            "main_logo": "school_logo",
            "principal_teacher": "principal_faculty",
        }
        for src_key, dest_key in alias_map.items():
            if src_key in mutable and dest_key not in mutable:
                mutable[dest_key] = mutable[src_key]

        if "principal_faculty" in mutable and str(mutable["principal_faculty"]).strip().lower() in (
            "",
            "null",
            "none",
            "undefined",
        ):
            mutable["principal_faculty"] = None

        if "website" in mutable:
            raw_web = str(mutable.get("website") or "").strip()
            mutable["website"] = (
                f"https://{raw_web}"
                if (raw_web and not raw_web.startswith(("http://", "https://")))
                else raw_web
            )

        for coord_key in ("latitude", "longitude"):
            if coord_key in mutable:
                raw_coord = mutable[coord_key]
                if raw_coord in (None, "", "null", "undefined"):
                    mutable.pop(coord_key, None)
                else:
                    try:
                        parsed = float(raw_coord)
                        if not math.isfinite(parsed):
                            raise ValueError("non-finite")
                        mutable[coord_key] = round(parsed, 7)
                    except (TypeError, ValueError):
                        raise serializers.ValidationError({coord_key: f"Invalid {coord_key} value."})

        if "geofence_radius_meters" in mutable:
            raw_rad = mutable["geofence_radius_meters"]
            if raw_rad in (None, "", "null", "undefined"):
                mutable.pop("geofence_radius_meters", None)
            else:
                try:
                    parsed_rad = float(raw_rad)
                    if not math.isfinite(parsed_rad):
                        raise ValueError("non-finite")
                    mutable["geofence_radius_meters"] = max(20, min(5000, int(parsed_rad)))
                except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(
                        {"geofence_radius_meters": "Invalid radius in meters."}
                    )

        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def _apply_3nf_relations(self, validated_data: dict, instance: SchoolSettings | None = None):
        has_div = any(k in validated_data for k in ("region", "division", "district"))
        curr_div = instance.division_ref if instance else None
        region = validated_data.pop("region", curr_div.region if curr_div else "")
        division = validated_data.pop("division", curr_div.division if curr_div else "")
        district = validated_data.pop("district", curr_div.district if curr_div else "")
        if has_div and (region or division or district):
            div_obj, _ = AdministrativeDivision.objects.get_or_create(
                region=str(region).strip()[:100],
                division=str(division).strip()[:100],
                district=str(district).strip()[:100],
            )
            validated_data["division_ref"] = div_obj

        sy_code = str(validated_data.pop("academic_year", "") or "").strip()
        if sy_code:
            sy_obj = SchoolYear.objects.filter(code__iexact=sy_code).first()
            if sy_obj:
                validated_data["active_school_year"] = sy_obj
        elif not instance and "active_school_year" not in validated_data:
            active_sy = SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
            if active_sy:
                validated_data["active_school_year"] = active_sy

        has_gps = any(k in validated_data for k in ("latitude", "longitude", "geofence_radius_meters"))
        curr_b = (instance.campus_boundary if instance and instance.campus_boundary else None) or (
            CampusBoundary.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or CampusBoundary.objects.first()
        )
        lat = validated_data.pop("latitude", curr_b.center_lat if curr_b else 8.4858)
        lng = validated_data.pop("longitude", curr_b.center_lng if curr_b else 124.6567)
        rad = validated_data.pop("geofence_radius_meters", curr_b.radius_meters if curr_b else 150)

        if has_gps or not curr_b:
            b_name = str(
                validated_data.get("school_name")
                or (instance.school_name if instance else "Main Campus")
            )[:120]
            if curr_b:
                curr_b.name = b_name
                curr_b.center_lat = float(lat)
                curr_b.center_lng = float(lng)
                curr_b.radius_meters = int(rad)
                curr_b.is_active = True
                curr_b.save()
                validated_data["campus_boundary"] = curr_b
            else:
                validated_data["campus_boundary"] = CampusBoundary.objects.create(
                    name=b_name,
                    center_lat=float(lat),
                    center_lng=float(lng),
                    radius_meters=int(rad),
                    is_active=True,
                )

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        if not validated_data.get("school_id"):
            validated_data["school_id"] = "304100"
        self._apply_3nf_relations(validated_data, instance=None)
        return super().create(validated_data)

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        self._apply_3nf_relations(validated_data, instance=instance)
        return super().update(instance, validated_data)

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        data = super().to_representation(instance)
        div = instance.division_ref
        sy = instance.active_school_year or SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
        b = instance.campus_boundary or CampusBoundary.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
        p = instance.principal_faculty

        data["region"] = div.region if div else ""
        data["division"] = div.division if div else ""
        data["district"] = div.district if div else ""
        data["academic_year"] = sy.code if sy else ""
        data["latitude"] = float(b.center_lat) if b else 8.4858
        data["longitude"] = float(b.center_lng) if b else 124.6567
        data["geofence_radius_meters"] = int(b.radius_meters) if b else 150
        data["principal_teacher"] = p.id if p else None
        data["principal_name"] = p.full_name if p else ""
        data["principal_title"] = p.position if (p and p.position) else "School Principal"
        data["principal_employee_id"] = p.employee_id if p else ""
        data["principal_department"] = p.department if p else "Administration"
        data["principal_email"] = p.email if p else ""
        data["principal_contact_number"] = p.contact_number if p else ""
        data["principal_photo_url"] = (
            build_media_url(p.photo, self.context.get("request")) if (p and p.photo) else None
        )
        return data


class SectionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):

        ]
        extra_kwargs = {"school_year": {"required": False}}
        validators = []

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        if "adviser" not in mutable and "adviser_id" in mutable:
            mutable["adviser"] = mutable.get("adviser_id")
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def get_adviser_photo_url(self, obj):
        if obj.adviser and obj.adviser.photo:
            return build_media_url(obj.adviser.photo, self.context.get("request"))
        return None

    def get_display_label(self, obj) -> str:
        return format_section_label(obj.grade_level.name if obj.grade_level else "", obj.name)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        sy = attrs.get("school_year") or (self.instance.school_year if self.instance else None)
        if not sy:
            sy = SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or SchoolYear.objects.first()
            if not sy:
                raise serializers.ValidationError(
                    {"school_year": "Create an active School Year before adding sections."}
                )
            attrs["school_year"] = sy

        grade_level = attrs.get("grade_level") or (self.instance.grade_level if self.instance else None)
        name = attrs.get("name") if "name" in attrs else (self.instance.name if self.instance else None)
        if sy and grade_level and name:
            dup_qs = Section.objects.filter(school_year=sy, grade_level=grade_level, name=name)
            if self.instance:
                dup_qs = dup_qs.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)
            if dup_qs.exists():
                raise serializers.ValidationError(
                    {"name": "A section with this name already exists for this grade level and school year."}
                )
        return attrs

    def _resolve_room(self, validated_data: dict):
        room_num = str(validated_data.pop("room_number", "") or "").strip()[:100]
        if room_num and not validated_data.get("room"):
            room_obj, _ = Room.objects.get_or_create(name=room_num)
            validated_data["room"] = room_obj

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        self._resolve_room(validated_data)
        section = super().create(validated_data)
        sync_uniform_subjects_for_section(section)
        return section

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        self._resolve_room(validated_data)
        return super().update(instance, validated_data)


class ClassPeriodSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    section_name = serializers.CharField(source="section.name", read_only=True)
    grade_level_name = serializers.CharField(source="section.grade_level.name", read_only=True, default="")
    subject_code = serializers.CharField(source="subject.code", read_only=True)
    subject_title = serializers.CharField(source="subject.title", read_only=True)
    room_name = serializers.CharField(source="room.name", read_only=True, default="")
    room_number = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    days_of_week = serializers.CharField(required=False, default="MON-FRI")
    faculty_id = serializers.IntegerField(read_only=True)
    faculty_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    faculty_employee_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    faculty_photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    teacher = serializers.IntegerField(source="faculty_id", read_only=True)
    teacher_id = serializers.IntegerField(source="faculty_id", read_only=True)
    teacher_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    teacher_employee_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    teacher_photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = ClassPeriod
        fields = [
            "id",
            "schedule_code",
            "section",
            "section_name",
            "grade_level_name",
            "subject",
            "subject_code",
            "subject_title",
            "faculty",
            "faculty_id",
            "faculty_name",
            "faculty_employee_id",
            "faculty_photo_url",
            "teacher",
            "teacher_id",
            "teacher_name",
            "teacher_employee_id",
            "teacher_photo_url",
            "room",
            "room_name",
            "room_number",
            "days_of_week",
            "start_time",
            "end_time",
            "is_active",
        ]

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        if "faculty" not in mutable:
            for alias in ("faculty_id", "teacher", "teacher_id"):
                if alias in mutable:
                    mutable["faculty"] = mutable[alias]
                    break
        if "section" not in mutable and "section_id" in mutable:
            mutable["section"] = mutable["section_id"]
        if "subject" not in mutable and "subject_id" in mutable:
            mutable["subject"] = mutable["subject_id"]
        if "room" not in mutable and "room_id" in mutable:
            mutable["room"] = mutable["room_id"]
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def get_faculty_name(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.faculty.full_name if obj.faculty else "Unassigned"

    def get_teacher_name(self, obj) -> str:
        return self.get_faculty_name(obj)

    def get_faculty_employee_id(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.faculty.employee_id if obj.faculty else ""

    def get_teacher_employee_id(self, obj) -> str:
        return self.get_faculty_employee_id(obj)

    def get_faculty_photo_url(self, obj):
        if obj.faculty and obj.faculty.photo:
            return build_media_url(obj.faculty.photo, self.context.get("request"))
        return None

    def get_teacher_photo_url(self, obj):
        return self.get_faculty_photo_url(obj)

    def _resolve_room(self, validated_data: dict):
        room_num = str(validated_data.pop("room_number", "") or "").strip()[:100]
        if room_num and not validated_data.get("room"):
            room_obj, _ = Room.objects.get_or_create(name=room_num)
            validated_data["room"] = room_obj

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        days_str = validated_data.pop("days_of_week", "MON-FRI")
        self._resolve_room(validated_data)
        period = super().create(validated_data)
        sync_period_days(period, days_str)
        return period

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        days_str = validated_data.pop("days_of_week", None)
        self._resolve_room(validated_data)
        period = super().update(instance, validated_data)
        if days_str is not None:
            sync_period_days(period, days_str)
        return period

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\school\services.py
from datetime import time
from .models import ClassPeriod, ClassPeriodDay, Section, Subject


def sync_uniform_subjects_for_section(section: Section) -> None:
    if not section or not section.grade_level_id:
        return
    subjects = list(Subject.objects.filter(grade_level_id=section.grade_level_id).order_by("id"))
    existing_ids = set(ClassPeriod.objects.filter(section=section).values_list("subject_id", flat=True))
    created_periods = []
    for idx, subj in enumerate(subjects):
        if subj.id in existing_ids:
            continue
        start_hr = min(7 + idx, 16)
        period = ClassPeriod.objects.create(
            schedule_code=f"{section.id}-{subj.code}"[:64],
            section=section,
            subject=subj,
            start_time=time(start_hr, 30),
            end_time=time(min(start_hr + 1, 17), 30),
            room=section.room,
            is_active=True,
        )
        created_periods.append(period)

    for period in created_periods:
        sync_period_days(period, "MON-FRI")


def sync_period_days(period: ClassPeriod, days_text: str) -> None:
    presets = {"MON-FRI": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], "MWF": [1, 3, 5], "TTH": [2, 4], "SAT": [6]}
    lookup = {"MON": 1, "TUE": 2, "WED": 3, "THU": 4, "FRI": 5, "SAT": 6, "SUN": 7}
    clean = str(days_text or "MON-FRI").upper().strip()
    day_nums = (
        presets.get(clean)
        or [lookup[p.strip()] for p in clean.split(",") if p.strip() in lookup]
        or [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    )

    ClassPeriodDay.objects.filter(class_period=period).exclude(day_of_week__in=day_nums).delete()
    existing = set(ClassPeriodDay.objects.filter(class_period=period).values_list("day_of_week", flat=True))
    ClassPeriodDay.objects.bulk_create(
        [ClassPeriodDay(class_period=period, day_of_week=d) for d in day_nums if d not in existing]
    )

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\school\views.py
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db import transaction
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from common.audit import AuditedModelViewSet, record_audit_log
from common.permissions import IsLeadershipOrReadOnly, is_school_leader
from .models import GradeLevel, Room, SchoolHoliday, SchoolSettings, SchoolYear, Subject
from .selectors import (
    SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY,
    get_class_periods_for_user,
    get_school_metadata,
    get_sections_for_user,
    migrate_inline_logos_to_files,
)

WEEKEND_REST_CACHE_KEY = "school:calendar:weekend_rest_enabled"


def _wants_fresh_cache(request) -> bool:
    flag = str(request.query_params.get("nocache", "")).strip().lower()
    return flag in ("1", "true", "yes")


class PublicSchoolBrandingView(APIView):
    """Returns non-sensitive public school letterhead info for Web & Mobile Sign-In screens."""

    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]

    def get(self, request):
        if _wants_fresh_cache(request):
            cache.delete(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY)

        school = SchoolSettings.objects.select_related(
            "division_ref",
            "active_school_year",
            "campus_boundary",
            "principal_faculty",
            "principal_faculty__position_ref",
        ).first()
        meta = get_school_metadata(request, school=school)
        sid = str(meta.get("school_id") or "").strip()

        return Response(
            {
                "school_id": sid,
                "deped_school_id": sid,
                "school_name": meta.get("school_name", ""),
                "short_name": "",
                "region": meta.get("region", ""),
                "division": meta.get("division", ""),
                "district": meta.get("district", ""),
                "address": meta.get("school_address", ""),
                "contact_number": meta.get("school_contact", ""),
                "email": meta.get("school_email", ""),
                "principal_name": meta.get("principal_name", ""),
                "principal_title": meta.get("principal_title", ""),
                "academic_year": meta.get("academic_year", ""),
                "school_logo": meta.get("school_logo"),
                "left_logo": meta.get("left_logo"),
                "right_logo": meta.get("right_logo"),
                "is_configured": bool(meta.get("is_configured", False)),
            }
        )


class SchoolSettingsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrReadOnly]

    def get(self, request):
        if _wants_fresh_cache(request):
            cache.delete(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY)

        school = SchoolSettings.objects.select_related(
            "division_ref",
            "active_school_year",
            "campus_boundary",
            "principal_faculty",
            "principal_faculty__position_ref",
            "principal_faculty__department_ref",
        ).first()
        if not school:
            meta = get_school_metadata(request)
            meta["deped_school_id"] = meta.get("school_id", "")
            return Response(meta)

        school = migrate_inline_logos_to_files(school)
        serialized = dict(SchoolSettingsSerializer(school, context={"request": request}).data)
        meta = get_school_metadata(request, school=school)
        merged = {**meta, **{k: v for k, v in serialized.items() if v not in (None, "")}}
        merged["deped_school_id"] = merged.get("school_id", "")
        for logo_key in ("school_logo", "left_logo", "right_logo"):
            if meta.get(logo_key):
                merged[logo_key] = meta[logo_key]
        return Response(merged)

    @transaction.atomic
    def put(self, request):
        school = SchoolSettings.objects.select_related(
            "division_ref", "active_school_year", "campus_boundary", "principal_faculty"
        ).first()
        raw_payload = (
            {k: request.data.get(k) for k in request.data.keys()}
            if hasattr(request.data, "keys")
            else dict(request.data)
        )
        if not raw_payload.get("school_id") and raw_payload.get("deped_school_id"):
            raw_payload["school_id"] = str(raw_payload["deped_school_id"]).strip()[:50]

        if school:
            serializer = SchoolSettingsSerializer(
                school, data=raw_payload, partial=True, context={"request": request}
            )
        else:
            if not str(raw_payload.get("school_name") or "").strip():
                return Response(
                    {"error": "School name is required."},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )
            serializer = SchoolSettingsSerializer(data=raw_payload, context={"request": request})

        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        saved = migrate_inline_logos_to_files(serializer.save())
        cache.delete_many([SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY, "reports:system_status"])

        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name=saved._meta.db_table,
            record_id=saved.pk,
            action="UPDATE",
            changed_fields=list(raw_payload.keys()),
        )
        return self.get(request)

    def post(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    def delete(self, request):
        school = SchoolSettings.objects.first()
        field = str(request.query_params.get("field") or request.data.get("field") or "").strip()
        if school and field in ("school_logo", "left_logo", "right_logo"):
            setattr(school, field, None)
            school.save(update_fields=[field, "updated_at"])
            cache.delete_many([SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY, "reports:system_status"])
            record_audit_log(
                request=request,
                table_name=school._meta.db_table,
                record_id=school.pk,
                action="DELETE",
                changed_fields=[field],
            )
            return self.get(request)
        return Response(
            {"error": "Specify school_logo, left_logo, or right_logo."},
            status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
        )


class SchoolHolidayViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    queryset = SchoolHoliday.objects.order_by("holiday_date")
    serializer_class = SchoolHolidaySerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrReadOnly]

    def _get_weekend_rest(self) -> bool:
        cached = cache.get(WEEKEND_REST_CACHE_KEY)
        if cached is not None:
            return bool(cached)
        school = SchoolSettings.objects.only("weekend_rest_enabled").first()
        val = bool(school.weekend_rest_enabled) if school else True
        cache.set(WEEKEND_REST_CACHE_KEY, val, timeout=300)
        return val

    def list(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        response = super().list(request, *args, **kwargs)
        if isinstance(response.data, dict):
            response.data["weekend_rest_enabled"] = self._get_weekend_rest()
            if "results" in response.data and "exceptions" not in response.data:
                response.data["exceptions"] = response.data["results"]
        return response

    def patch(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        if "pk" not in kwargs:
            return self.partial_update_settings(request)
        return super().partial_update(request, *args, **kwargs)

    def partial_update_settings(self, request):
        if not is_school_leader(request.user):
            return Response(
                {"error": "School leadership access required."},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )
        val = request.data.get("weekend_rest_enabled")
        if val is not None:
            enabled = (
                bool(val)
                if isinstance(val, bool)
                else str(val).strip().lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
            )
            SchoolSettings.objects.all().update(weekend_rest_enabled=enabled)
            cache.set(WEEKEND_REST_CACHE_KEY, enabled, timeout=300)
            cache.delete(SCHOOL_META_CACHE_KEY)
            record_audit_log(
                request=request,
                table_name=SchoolHoliday._meta.db_table,
                record_id=0,
                action="UPDATE",
                new_data={"weekend_rest_enabled": enabled},
            )
            return Response({"ok": True, "weekend_rest_enabled": enabled})
        return Response({"ok": True, "weekend_rest_enabled": self._get_weekend_rest()})

from datetime import datetime
import secrets
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import UniqueConstraint
from django.utils import timezone
from common.phone import normalize_ph_phone

SEX_CHOICES = [("Male", "Male"), ("Female", "Female")]
ENROLLMENT_STATUS_CHOICES = [
    ("ENROLLED", "Enrolled"),
    ("DROPPED", "Dropped Out"),
    ("TRANSFERRED_IN", "Transferred In"),
    ("TRANSFERRED_OUT", "Transferred Out"),
]
ENROLLMENT_TYPE_CHOICES = [
    ("REGULAR", "Regular"),
    ("LATE_ENROLLEE", "Late Enrollee"),
    ("TRANSFERRED_IN", "Transferred In"),
    ("RETURNING", "Returning"),
    ("REPEATER", "Repeater"),
]


class AddressLocation(models.Model):
    """3NF Geographic Lookup: Barangay -> Municipality/City -> Province."""

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    barangay = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    municipality_city = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    province = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")

    class Meta:
        db_table = "address_locations"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["barangay", "municipality_city", "province"],
                name="uq_barangay_city_province",
            )
        ]


class Student(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    lrn = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, db_index=True)
    first_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    middle_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    last_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    suffix = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, default="")
    sex = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=SEX_CHOICES)
    birthdate = models.DateField()

    mother_tongue = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    ethnic_group = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")
    religion = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")

    house_street_sitio = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")
    location = models.ForeignKey(
        AddressLocation,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="students",
    )

    father_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default="")
    mother_maiden_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default="")
    guardian_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default="")
    guardian_relationship = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    parent_contact = models.CharField(max_length=50, db_index=True)
    parent_user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="children",
    )

    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to="students/", null=True, blank=True)
    photo_updated_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    qr_token = models.CharField(max_length=128, unique=True, db_index=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, db_index=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="created_students",
    )
    updated_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="updated_students",
    )

    class Meta:
        db_table = "students"
        ordering = ["last_name", "first_name"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["is_active", "last_name", "first_name"], name="idx_stu_active_name"),
        ]

    @property
    def barangay(self) -> str:
        return self.location.barangay if self.location_id and self.location else ""

    @property
    def municipality_city(self) -> str:
        return self.location.municipality_city if self.location_id and self.location else ""

    @property
    def province(self) -> str:
        return self.location.province if self.location_id and self.location else ""

    def set_address_location(self, barangay: str = "", municipality_city: str = "", province: str = "") -> None:
        b = str(barangay or "").strip()[:100]
        m = str(municipality_city or "").strip()[:100]
        p = str(province or "").strip()[:100]
        if b or m or p:
            loc, _ = AddressLocation.objects.get_or_create(
                barangay=b, municipality_city=m, province=p
            )
            self.location = loc
        else:
            self.location = None

    @property
    def full_name(self) -> str:
        mid = f" {self.middle_name[:1]}." if self.middle_name else ""
        suf = f" {self.suffix}" if self.suffix else ""
        return f"{self.last_name}, {self.first_name}{mid}{suf}".strip()

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.parent_contact:
            self.parent_contact = normalize_ph_phone(self.parent_contact)[:50]
        if not self.qr_token:
            self.qr_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
        if not self.parent_user_id and self.parent_contact:
            from apps.accounts.models import UserProfile

            matched = (
                UserProfile.objects.filter(role="PARENT", phone_number=self.parent_contact)
                .select_related("user")
                .first()
            )
            if matched:
                self.parent_user = matched.user
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.full_name} ({self.lrn})"


class Enrollment(models.Model):
    """
    3NF Enrollment:
    - Removed transitive school_year column because Enrollment -> Section -> SchoolYear.
    - Accessed in zero extra queries via select_related('section__school_year').
    """

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="enrollments")
    section = models.ForeignKey("school.Section", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="enrollments")
    enrollment_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    enrollment_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ENROLLMENT_TYPE_CHOICES, default="REGULAR")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ENROLLMENT_STATUS_CHOICES, default="ENROLLED", db_index=True)
    status_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    status_reason_code = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    transferred_school = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="")
    is_cct_recipient = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    cct_id_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="")
    disability_detail = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True, default="")
    accelerated_detail = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True, default="")
    remarks = models.TextField(blank=True, default="")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "enrollments"
        constraints = [
            UniqueConstraint(fields=["student", "section"], name="uq_enrollment_student_section")
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["section", "status"], name="idx_enr_section_status"),
            models.Index(fields=["student", "status"], name="idx_enr_student_status"),
        ]

    @property
    def school_year(self):
        return self.section.school_year if self.section_id and self.section else None

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if isinstance(self.enrollment_date, datetime):
            self.enrollment_date = (
                timezone.localtime(self.enrollment_date).date()
                if timezone.is_aware(self.enrollment_date)
                else self.enrollment_date.date()
            )
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

# backend/apps/students/selectors.py
from django.db.models import Prefetch, Q
from django.utils import timezone

from common.media import build_media_url
from common.permissions import get_user_role, is_school_leader
from common.qr_signer import sign_student_qr
from apps.accounts.selectors import get_faculty_student_ids, resolve_faculty_for_user
from apps.attendance.models import ClassAttendance, DailyAttendance, ExitPass
from apps.messaging.models import Notification
from apps.school.selectors import format_section_label
from .models import Enrollment, Student


def get_students_for_user(*, user, search: str = "", section_id: int | None = None):
    role = get_user_role(user)
    active_enrollments = Prefetch(
        "enrollments",
        queryset=Enrollment.objects.select_related(
            "section",
            "section__grade_level",
            "section__school_year",
            "section__room",
        ).order_by("-section__school_year__is_active", "-id"),
    )

    qs = (
        Student.objects.select_related("location", "parent_user")
        .prefetch_related(active_enrollments)
        .order_by("last_name", "first_name")
    )

    if search:
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(lrn__icontains=search)
            | Q(first_name__icontains=search)
            | Q(last_name__icontains=search)
        )
    if section_id:
        qs = qs.filter(enrollments__section_id=section_id, enrollments__status="ENROLLED")
    if role == "PARENT":
        return qs.filter(parent_user=user, is_active=True).distinct()
    if not is_school_leader(user):
        faculty_member = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        qs = qs.filter(id__in=get_faculty_student_ids(faculty_member)) if faculty_member else qs.none()
    return qs.distinct()


def get_signed_qr_cards(*, section_id: int | None = None, request=None) -> list[dict]:
    enr_qs = Enrollment.objects.filter(status="ENROLLED", student__is_active=True).select_related(
        "student", "section", "section__grade_level"
    )
    if section_id:
        enr_qs = enr_qs.filter(section_id=section_id)

    cards = []
    for enr in enr_qs.order_by("section__grade_level__level_order", "section__name", "student__last_name")[:500]:
        st = enr.student
        sec = enr.section
        cards.append(
            {
                "id": st.id,
                "student_id": st.id,
                "lrn": st.lrn,
                "full_name": st.full_name,
                "sex": st.sex,
                "section_id": sec.id if sec else None,
                "section_name": sec.name if sec else "",
                "grade_level": sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else "",
                "section_label": format_section_label(
                    sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else "",
                    sec.name if sec else "",
                ),
                "photo_url": build_media_url(st.photo, request),
                "parent_contact": st.parent_contact,
                "qr_token": st.qr_token,
                "qr_payload": sign_student_qr(st.lrn, st.qr_token),
            }
        )
    return cards


def get_parent_portal_data(*, user, request=None) -> dict:
    today = timezone.localdate()
    children_qs = (
        Student.objects.filter(parent_user=user, is_active=True)
        .select_related("location")
        .prefetch_related(
            Prefetch(
                "enrollments",
                queryset=Enrollment.objects.filter(status="ENROLLED")
                .select_related("section", "section__grade_level", "section__adviser")
                .order_by("-id"),
            )
        )
        .order_by("first_name")
    )
    children = list(children_qs)
    child_ids = [c.id for c in children]

    daily_map = dict(
        DailyAttendance.objects.filter(student_id__in=child_ids, attendance_date=today).values_list(
            "student_id", "status"
        )
    )
    recent_scans_by_student: dict[int, list[dict]] = {}
    for log in (
        ClassAttendance.objects.filter(student_id__in=child_ids)
        .select_related("class_period__subject")
        .order_by("-scanned_at")[:60]
    ):
        bucket = recent_scans_by_student.setdefault(log.student_id, [])
        if len(bucket) < 10:
            bucket.append(
                {
                    "id": log.id,
                    "date": str(log.attendance_date),
                    "time": timezone.localtime(log.scanned_at).strftime("%I:%M %p"),
                    "subject": log.class_period.subject.title
                    if (log.class_period and log.class_period.subject)
                    else "Class",
                    "status": log.status,
                }
            )

    active_passes_by_student: dict[int, list[dict]] = {}
    for ep in ExitPass.objects.filter(student_id__in=child_ids).order_by("-created_at")[:30]:
        active_passes_by_student.setdefault(ep.student_id, []).append(
            {
                "id": ep.id,
                "pass_number": ep.pass_number,
                "pass_type": ep.pass_type,
                "reason": ep.reason,
                "status": ep.status,
                "valid_until": timezone.localtime(ep.valid_until).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p"),
            }
        )

    children_payload = []
    for child in children:
        enrs = list(child.enrollments.all())
        enr = enrs[0] if enrs else None
        sec = enr.section if enr else None
        children_payload.append(
            {
                "id": child.id,
                "lrn": child.lrn,
                "full_name": child.full_name,
                "first_name": child.first_name,
                "sex": child.sex,
                "photo_url": build_media_url(child.photo, request),
                "section": format_section_label(
                    sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else "",
                    sec.name if sec else "",
                ),
                "adviser_name": sec.adviser.full_name if (sec and sec.adviser) else "Unassigned",
                "adviser_id": sec.adviser_id if sec else None,
                "today_status": daily_map.get(child.id, "UNSCANNED"),
                "qr_payload": sign_student_qr(child.lrn, child.qr_token),
                "recent_attendance": recent_scans_by_student.get(child.id, []),
                "exit_passes": active_passes_by_student.get(child.id, []),
            }
        )

    notifications = [
        {
            "id": n.id,
            "title": n.title,
            "message": n.message,
            "category": n.category,
            "is_read": n.is_read,
            "created_at": timezone.localtime(n.created_at).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p"),
        }
        for n in Notification.objects.filter(recipient=user).order_by("-created_at")[:40]
    ]

    return {
        "children": children_payload,
        "students": children_payload,
        "notifications": notifications,
        "unread_notifications": sum(1 for n in notifications if not n["is_read"]),
    }
