# backend/apps/students/serializers.py
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.school.models import Section
from common.media import FlexibleImageField, build_media_url
from common.qr_signer import sign_student_qr
from .models import Enrollment, Student
from .services import sync_student_enrollment


class StudentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    full_name = serializers.CharField(read_only=True)
    barangay = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    municipality_city = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    province = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
    photo = FlexibleImageField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    remove_photo = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, write_only=True, default=False)
    qr_payload = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    section_id = serializers.IntegerField(required=False, allow_null=True, write_only=True)
    current_section = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    current_section_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Student
        fields = [
            "id",
            "lrn",
            "first_name",
            "middle_name",
            "last_name",
            "suffix",
            "full_name",
            "sex",
            "birthdate",
            "mother_tongue",
            "ethnic_group",
            "religion",
            "house_street_sitio",
            "barangay",
            "municipality_city",
            "province",
            "father_name",
            "mother_maiden_name",
            "guardian_name",
            "guardian_relationship",
            "parent_contact",
            "photo",
            "photo_url",
            "remove_photo",
            "qr_token",
            "qr_payload",
            "is_active",
            "current_section",
            "current_section_id",
            "section_id",
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["id", "qr_token"]

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = {k: data.get(k) for k in data.keys()} if hasattr(data, "keys") else dict(data)
        if "sex" in mutable and mutable["sex"] is not None:
            raw_sex = str(mutable["sex"]).strip().upper()
            if raw_sex in ("M", "MALE"):
                mutable["sex"] = "Male"
            elif raw_sex in ("F", "FEMALE"):
                mutable["sex"] = "Female"
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def validate_section_id(self, value):
        if value is not None and not Section.objects.filter(id=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Selected section does not exist.")
        return value

    def get_photo_url(self, obj):
        return build_media_url(obj.photo, self.context.get("request"))

    def get_qr_payload(self, obj) -> str:
        return sign_student_qr(obj.lrn, obj.qr_token)

    def _active_enrollment(self, obj):
        fallback = None
        for enr in obj.enrollments.all():
            if enr.section:
                if enr.section.school_year and enr.section.school_year.is_active:
                    return enr
                fallback = fallback or enr
        return fallback

    def get_current_section(self, obj) -> str:
        enr = self._active_enrollment(obj)
        return enr.section.name if enr and enr.section else "Unassigned"

    def get_current_section_id(self, obj) -> int | None:
        enr = self._active_enrollment(obj)
        return enr.section_id if enr else None

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        section_id = validated_data.pop("section_id", None)
        validated_data.pop("remove_photo", None)
        barangay = validated_data.pop("barangay", "")
        city = validated_data.pop("municipality_city", "")
        province = validated_data.pop("province", "")

        student = Student(**validated_data)
        student.set_address_location(barangay=barangay, municipality_city=city, province=province)
        student.save()
        sync_student_enrollment(student=student, section_id=section_id)
        return student

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        section_id = validated_data.pop("section_id", None)
        remove_photo = bool(validated_data.pop("remove_photo", False))
        if remove_photo:
            validated_data["photo"] = None

        has_loc_change = any(k in validated_data for k in ("barangay", "municipality_city", "province"))
        barangay = validated_data.pop("barangay", instance.barangay)
        city = validated_data.pop("municipality_city", instance.municipality_city)
        province = validated_data.pop("province", instance.province)

        for attr, val in validated_data.items():
            setattr(instance, attr, val)
        if has_loc_change:
            instance.set_address_location(barangay=barangay, municipality_city=city, province=province)
        instance.save()
        sync_student_enrollment(student=instance, section_id=section_id)
        return instance


class EnrollmentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source="student.full_name", read_only=True)
    lrn = serializers.CharField(source="student.lrn", read_only=True)
    section_name = serializers.CharField(source="section.name", read_only=True)
    school_year = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(source="section.school_year", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Enrollment
        fields = [
            "id",
            "student",
            "student_name",
            "lrn",
            "section",
            "section_name",
            "school_year",
            "enrollment_date",
            "status",
            "status_date",
            "remarks",
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            "enrollment_date": {"required": False},
        }
        validators = []

    def validate(self, attrs):
        student = attrs.get("student") or (self.instance.student if self.instance else None)
        section = attrs.get("section") or (self.instance.section if self.instance else None)
        if student and section:
            dup = Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, section__school_year=section.school_year)
            if self.instance:
                dup = dup.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)
            if dup.exists():
                raise serializers.ValidationError(
                    {"student": "This student is already enrolled for the selected school year."}
                )

        if not self.instance and not attrs.get("enrollment_date"):
            attrs["enrollment_date"] = timezone.localdate()
        return attrs

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\apps\students\services.py

import csv
import io
import re
from datetime import date, datetime
from django.db import transaction

from apps.school.models import GradeLevel, SchoolYear, Section
from common.exceptions import AppError
from common.phone import normalize_ph_phone
from .models import Enrollment, Student

MAX_CSV_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMPORT_ROWS = 2000
CONTROL_HTML_RE = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f<>]")


def _clean_cell(raw: object, max_len: int = 120) -> str:
    text = CONTROL_HTML_RE.sub("", str(raw or "")).strip()
    if text.startswith(("=", "+", "-", "@", "|")) and not re.match(r"^\+?\d+$", text):
        text = text.lstrip("=+-@|").strip()
    return text[:max_len]


def _parse_csv_birthdate(raw_val: str) -> date:
    clean = _clean_cell(raw_val, 30)
    if not clean:
        return date(2012, 1, 1)
    for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d", "%m/%d/%Y", "%d/%m/%Y", "%Y/%m/%d"):
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(clean, fmt).date()
        except ValueError:
            continue
    raise ValueError(f"Invalid birthdate '{clean}' (use YYYY-MM-DD or MM/DD/YYYY).")


def _normalize_sex(raw_val: str) -> str:
    val = _clean_cell(raw_val, 15).upper()
    if val in ("F", "FEMALE", "GIRL"):
        return "Female"
    return "Male"


def sync_student_enrollment(*, student: Student, section_id: int | None) -> None:
    if not section_id:
        return
    sec = Section.objects.select_related("school_year").filter(id=section_id).first()
    if not sec:
        return
    existing = Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, section__school_year=sec.school_year).first()
    if existing:
        existing.section = sec
        existing.status = "ENROLLED"
        existing.save(update_fields=["section", "status", "updated_at"])
    else:
        Enrollment.objects.create(student=student, section=sec, status="ENROLLED")

def bulk_import_students_csv(
    *,
    uploaded_file,
    default_section_id: int | None = None,
    auto_create_sections: bool = True,
    actor=None,
) -> dict:
    if not uploaded_file:
        raise AppError("MISSING_FILE", "Please choose a CSV file to upload.", 400)

    filename = str(getattr(uploaded_file, "name", "") or "").lower()
    if filename and not filename.endswith(".csv"):
        raise AppError("INVALID_FILE_TYPE", "Only .csv class list files are supported.", 400)

    file_size = getattr(uploaded_file, "size", 0) or 0
    if file_size > MAX_CSV_BYTES:
        raise AppError("FILE_TOO_LARGE", "CSV file must be 5 MB or smaller.", 400)

    raw_bytes = uploaded_file.read()
    if len(raw_bytes) > MAX_CSV_BYTES:
        raise AppError("FILE_TOO_LARGE", "CSV file must be 5 MB or smaller.", 400)

    try:
        decoded = raw_bytes.decode("utf-8-sig")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        decoded = raw_bytes.decode("latin-1", errors="replace")

    reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(decoded))
    if not reader.fieldnames:
        raise AppError("EMPTY_CSV", "The uploaded CSV file has no header row.", 400)

    school_year = SchoolYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or SchoolYear.objects.first()
    if not school_year:
        today = date.today()
        sy_code = f"{today.year}-{today.year + 1}" if today.month >= 6 else f"{today.year - 1}-{today.year}"
        school_year = SchoolYear.objects.create(
            code=sy_code,
            start_date=date(today.year, 6, 1),
            baseline_cutoff_date=date(today.year, 6, 5),
            end_date=date(today.year + 1, 3, 31),
            is_active=True,
        )

    sections_by_name: dict[str, Section] = {
        sec.name.strip().lower(): sec
        for sec in Section.objects.filter(is_active=True).select_related("grade_level")
    }
    default_section = (
        Section.objects.filter(id=default_section_id, is_active=True).first()
        if default_section_id
        else None
    )
    grades_by_key: dict[str, GradeLevel] = {}
    for gl in GradeLevel.objects.all():
        grades_by_key[gl.name.strip().lower()] = gl
        grades_by_key[gl.code.strip().lower()] = gl

    existing_students: dict[str, Student] = {
        st.lrn: st for st in Student.objects.all()
    }

    created_count = 0
    updated_count = 0
    skipped_count = 0
    errors: list[dict] = []

    rows = list(reader)
    if len(rows) > MAX_IMPORT_ROWS:
        raise AppError(
            "TOO_MANY_ROWS",
            f"CSV exceeds the {MAX_IMPORT_ROWS}-row limit per upload.",
            400,
        )

    for idx, raw_row in enumerate(rows, start=2):
        norm = {
            str(k or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_"): v
            for k, v in raw_row.items()
            if k is not None
        }

        if not any(str(v or "").strip() for v in norm.values()):
            continue

        raw_lrn = re.sub(r"\D+", "", str(norm.get("lrn") or ""))
        if len(raw_lrn) != 12:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({
                "row": idx,
                "lrn": _clean_cell(norm.get("lrn"), 20),
                "reason": "LRN must be exactly 12 digits.",
            })
            continue

        first_name = _clean_cell(norm.get("first_name") or norm.get("firstname"), 100)
        last_name = _clean_cell(norm.get("last_name") or norm.get("lastname"), 100)
        if not first_name or not last_name:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({
                "row": idx,
                "lrn": raw_lrn,
                "reason": "Both first_name and last_name are required.",
            })
            continue

        raw_phone = _clean_cell(
            norm.get("parent_contact") or norm.get("contact_number") or norm.get("phone"),
            40,
        )
        normalized_phone = normalize_ph_phone(raw_phone) if raw_phone else ""
        if not normalized_phone:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({
                "row": idx,
                "lrn": raw_lrn,
                "reason": "Valid parent_contact phone number is required.",
            })
            continue

        try:
            birthdate = _parse_csv_birthdate(str(norm.get("birthdate") or norm.get("birth_date") or ""))
        except ValueError as exc:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({"row": idx, "lrn": raw_lrn, "reason": str(exc)})
            continue

        section_name = _clean_cell(norm.get("section_name") or norm.get("section"), 100)
        grade_raw = _clean_cell(norm.get("grade_level") or norm.get("grade"), 60)

        target_section = default_section
        if section_name:
            sec_key = section_name.lower()
            target_section = sections_by_name.get(sec_key)
            if not target_section and auto_create_sections:
                grade_obj = grades_by_key.get(grade_raw.lower()) if grade_raw else None
                if not grade_obj:
                    num_match = re.search(r"(\d+)", grade_raw or "7")
                    lvl_num = int(num_match.group(1)) if num_match else 7
                    g_code = f"G{lvl_num}"
                    g_name = grade_raw if grade_raw else f"Grade {lvl_num}"
                    grade_obj = grades_by_key.get(g_code.lower())
                    if not grade_obj:
                        grade_obj, _ = GradeLevel.objects.get_or_create(
                            code=g_code[:20],
                            defaults={
                                "name": g_name[:100],
                                "stage": "SHS" if lvl_num >= 11 else "JHS",
                                "level_order": lvl_num,
                            },
                        )
                        grades_by_key[g_code.lower()] = grade_obj
                        grades_by_key[grade_obj.name.lower()] = grade_obj

                target_section, _ = Section.objects.get_or_create(
                    school_year=school_year,
                    grade_level=grade_obj,
                    name=section_name,
                    defaults={"is_active": True, "capacity": 45},
                )
                sections_by_name[sec_key] = target_section

        if not target_section:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({
                "row": idx,
                "lrn": raw_lrn,
                "reason": "No target section found (provide section_name or select a default section).",
            })
            continue

        barangay_val = _clean_cell(norm.get("barangay"), 100)
        city_val = _clean_cell(norm.get("municipality_city") or norm.get("city"), 100)
        province_val = _clean_cell(norm.get("province"), 100)

        student_fields = {
            "first_name": first_name,
            "middle_name": _clean_cell(norm.get("middle_name") or norm.get("middlename"), 100),
            "last_name": last_name,
            "suffix": _clean_cell(norm.get("suffix"), 20),
            "sex": _normalize_sex(str(norm.get("sex") or norm.get("gender") or "Male")),
            "birthdate": birthdate,
            "mother_tongue": _clean_cell(norm.get("mother_tongue"), 100),
            "ethnic_group": _clean_cell(norm.get("ethnic_group"), 100),
            "religion": _clean_cell(norm.get("religion"), 100),
            "house_street_sitio": _clean_cell(norm.get("house_street_sitio") or norm.get("house_street"), 255),
            "father_name": _clean_cell(norm.get("father_name"), 200),
            "mother_maiden_name": _clean_cell(norm.get("mother_maiden_name"), 200),
            "guardian_name": _clean_cell(norm.get("guardian_name"), 200),
            "guardian_relationship": _clean_cell(norm.get("guardian_relationship"), 50),
            "parent_contact": normalized_phone,
            "is_active": True,
        }

        try:
            with transaction.atomic():
                existing = existing_students.get(raw_lrn)
                if existing:
                    for attr, val in student_fields.items():
                        if val != "" or attr in ("first_name", "last_name", "sex", "birthdate", "parent_contact", "is_active"):
                            setattr(existing, attr, val)
                    if barangay_val or city_val or province_val:
                        existing.set_address_location(
                            barangay=barangay_val or existing.barangay,
                            municipality_city=city_val or existing.municipality_city,
                            province=province_val or existing.province,
                        )
                    if actor and getattr(actor, "is_authenticated", False):
                        existing.updated_by = actor
                    existing.save()
                    student_obj = existing
                    updated_count += 1
                else:
                    if actor and getattr(actor, "is_authenticated", False):
                        student_fields["created_by"] = actor
                    student_obj = Student(lrn=raw_lrn, **student_fields)
                    student_obj.set_address_location(
                        barangay=barangay_val,
                        municipality_city=city_val,
                        province=province_val,
                    )
                    student_obj.save()
                    existing_students[raw_lrn] = student_obj
                    created_count += 1

                sync_student_enrollment(student=student_obj, section_id=target_section.id)
        except Exception as exc:
            skipped_count += 1
            errors.append({
                "row": idx,
                "lrn": raw_lrn,
                "reason": _clean_cell(str(exc), 140) or "Could not save row.",
            })

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "created_count": created_count,
        "updated_count": updated_count,
        "skipped_count": skipped_count,
        "errors": errors[:50],
    }

from django.db.models import Count, Q
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.parsers import FormParser, JSONParser, MultiPartParser
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.school.selectors import get_sections_for_user
from common.audit import AuditedModelViewSet, record_audit_log
from common.permissions import IsLeadershipOrReadOnly, IsSchoolLeader
from .models import Enrollment
from .selectors import get_parent_portal_data, get_signed_qr_cards, get_students_for_user
from .serializers import EnrollmentSerializer, StudentSerializer
from .services import bulk_import_students_csv


class StudentViewSet(AuditedModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = StudentSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrReadOnly]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser]

    def get_queryset(self):
        sec_id = self.request.query_params.get("section_id") or self.request.query_params.get("section")
        return get_students_for_user(
            user=self.request.user,
            search=self.request.query_params.get("search", "").strip(),
            section_id=int(sec_id) if (sec_id and str(sec_id).isdigit()) else None,
        )

    @action(detail=False, methods=["get"], url_path="metrics")
    def metrics(self, request):
        base_qs = self.get_queryset()
        agg = base_qs.aggregate(
            total=Count("id", distinct=True),
            active=Count("id", filter=Q(is_active=True), distinct=True),
            male=Count("id", filter=Q(is_active=True, sex="Male"), distinct=True),
            female=Count("id", filter=Q(is_active=True, sex="Female"), distinct=True),
            enrolled=Count("id", filter=Q(is_active=True, enrollments__status="ENROLLED"), distinct=True),
            paired=Count(
                "id",
                filter=Q(is_active=True, qr_token__isnull=False) & ~Q(qr_token=""),
                distinct=True,
            ),
        )
        total = agg["total"] or 0
        active = agg["active"] or 0
        enrolled = agg["enrolled"] or 0
        total_sections = get_sections_for_user(request.user).count()

        return Response({
            "total_students": active,
            "total": total,
            "active": active,
            "paired": agg["paired"] or 0,
            "total_sections": total_sections,
            "male": agg["male"] or 0,
            "female": agg["female"] or 0,
            "enrolled": enrolled,
            "unassigned": max(0, active - enrolled),
        })

    @action(detail=False, methods=["get"], url_path="dropdown")
    def dropdown(self, request):
        qs = (
            self.get_queryset()
            .select_related(None)
            .prefetch_related(None)
            .filter(is_active=True)
            .only("id", "lrn", "first_name", "middle_name", "last_name", "suffix", "sex")[:500]
        )
        return Response([
            {
                "id": st.id,
                "lrn": st.lrn,
                "first_name": st.first_name,
                "last_name": st.last_name,
                "full_name": st.full_name,
                "sex": st.sex,
            }
            for st in qs
        ])

    @action(detail=False, methods=["get"], url_path="qr-cards")
    def qr_cards(self, request):
        sec_id = request.query_params.get("section_id")
        cards = get_signed_qr_cards(
            section_id=int(sec_id) if (sec_id and str(sec_id).isdigit()) else None,
            request=request,
        )
        return Response({"cards": cards, "total": len(cards)})

    @action(
        detail=False,
        methods=["post"],
        url_path="bulk-import",
        permission_classes=[permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsSchoolLeader],
        parser_classes=[MultiPartParser, FormParser],
    )
    def bulk_import(self, request):
        uploaded_file = request.FILES.get("file") or request.FILES.get("csv_file")
        raw_section_id = request.data.get("section_id")
        default_sec_id = int(raw_section_id) if (raw_section_id and str(raw_section_id).isdigit()) else None
        auto_create = str(request.data.get("auto_create_sections", "true")).strip().lower() in (
            "1",
            "true",
            "yes",
        )

        summary = bulk_import_students_csv(
            uploaded_file=uploaded_file,
            default_section_id=default_sec_id,
            auto_create_sections=auto_create,
            actor=request.user,
        )
        record_audit_log(
            request=request,
            table_name="students",
            record_id=0,
            action="IMPORT",
            new_data={
                "created": summary["created_count"],
                "updated": summary["updated_count"],
                "skipped": summary["skipped_count"],
            },
        )
        return Response(summary, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

import logging
from django.db import transaction
from rest_framework import viewsets

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def get_client_ip(request) -> str:
    if not request:
        return "127.0.0.1"
    cf_ip = request.META.get("HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_IP")
    if cf_ip:
        return cf_ip.strip()
    xff = request.META.get("HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR")
    if xff:
        return xff.split(",")[0].strip()
    return request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR", "127.0.0.1")


def record_audit_log(
    *,
    request,
    table_name: str,
    record_id: int,
    action: str,
    changed_fields: list[str] | None = None,
    new_data: dict | None = None,
):
    from apps.accounts.models import AuditLog

    user = request.user if (request and request.user and request.user.is_authenticated) else None
    payload = dict(new_data or {})
    if changed_fields:
        payload["changed_fields"] = [str(field) for field in changed_fields]

    try:
        with transaction.atomic():
            AuditLog.objects.create(
                table_name=str(table_name)[:64],
                record_id=record_id or 0,
                action=str(action).upper()[:10],
                new_data=payload or None,
                performed_by=user,
                client_ip=get_client_ip(request),
                user_agent=str(request.META.get("HTTP_USER_AGENT", ""))[:255] if request else "",
            )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Audit log skipped for %s #%s: %s", table_name, record_id, exc)


class AuditedModelViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """Automatically records real CREATE, UPDATE, and DELETE entries in audit_logs."""

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        instance = serializer.save()
        record_audit_log(
            request=self.request,
            table_name=instance._meta.db_table,
            record_id=instance.pk or 0,
            action="CREATE",
            changed_fields=list(serializer.validated_data.keys()),
        )

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        changed = list(serializer.validated_data.keys())
        instance = serializer.save()
        record_audit_log(
            request=self.request,
            table_name=instance._meta.db_table,
            record_id=instance.pk or 0,
            action="UPDATE",
            changed_fields=changed,
        )

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        table_name = instance._meta.db_table
        record_id = instance.pk or 0
        instance.delete()
        record_audit_log(
            request=self.request,
            table_name=table_name,
            record_id=record_id,
            action="DELETE",
        )

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\common\exceptions.py

import logging
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import exception_handler

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class AppError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, error_code: str, message: str, status_code: int = 400):
        self.error_code = error_code
        self.message = message
        self.status_code = status_code
        super().__init__(message)


def _extract_first_error_message(detail_data) -> str:
    if isinstance(detail_data, str):
        return detail_data
    if isinstance(detail_data, list) and detail_data:
        return _extract_first_error_message(detail_data[0])
    if isinstance(detail_data, dict) and detail_data:
        first_key, first_val = next(iter(detail_data.items()))
        msg = _extract_first_error_message(first_val)
        if first_key in ("detail", "non_field_errors", "message", "error"):
            return msg
        return f"{str(first_key).replace('_', ' ').title()}: {msg}"
    return "Please check your input and try again."


def custom_exception_handler(exc, context):
    if isinstance(exc, AppError):
        return Response(
            {
                "ok": False,
                "error_code": exc.error_code,
                "message": exc.message,
                "detail": exc.message,
                "error": exc.message,
            },
            status=exc.status_code,
        )

    response = exception_handler(exc, context)
    if response is not None:
        raw_details = response.data
        readable = _extract_first_error_message(raw_details)
        response.data = {
            "ok": False,
            "error_code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
            "message": readable,
            "detail": readable,
            "error": readable,
            "details": raw_details,
        }
        return response

    view_name = context.get("view").__class__.__name__ if context.get("view") else "UnknownView"
    logger.exception("Unhandled server error in %s: %s", view_name, exc)
    fallback_msg = "An unexpected server error occurred. Please try again."
    return Response(
        {
            "ok": False,
            "error_code": "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
            "message": fallback_msg,
            "detail": fallback_msg,
            "error": fallback_msg,
        },
        status=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
    )

import json
from django.db import models


class SafeJsonField(models.JSONField):
    """
    Safely reads PostgreSQL JSONB and array columns that psycopg2
    already deserializes into Python list or dict objects.
    """

    def from_db_value(self, value, expression, connection):
        if value is None or isinstance(value, (dict, list, int, float, bool)):
            return value
        if isinstance(value, (bytes, bytearray)):
            value = value.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        if isinstance(value, str):
            try:
                return json.loads(value, cls=self.decoder)
            except ValueError:
                return value
        return value

    def deconstruct(self):
        name, _, args, kwargs = super().deconstruct()
        return name, "django.db.models.JSONField", args, kwargs

import math


def calculate_distance_meters(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) -> float:
    try:
        u_lat, u_lon = float(lat1), float(lon1)
        c_lat, c_lon = float(lat2), float(lon2)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return float("inf")

    if not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (u_lat, u_lon, c_lat, c_lon)):
        return float("inf")

    earth_radius = 6371000.0
    phi1, phi2 = math.radians(c_lat), math.radians(u_lat)
    d_phi = math.radians(u_lat - c_lat)
    d_lambda = math.radians(u_lon - c_lon)
    a = (math.sin(d_phi / 2.0) ** 2) + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * (math.sin(d_lambda / 2.0) ** 2)
    a = max(0.0, min(1.0, a))
    return round(earth_radius * (2.0 * math.atan2(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1.0 - a))), 1)


def verify_campus_gps(
    *,
    latitude,
    longitude,
    campus_lat,
    campus_lon,
    radius_meters: int = 150,
    accuracy_meters=None,
    is_mock_location: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    if is_mock_location:
        return {
            "is_valid": False,
            "is_inside": False,
            "distance_meters": None,
            "allowed_radius": radius_meters,
            "reason": "Fake or mock GPS location detected.",
        }

    try:
        u_lat, u_lon = float(latitude), float(longitude)
        c_lat, c_lon = float(campus_lat), float(campus_lon)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {
            "is_valid": False,
            "is_inside": False,
            "distance_meters": None,
            "allowed_radius": radius_meters,
            "reason": "Invalid or missing GPS coordinates.",
        }

    if (
        not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (u_lat, u_lon, c_lat, c_lon))
        or not (-90.0 <= u_lat <= 90.0)
        or not (-180.0 <= u_lon <= 180.0)
        or (u_lat == 0.0 and u_lon == 0.0)
    ):
        return {
            "is_valid": False,
            "is_inside": False,
            "distance_meters": None,
            "allowed_radius": radius_meters,
            "reason": "GPS coordinates are outside valid earth bounds.",
        }

    distance = calculate_distance_meters(u_lat, u_lon, c_lat, c_lon)
    try:
        acc_val = float(accuracy_meters) if accuracy_meters is not None else 0.0
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        acc_val = 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(acc_val):
        acc_val = 0.0
    drift_buffer = min(max(acc_val, 0.0), 35.0)
    is_inside = math.isfinite(distance) and distance <= (radius_meters + drift_buffer)

    return {
        "is_valid": True,
        "is_inside": is_inside,
        "distance_meters": distance if math.isfinite(distance) else None,
        "allowed_radius": radius_meters,
        "reason": "Within campus boundary." if is_inside else f"Outside campus ({round(distance)}m away).",
    }

import base64
import hashlib
import io
import os
import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from PIL import Image, UnidentifiedImageError
from rest_framework import serializers

MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION = 1024
ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTS = {"png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp"}


def save_base64_logo_to_disk(raw_value: str) -> str | None:
    """Converts raw Base64 logo strings into small static files under /media/school_logos/."""
    clean = str(raw_value or "").strip()
    if not clean or clean.lower() in ("none", "null", "undefined"):
        return None

    if clean.startswith(("http://", "https://", "/media/")):
        return clean
    if clean.startswith("school_logos/"):
        return f"/media/{clean}"

    if clean.startswith("data:image") or clean.startswith(("/9j/", "iVBORw0KGgo")):
        try:
            if ";base64," in clean:
                header, b64_part = clean.split(";base64,", 1)
                raw_ext = header.split("/")[-1].split(";")[0].lower()
                ext = "jpg" if raw_ext in ("jpeg", "pjpeg") else raw_ext
            else:
                b64_part = clean
                ext = "jpg" if clean.startswith("/9j/") else "png"

            if ext not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTS:
                ext = "png"

            raw_bytes = base64.b64decode(b64_part, validate=True)
            if len(raw_bytes) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
                return None

            with Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw_bytes)) as img:
                img.verify()

            digest = hashlib.sha256(raw_bytes).hexdigest()[:16]
            target_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "school_logos")
            os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
            filename = f"logo_{digest}.{ext}"
            full_path = os.path.join(target_dir, filename)
            if not os.path.exists(full_path):
                with open(full_path, "wb") as fh:
                    fh.write(raw_bytes)
            return f"/media/school_logos/{filename}"
        except Exception:
            return None
    return None


def build_media_url(photo_field, request=None) -> str | None:
    if not photo_field:
        return None
    if isinstance(photo_field, str):
        clean = photo_field.strip()
        if not clean or clean.lower() in ("none", "null", "undefined"):
            return None
        if clean.startswith("data:image") or clean.startswith(("/9j/", "iVBORw0KGgo")):
            disk_rel = save_base64_logo_to_disk(clean)
            if not disk_rel:
                return None
            return request.build_absolute_uri(disk_rel) if request else disk_rel
        if clean.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
            return clean
        rel = clean if clean.startswith("/") else f"/{clean}"
        return request.build_absolute_uri(rel) if request else rel

    if hasattr(photo_field, "url"):
        try:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(photo_field.url) if request else photo_field.url
        except ValueError:
            return None
    return None


def validate_and_optimize_image(raw_bytes: bytes, prefix: str = "photo") -> ContentFile:
    """Verifies real image magic bytes via Pillow and compresses oversized uploads."""
    if len(raw_bytes) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise serializers.ValidationError("Image exceeds the 5 MB maximum size.")

    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw_bytes)) as probe:
            probe.verify()

        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw_bytes)) as img:
            has_alpha = img.mode in ("RGBA", "LA") or (img.mode == "P" and "transparency" in img.info)
            if max(img.size) > MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION:
                img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION, MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

            buffer = io.BytesIO()
            if has_alpha:
                img.save(buffer, format="PNG", optimize=True)
                ext = "png"
            else:
                rgb = img.convert("RGB")
                rgb.save(buffer, format="JPEG", quality=82, optimize=True)
                ext = "jpg"
            return ContentFile(buffer.getvalue(), name=f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.{ext}")
    except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError) as exc:
        raise serializers.ValidationError("Invalid or corrupted image file.") from exc


def decode_uploaded_image(data, prefix: str = "upload"):
    if data in (None, "", "null", "undefined"):
        return None
    if hasattr(data, "read"):
        raw_bytes = data.read()
        data.seek(0)
        return validate_and_optimize_image(raw_bytes, prefix=prefix)
    if isinstance(data, str):
        clean = data.strip()
        if clean.startswith("data:image") and ";base64," in clean:
            _, b64_part = clean.split(";base64,", 1)
            try:
                raw_bytes = base64.b64decode(b64_part, validate=True)
            except Exception as exc:
                raise serializers.ValidationError("Invalid Base64 image data.") from exc
            return validate_and_optimize_image(raw_bytes, prefix=prefix)
    return serializers.SkipField()


class FlexibleImageField(serializers.Field):
    def to_representation(self, value):
        return build_media_url(value, self.context.get("request"))

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        result = decode_uploaded_image(data, prefix="photo")
        if isinstance(result, serializers.SkipField):
            raise result
        return result


class SchoolLogoField(serializers.Field):
    def to_representation(self, value):
        return build_media_url(value, self.context.get("request"))

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        file_obj = decode_uploaded_image(data, prefix="logo")
        if isinstance(file_obj, serializers.SkipField):
            raise file_obj
        if file_obj is None:
            return None
        raw_bytes = file_obj.read()
        ext = getattr(file_obj, "name", "logo.png").rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower()
        digest = hashlib.sha256(raw_bytes).hexdigest()[:16]
        target_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "school_logos")
        os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
        filename = f"logo_{digest}.{ext}"
        with open(os.path.join(target_dir, filename), "wb") as fh:
            fh.write(raw_bytes)
        return f"/media/school_logos/{filename}"

from rest_framework.pagination import PageNumberPagination
from rest_framework.response import Response


class StandardPagination(PageNumberPagination):
    page_size = 25
    page_size_query_param = "page_size"
    max_page_size = 500

    def get_page_size(self, request):
        raw = str(request.query_params.get(self.page_size_query_param, "")).strip().upper()
        if raw in ("ALL", "MAX"):
            return self.max_page_size
        return super().get_page_size(request)

    def get_paginated_response(self, data):
        return Response({
            "count": self.page.paginator.count,
            "total_pages": self.page.paginator.num_pages,
            "current_page": self.page.number,
            "page_size": self.get_page_size(self.request),
            "next": self.get_next_link(),
            "previous": self.get_previous_link(),
            "results": data,
        })

from rest_framework import permissions

LEADERSHIP_ROLES = {"ADMIN", "SUPERADMIN", "PRINCIPAL", "DEPT_HEAD"}
STAFF_AND_FACULTY_ROLES = {"ADMIN", "SUPERADMIN", "PRINCIPAL", "DEPT_HEAD", "FACULTY"}


def get_user_role(user) -> str:
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return "ANONYMOUS"
    profile = getattr(user, "profile", None)
    if profile and profile.role:
        role = str(profile.role).upper()
        if user.is_superuser and role not in {"SUPERADMIN", "ADMIN"}:
            return "SUPERADMIN"
        return role
    if user.is_superuser:
        return "SUPERADMIN"
    return "ADMIN" if user.is_staff else "FACULTY"


def is_school_leader(user) -> bool:
    return bool(
        user
        and user.is_authenticated
        and (user.is_superuser or get_user_role(user) in LEADERSHIP_ROLES)
    )


def is_staff_or_faculty(user) -> bool:
    return bool(
        user
        and user.is_authenticated
        and (user.is_superuser or get_user_role(user) in STAFF_AND_FACULTY_ROLES)
    )


class IsSchoolLeader(permissions.BasePermission):
    """Allows access only to Admin, Superadmin, Principal, or Department Head."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return is_school_leader(request.user)


class IsStaffOrFaculty(permissions.BasePermission):
    """Blocks PARENT accounts from internal staff/faculty endpoints."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return is_staff_or_faculty(request.user)


class IsLeadershipOrReadOnly(permissions.BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return False
        if request.method in permissions.SAFE_METHODS:
            return True
        return is_school_leader(request.user)


class IsLeadershipOrStaffReadOnly(permissions.BasePermission):
    """Read access for school staff/faculty (excludes PARENT); write access for school leaders."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not is_staff_or_faculty(request.user):
            return False
        if request.method in permissions.SAFE_METHODS:
            return True
        return is_school_leader(request.user)


class IsSystemAdmin(permissions.BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return bool(
            request.user
            and request.user.is_authenticated
            and (request.user.is_superuser or get_user_role(request.user) in {"ADMIN", "SUPERADMIN"})
        )

"""International (E.164) & Philippines mobile phone number normalization."""

import re


def normalize_international_phone(raw_phone: str, default_country: str = "PH") -> str:
    """
    Normalizes both Philippines local/mobile formats and global E.164 phone numbers.
    - Preserves explicit international prefixes (`+1...`, `+44...`, `+65...`, `00...`).
    - Normalizes PH mobile numbers (`09XXXXXXXXX`, `9XXXXXXXXX`, `639XXXXXXXXX`) to `+639XXXXXXXXX`.
    - Returns empty string when no digits are provided.
    """
    raw = str(raw_phone or "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return ""

    has_plus = raw.startswith("+")
    digits = re.sub(r"\D+", "", raw)
    if not digits:
        return ""

    # International dialing prefix 00<country><number>
    if not has_plus and digits.startswith("00") and len(digits) >= 10:
        return f"+{digits[2:][:15]}"

    # Explicit E.164 international number (+<country_code><subscriber>, 8..15 digits)
    if has_plus:
        if digits.startswith("6309") and len(digits) == 13:
            return f"+63{digits[3:]}"
        return f"+{digits[:15]}"

    # Philippines local/mobile shorthand normalization
    if default_country.upper() == "PH":
        if digits.startswith("639") and len(digits) == 12:
            return f"+{digits}"
        if digits.startswith("09") and len(digits) == 11:
            return f"+63{digits[1:]}"
        if digits.startswith("9") and len(digits) == 10:
            return f"+63{digits}"

    # Fallback: emit valid E.164 format up to 15 digits
    return f"+{digits[:15]}"


def normalize_ph_phone(raw_phone: str) -> str:
    """Backward-compatible alias supporting both PH and international E.164 numbers."""
    return normalize_international_phone(raw_phone, default_country="PH")

import hashlib
import hmac
from dataclasses import dataclass
from django.conf import settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerifiedQrData:
    lrn: str
    qr_token: str
    is_signed: bool


def sign_student_qr(lrn: str, qr_token: str) -> str:
    clean_lrn = str(lrn or "").strip()
    clean_token = str(qr_token or "").strip()
    payload = f"{clean_lrn}:{clean_token}".encode("utf-8")
    sig = hmac.new(settings.SECRET_KEY.encode("utf-8"), payload, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()[:12].upper()
    return f"ATTENDSURE:{clean_lrn}:{clean_token}:{sig}"


def verify_student_qr(raw_scan: str, *, allow_legacy_unsigned: bool | None = None) -> VerifiedQrData | None:
    if allow_legacy_unsigned is None:
        allow_legacy_unsigned = bool(getattr(settings, "ALLOW_LEGACY_UNSIGNED_QR", False))
    clean = str(raw_scan or "").strip().replace("\r", "").replace("\n", "").replace("\0", "")[:128]
    if not clean:
        return None

    parts = clean.split(":")
    if len(parts) == 4 and parts[0].upper() == "ATTENDSURE":
        _, lrn, token, provided_sig = parts
        expected_sig = sign_student_qr(lrn, token).rsplit(":", 1)[-1]
        if not hmac.compare_digest(provided_sig.upper(), expected_sig):
            return None
        return VerifiedQrData(lrn=lrn.strip(), qr_token=token.strip(), is_signed=True)

    if not allow_legacy_unsigned:
        return None

    token_only = parts[-1].strip()
    return VerifiedQrData(lrn=token_only, qr_token=token_only, is_signed=False)

r"""
AttendSure - Thread-Safe Color Terminal API & Security Logger
File: D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\common\request_logger.py
"""

import ipaddress
import logging
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
import time
import unicodedata
from urllib.parse import parse_qsl, urlencode
from django.utils import timezone

security_logger = logging.getLogger("attendsure.security")

_CONSOLE_LOCK = threading.Lock()

# Strips ANSI CSI, OSC, and single-char ESC sequences
TERMINAL_ESCAPE_RE = re.compile(
    r"\x1b(?:[@-Z\\-_]|\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]|\][^\x07\x1b]*(?:\x07|\x1b\\))"
)
KEY_SPLIT_RE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9]+")

# Exact parameter names that must always be redacted
EXACT_SENSITIVE_KEYS = {
    "pass",
    "code",
    "key",
    "sig",
    "signature",
    "pin",
    "otp",
    "jwt",
    "bearer",
    "authorization",
}

# Word segments (split on _, -, .) that indicate a credential or token
SENSITIVE_KEY_SEGMENTS = {
    "password",
    "passwd",
    "pwd",
    "passcode",
    "passphrase",
    "token",
    "secret",
    "apikey",
    "otp",
    "jwt",
    "refresh",
    "access",
    "session",
    "sessionid",
    "csrf",
    "csrftoken",
    "private",
    "credential",
}

HIDDEN_POLL_PARAMS = {"silent", "nocache", "_t"}

QUIET_POLL_PATHS = {
    "/api/notifications/",
    "/api/v1/messaging/notifications/",
    "/api/system/status/",
    "/api/v1/reports/system-status/",
    "/api/core/maintenance/db-check/",
    "/api/health/db/",
    "/api/v1/health/db/",
    "/api/v1/school/public-branding/",
}

TRUSTED_PROXIES = {
    ip.strip()
    for ip in os.getenv("TRUSTED_PROXY_IPS", "127.0.0.1,::1").split(",")
    if ip.strip()
}


def _enable_windows_ansi_colors() -> bool:
    """Enables ANSI color codes on Windows terminals unless NO_COLOR is set."""
    if os.getenv("NO_COLOR") or "test" in sys.argv:
        return False
    if not hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty") or not sys.stdout.isatty():
        return False
    if os.name != "nt":
        return True
    try:
        import ctypes

        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        handle = kernel32.GetStdHandle(-11)  # STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE
        mode = ctypes.c_ulong()
        if kernel32.GetConsoleMode(handle, ctypes.byref(mode)):
            kernel32.SetConsoleMode(handle, mode.value | 0x0004)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return True


def _atomic_print(line: str) -> None:
    """Writes a complete newline-terminated log row atomically across threads."""
    if sys.stdout is None:
        return
    try:
        with _CONSOLE_LOCK:
            sys.stdout.write(f"{line}\n")
            sys.stdout.flush()
    except OSError:
        pass


class TerminalColors:
    RESET = "\033[0m"
    BOLD = "\033[1m"
    DIM = "\033[2m"

    RED = "\033[91m"
    GREEN = "\033[92m"
    YELLOW = "\033[93m"
    CYAN = "\033[96m"
    MAGENTA = "\033[95m"
    WHITE = "\033[97m"

    BG_RED = "\033[41m\033[97m\033[1m"
    BG_YELLOW = "\033[43m\033[30m\033[1m"


def _sanitize_log_text(value: object, max_len: int = 140) -> str:
    """Strips ANSI escapes, C0/C1 controls, and Unicode BiDi/format spoofing chars."""
    cleaned = TERMINAL_ESCAPE_RE.sub("", str(value or ""))
    safe_chars = [
        ch for ch in cleaned if unicodedata.category(ch)[0] != "C"
    ]
    return "".join(safe_chars)[:max_len]


def _is_sensitive_key(key: str) -> bool:
    """Redacts tokens and secrets without masking benign keys like pass_type or subject_code."""
    lowered = key.strip().lower()
    if lowered in EXACT_SENSITIVE_KEYS:
        return True
    if lowered.endswith(("_key", "-key", "_code", "-code")) and lowered.startswith(
        ("api", "auth", "secret", "qr", "otp", "reset", "verify", "client")
    ):
        return True
    segments = set(KEY_SPLIT_RE.split(lowered))
    return bool(segments & SENSITIVE_KEY_SEGMENTS)


def _safe_query_string(raw_query: str) -> str:
    """Formats query parameters safely while masking credentials and tokens."""
    if not raw_query:
        return ""
    pairs = parse_qsl(raw_query[:1024], keep_blank_values=True)
    masked = [
        (k, "***" if _is_sensitive_key(k) else v)
        for k, v in pairs
        if k not in HIDDEN_POLL_PARAMS
    ]
    if not masked:
        return ""
    return f"?{_sanitize_log_text(urlencode(masked), max_len=90)}"


def _validate_ip(raw_ip: str) -> str | None:
    candidate = raw_ip.strip()
    if not candidate:
        return None
    try:
        return str(ipaddress.ip_address(candidate))
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _extract_client_ip(request) -> str:
    """Honors forwarded headers only when REMOTE_ADDR is a trusted local reverse proxy."""
    remote_addr = str(request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR") or "127.0.0.1").strip()

    if remote_addr in TRUSTED_PROXIES:
        cf_ip = _validate_ip(str(request.META.get("HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_IP") or ""))
        if cf_ip:
            return cf_ip

        xff = str(request.META.get("HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR") or "")
        if xff:
            forwarded_ip = _validate_ip(xff.split(",")[0])
            if forwarded_ip:
                return forwarded_ip

    return _validate_ip(remote_addr) or "127.0.0.1"


def _resolve_authenticated_username(request, response) -> str | None:
    """Reads already-resolved DRF or Django user objects with zero extra DB queries."""
    if response is not None:
        renderer_ctx = getattr(response, "renderer_context", None)
        if isinstance(renderer_ctx, dict):
            drf_request = renderer_ctx.get("request")
            drf_user = getattr(drf_request, "_user", None)
            if drf_user and getattr(drf_user, "is_authenticated", False):
                return _sanitize_log_text(drf_user.username, max_len=40)

    # Read _cached_user from __dict__ so SimpleLazyObject never triggers a DB hit on 404s
    cached_user = getattr(request, "__dict__", {}).get("_cached_user")
    if cached_user and getattr(cached_user, "is_authenticated", False):
        return _sanitize_log_text(cached_user.username, max_len=40)

    return None


def _format_response_size(response, status_code: int) -> str:
    """Reads Content-Length first to avoid copying response byte buffers."""
    if response is None or status_code in (204, 304):
        return "0B"

    header_len = response.get("Content-Length")
    if header_len and str(header_len).isdigit():
        byte_len = int(header_len)
    elif getattr(response, "streaming", False):
        return "stream"
    else:
        content = getattr(response, "content", None)
        byte_len = len(content) if content is not None else None

    if byte_len is None:
        return "stream"
    if byte_len >= 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{byte_len / (1024 * 1024):.2f}MB"
    if byte_len >= 1024:
        return f"{byte_len / 1024:.1f}KB"
    return f"{byte_len}B"


class RequestLoggerMiddleware:
    """Prints thread-safe, color-coded API status, user, size, and latency lines."""

    sync_capable = True
    async_capable = False

    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response
        self.use_colors = _enable_windows_ansi_colors()
        self.quiet_mode = os.getenv("ATTENDSURE_QUIET_LOGGER", "").lower() in ("1", "true")

        reset = self._color(TerminalColors.RESET)
        bold = self._color(TerminalColors.BOLD)
        self._reset = reset
        self._dim = self._color(TerminalColors.DIM)
        self._bold = bold
        self._white = self._color(TerminalColors.WHITE)
        self._red = self._color(TerminalColors.RED)

        # Precompute method badges once at startup
        self._method_badges = {
            "POST": (
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.GREEN)}{bold}[CREATE]{reset}",
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.GREEN)}{'POST':<6}{reset}",
            ),
            "GET": (
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.CYAN)}{bold}[READ]  {reset}",
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.CYAN)}{'GET':<6}{reset}",
            ),
            "PUT": (
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.YELLOW)}{bold}[UPDATE]{reset}",
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.YELLOW)}{'PUT':<6}{reset}",
            ),
            "PATCH": (
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.YELLOW)}{bold}[UPDATE]{reset}",
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.YELLOW)}{'PATCH':<6}{reset}",
            ),
            "DELETE": (
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.MAGENTA)}{bold}[DELETE]{reset}",
                f"{self._color(TerminalColors.MAGENTA)}{'DELETE':<6}{reset}",
            ),
        }

    def __call__(self, request):
        started = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            response = self.get_response(request)
        except Exception:
            duration_ms = max(1, round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000))
            if not self.quiet_mode:
                self.log_request(request, None, duration_ms, forced_code=500)
            raise

        duration_ms = max(1, round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000))
        if not self.quiet_mode:
            self.log_request(request, response, duration_ms)
        return response

    def _color(self, ansi_code: str) -> str:
        return ansi_code if self.use_colors else ""

    def _status_badge(self, code: int) -> str:
        reset, bold = self._reset, self._bold
        if 200 <= code < 300:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.GREEN)}{bold}[OK {code}]       {reset}"
        if code == 304:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.CYAN)}[CACHED 304]   {reset}"
        if 300 <= code < 400:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.CYAN)}[REDIRECT {code}] {reset}"
        if code in (401, 403):
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.BG_YELLOW)} [DENIED {code}]  {reset}"
        if code == 404:
            return f"{self._red}{bold}[NOT FOUND 404]{reset}"
        if code == 405:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.MAGENTA)}{bold}[METHOD 405]   {reset}"
        if code == 429:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.BG_YELLOW)} [LIMIT 429]   {reset}"
        if 400 <= code < 500:
            return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.YELLOW)}{bold}[BAD REQ {code}]  {reset}"
        return f"{self._color(TerminalColors.BG_RED)} [ERROR {code}]   {reset}"

    def log_request(
        self,
        request,
        response,
        duration_ms: int,
        forced_code: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        path = _sanitize_log_text(request.path)
        if path.startswith("/static/"):
            return

        method = _sanitize_log_text(request.method, max_len=10).upper()
        code = forced_code if forced_code is not None else int(getattr(response, "status_code", 200))

        if method == "OPTIONS" and 200 <= code < 400:
            return

        now_str = timezone.localtime().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        raw_qs = request.META.get("QUERY_STRING", "")
        is_media = path.startswith("/media/")
        is_silent_poll = method == "GET" and ("silent=1" in raw_qs or path in QUIET_POLL_PATHS)

        if (is_silent_poll or is_media) and 200 <= code < 400 and duration_ms <= 500:
            tag = "MEDIA" if is_media else "POLL "
            state_str = "CACHED 304" if code == 304 else f"OK {code}"
            _atomic_print(
                f"{self._dim}[{now_str}] [{tag}]   [{state_str}] {method} {path} ({duration_ms}ms){self._reset}"
            )
            return

        action_badge, method_str = self._method_badges.get(
            method,
            (f"{self._white}[CHECK] {self._reset}", f"{method:<6}"),
        )
        status_badge = self._status_badge(code)

        username = None if is_media else _resolve_authenticated_username(request, response)
        user_label = (
            f"{self._white}(User: {username}){self._reset}"
            if username
            else f"{self._dim}(Guest){self._reset}"
        )

        size_str = _format_response_size(response, code)
        if duration_ms > 500:
            perf_badge = f"{self._red}{self._bold}{duration_ms}ms SLOW{self._reset} {self._dim}[{size_str}]{self._reset}"
        else:
            perf_badge = f"{self._dim}{duration_ms}ms [{size_str}]{self._reset}"

        full_target = f"{path}{_safe_query_string(raw_qs)}"
        _atomic_print(
            f"[{now_str}] {action_badge} {status_badge} {method_str} {full_target} {user_label} {perf_badge}"
        )

        if code >= 400:
            client_ip = _extract_client_ip(request)
            security_logger.warning(
                "%s %s -> %s (User: %s, IP: %s, %sms)",
                method,
                full_target,
                code,
                username or "Guest",
                client_ip,
                duration_ms,
            )


ColorTerminalMiddleware = RequestLoggerMiddleware

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\common\security.py

import time
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db import connection
from django.http import JsonResponse
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.decorators import api_view, permission_classes
from rest_framework.response import Response

from common.audit import record_audit_log
from common.permissions import IsSchoolLeader


class ApiSecurityMiddleware:
    """
    Applies OWASP security headers and prevents Django debug HTML pages
    from leaking internal URL patterns when an /api/ path returns 404.
    """

    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response

    def __call__(self, request):
        response = self.get_response(request)

        if request.path.startswith("/api/") and response.status_code == 404:
            content_type = response.get("Content-Type", "")
            if "text/html" in content_type:
                msg = f"API route not found: {request.path}"
                response = JsonResponse(
                    {
                        "ok": False,
                        "error_code": "ENDPOINT_NOT_FOUND",
                        "message": msg,
                        "detail": msg,
                        "error": msg,
                    },
                    status=404,
                )

        response["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
        response["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY"
        response["Referrer-Policy"] = "same-origin"
        response["Permissions-Policy"] = "camera=(self), geolocation=(self), microphone=()"

        if request.path.startswith(("/api/auth/", "/api/v1/accounts/login", "/api/me", "/api/v1/accounts/me")):
            response["Cache-Control"] = "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private"

        return response


@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([permissions.AllowAny])
def database_health_view(request):
    """Checks live PostgreSQL connectivity without exposing database credentials or hosts."""
    started = time.perf_counter()
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute("SELECT 1;")
        cursor.fetchone()
    latency_ms = max(1, round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000))
    return Response(
        {
            "ok": True,
            "status": "CONNECTED",
            "database": "CONNECTED",
            "latency_ms": latency_ms,
        },
        status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    )


@api_view(["POST"])
@permission_classes([permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsSchoolLeader])
def clear_system_cache_view(request):
    """Clears server cache for school leaders (Principals & Admins) and logs the action."""
    cache.clear()
    record_audit_log(
        request=request,
        table_name="system_cache",
        record_id=0,
        action="FLUSH",
    )
    return Response(
        {"ok": True, "success": True, "message": "System cache cleared."},
        status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    )

from django.conf import settings
from django.conf.urls.static import static
from django.contrib import admin
from django.urls import include, path, re_path
from django.views.static import serve

from common.security import clear_system_cache_view, database_health_view


def serve_cached_media(request, path):
    response = serve(request, path, document_root=settings.MEDIA_ROOT)
    response["Cache-Control"] = "public, max-age=86400"
    response["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"] = "*"
    return response


v1_patterns = [
    path("health/db/", database_health_view, name="health-db"),
    path("cache/flush/", clear_system_cache_view, name="cache-flush"),
    path("accounts/", include("apps.accounts.urls")),
    path("school/", include("apps.school.urls")),
    path("students/", include("apps.students.urls")),
    path("attendance/", include("apps.attendance.urls")),
    path("campus/", include("apps.campus.urls")),
    path("messaging/", include("apps.messaging.urls")),
    path("reports/", include("apps.reports.urls")),
]

urlpatterns = [
    path("admin/", admin.site.urls),
    path("api/v1/", include((v1_patterns, "v1"))),
    path("api/", include("config.legacy_urls")),
    re_path(r"^media/(?P<path>.*)$", serve_cached_media),
]

if settings.DEBUG:
    urlpatterns += static(settings.STATIC_URL, document_root=settings.STATIC_ROOT)

# D:\AttendSure_V3\backend\Dockerfile
FROM python:3.12-slim AS base

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1 \
    PIP_DISABLE_PIP_VERSION_CHECK=1

WORKDIR /app

# Install system dependencies required by Pillow and psycopg2
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    build-essential \
    libpq-dev \
    libjpeg62-turbo-dev \
    zlib1g-dev \
    curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt /app/requirements.txt
RUN pip install --upgrade pip && pip install -r /app/requirements.txt

COPY . /app

# Create non-root runtime user and ensure writable static/media/log paths
RUN useradd --create-home --shell /bin/bash attendsure \
    && mkdir -p /app/staticfiles /app/media /app/logs \
    && chown -R attendsure:attendsure /app

USER attendsure

EXPOSE 8000 8001 8002

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=20s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT:-8000}/api/health/db/" || exit 1

CMD ["gunicorn", "config.wsgi:application", "-c", "gunicorn.conf.py"]

"""
ATTENDSURE - Gunicorn Production Configuration
Reads GUNICORN_BIND so Blue (8001), Green (8002), and standalone (8000) all work from one config.
"""

import multiprocessing
import os

bind = os.getenv("GUNICORN_BIND", "0.0.0.0:8000")
workers = int(os.getenv("GUNICORN_WORKERS", str(min((multiprocessing.cpu_count() * 2) + 1, 9))))
threads = int(os.getenv("GUNICORN_THREADS", "2"))
worker_class = "gthread"
timeout = 60
keepalive = 5

# Periodically recycle workers to prevent Pillow image memory growth

#!/usr/bin/env python
import os
import sys


def main():
    if os.environ.get("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE") == "attendsure_core.settings":
        os.environ.pop("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", None)
    os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings")
    from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
os.environ["DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE"] = "config.settings"

DOMAIN_APPS = [
    "accounts",
    "school",
    "students",
    "attendance",
    "campus",
    "messaging",
    "reports",
]


def ensure_app_packages_and_urls():
    """Guarantees every app has __init__.py, migrations/__init__.py, and urls.py."""
    (BASE_DIR / "apps" / "__init__.py").touch(exist_ok=True)
    for app_name in DOMAIN_APPS:
        app_dir = BASE_DIR / "apps" / app_name
        mig_dir = app_dir / "migrations"
        mig_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (app_dir / "__init__.py").touch(exist_ok=True)
        (mig_dir / "__init__.py").touch(exist_ok=True)

        urls_file = app_dir / "urls.py"
        if not urls_file.exists():
            urls_file.write_text("urlpatterns = []\n", encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"[CREATED] apps/{app_name}/urls.py")


def archive_legacy_folders():
    backup_dir = BASE_DIR / "_legacy_backup"
    legacy_paths = [
        BASE_DIR / "attendsure_core",
        BASE_DIR / "apps" / "academics",
        BASE_DIR / "apps" / "api",
        BASE_DIR / "apps" / "common",
        BASE_DIR / "apps" / "core",
        BASE_DIR / "apps" / "telemetry",
    ]
    for path in legacy_paths:
        if path.exists():
            backup_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            target = backup_dir / path.name
            if target.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(target)
            shutil.move(str(path), str(target))
            print(f"[ARCHIVED] {path.relative_to(BASE_DIR)} -> _legacy_backup/{path.name}")


def ensure_holidays_table_for_existing_db(connection):
    """Creates school_holidays if attaching to an existing AttendSure database."""
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        tables = set(connection.introspection.table_names(cursor))
        if "academic_years" in tables and "school_holidays" not in tables:
            if connection.vendor == "postgresql":
                cursor.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS school_holidays (
                        id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                        holiday_date DATE NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                        title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
                        created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
                    );
                """)
            else:
                cursor.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS school_holidays (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        holiday_date DATE NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                        title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
                        created_at DATETIME NOT NULL
                    );
                """)
            print("[CREATED TABLE] school_holidays")


def sync_database_state():
    import django
    from django.core.management import call_command
    from django.db import connection

    django.setup()

    print("\n[1/3] Running Django system check...")
    call_command("check")

    print("\n[2/3] Generating migrations for domain apps...")
    call_command(
        "makemigrations",
        "accounts",
        "school",
        "students",
        "attendance",
        "campus",
        "messaging",
    )

    ensure_holidays_table_for_existing_db(connection)

    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        existing_tables = set(connection.introspection.table_names(cursor))

    print("\n[3/3] Syncing database state (preserving all existing records)...")
    if "students" in existing_tables:
        call_command("migrate", "--fake-initial")
    else:
        call_command("migrate")

    print("\n[SUCCESS] All domain apps checked and synced with zero errors!")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ensure_app_packages_and_urls()
    archive_legacy_folders()
    sync_database_state()
